### 235 subject events

- **1914.11** `iw18-infancy.ceiling-shadows::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-tenement/fever-behind-the-shadows` tags: infancy, night, wonder, gaslight, fear, family, vienna, urban
  后半夜Klara烧起来，额头烫得像炉盖。Josef Bauer把湿布叠成窄条，一遍遍擦孩子的手心；煤气灯的影子在墙上忽长忽短，Klara哭一阵停一阵。天快亮时，Josef Bauer抱着孩子去敲隔壁的门，借来一支体温计。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (an infant fever at night); condition; cash -1 (a borrowed thermometer and medicine); mood -0.03999999999999998; flag +infant_night_fever_

- **1915.09** `vienna-early-childhood.stairwell-fall/arm-bruise` tags: childhood, family, home, street, injury
  黑楼梯间只有门缝透进来的一点光。Klara踩空时伸手去抓扶手，手臂先撞在铁端上，整个人滚下三级。Josef Bauer把Klara抱到灶边，拿湿冷毛巾按住肿起的地方，又撕了一条旧布缠紧。第二天Klara举着胳膊下楼，照样去院子里玩。
  _effects: health -0.040000000000000036 (从楼梯滚下，小臂挫伤); mood -0.04999999999999999_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-half-flour` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  面粉袋压在胸口，越抱越沉。Klara前面的大婶一直往后靠，Klara就把膝盖顶住对方的篮子，硬留出半步。买到半袋面粉后，Klara的鞋跟留在人堆里，找回来时鞋面全是泥。回家路上布袋换了两次肩，赤脚踩过石板时脚底发麻。
  _effects: food; health -0.020000000000000018 (赤脚走石板路磨伤脚底); skill_

- **1918.04** `vienna-childhood-street.hoop/hoop-win` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  巷子窄，两个孩子并排推着铁环，铁环撞在一起叮当作响。Klara的钩子先绕过最后一块界石。Mitzi Lechner不服，说再比一次，手却已经伸进口袋，摸出三颗彩色弹珠。Klara把弹珠举到眼前看，Mitzi Lechner在旁边踢墙，踢得鞋尖发白。
  _effects: relation friend with person-mitzi-lechner; mood 0.05; skill_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-three-heller` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  床单浸了碱水，沉得拽不动。Klara站在木凳上，把湿布一点点递过绳，水顺着肘弯滴进袖口。Mitzi Hofer在绳头数夹子，最后一条挂好，从围裙口袋里摸出三枚赫勒，按进Klara掌心。晚上洗手时，虎口那道口子被水一蜇，Klara才看见。
  _effects: cash 0.03 (帮邻居晾床单挣的三赫勒); health -0.030000000000000027 (碱水泡手，虎口裂开); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-22_

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-both-families` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  队伍拐弯时，Mitzi Hofer朝Klara招手。Klara挤过去，替两家把面包领出来，纸包用不同的绳结系住。家里人晚上解开绳结，先掂了掂份量，再问价钱；Klara把每一包的面包数、找零和欠的铜板都说了一遍，家里人听完才把两包分别收进柜子。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30_

- **1921.01** `statistical_infection`
  Klara在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1921.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-dragged-docked-pay` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  “你看看这角。”Maria Kral把床单拎起来，煤渣簌簌往下掉。Klara伸手去拍，反而把灰按进布纹里。Maria Kral从工钱里数回两枚，只留一赫勒在Klara手心，让重洗那角。晚饭前Klara一直蹲在井台边，换了两盆水，指甲缝里全是黑泥。
  _effects: cash 0.01 (被扣后只剩一赫勒); mood -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-maria-kral_

- **1921.03** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Klara的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1921.04** `statistical_recovery`
  Klara从肺炎中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

- **1923.06** `vienna-childhood-street.market-errand/errand-ok` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  市场里的菜摊一字排开，Klara把十五克鲁泽排在掌心，先买了土豆，又去盐摊上要了一小包。摊主用旧报纸把盐裹好，Klara把它塞进围裙内袋，土豆兜在身前。回家时Josef Bauer接过铜板一枚枚数，土豆一颗没少。
  _effects: food; cash -0.15 (市场花销); relation kin with person-josef-bauer_

- **1924.01** `vienna-youth-threshold.sibling-departure/quarrel-who-leaves` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, transition, injury
  车票只够一个人走，晚饭桌上话越说越大声。Anna Bauer说该自己走，Klara说自己也快够岁数了。母亲把汤勺往锅沿一磕，屋里静下来，最后Klara摔门出去在楼梯上坐了半宿，直到楼上有人喊冷。
  _effects: relation kin with person-anna-bauer; mood -0.07; health -0.030000000000000027 (在冰冷的楼梯上坐到半夜)_

- **1924.02** `p9-childhood-adolescence.church-club/club-one-visit-only` tags: adolescence, friendship, identity, work, school, secret, church
  Klara去了一个下午，看那些旧歌本里夹着褪色的圣像卡，工厂来的几个年轻人在最后一排用手指敲长凳打拍子。第二个星期天Klara没再去，在院子里帮母亲晾被单。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-mitzi-lechner; mood 0.01; skill_

- **1925.01** `r13-edu.school-transfer::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/acquaintance-in-office` tags: education, school, transfer, bureaucracy, family, reform, medical, schooling
  Klara的邻居在市政厅当差，替Klara把材料直接送到教育科。三天后，转学证寄到家里，上面多了一行手写批注：“准予插入三年级。”Klara把证翻来覆去看，发现自己的名字被写错了一个字母。名字写错的事Klara没去改，反正学籍号是对的，老师点名时也这么叫。
  _effects: flag +school_transfer_approved; knowledge “the school transfer certificate misspells the name”_

  _effects: cash -1 (寄给作者的信); mood -0.09999999999999998; condition; flag +hid_letter_in_bookshop; knowledge “the bookshop sold its old stock by weight”_

  巷口的风把外套下摆吹起来。旧货贩子闻了闻领子，说给两克朗。Klara把钱卷进袜筒，回家前在楼梯口站了一会儿。Josef Bauer发现衣钩空了，问了三遍，Klara都说没看见，脚趾在鞋里把硬币越踩越紧。
  _effects: cash 2 (卖掉哥哥旧外套); relation kin with person-josef-bauer; mood -0.040000000000000036_

  _effects: reputation -0.03; flag +yf18_exam_suspicion; mood -0.040000000000000036_

  _effects: reputation -0.05; mood -0.040000000000000036; flag +yf18_prater_caught; cash -2 (a fine paid at the Prater office)_

  _effects: state_add yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:220; flag +yf18_stayed_dry; mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +yf18_first_awakening_active_

  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 40 -> 45; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 0 -> 8; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.reciprocated false -> true; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  医院派来一名实习医生，身后跟着两个学生。他们把Klara的胸口听了个遍，最后请来上级；药费减半，但Klara被当成了讲课的病例。学生们走后，实习医生把听诊器在手里捂了很久，才敢再贴上来。
  _effects: cash -4 (reduced fee for a teaching-house call); health 0.03 (examined by a teaching team); condition; role; knowledge “教学出诊会有实习医生和学生轮流听诊，最后请上级定方案，药费可以减半。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-46_

  窗子推开只有一线天光，墙纸边角翘起，Klara用指甲一刮便掉下一层湿粉。看门人揣好定金，说这间是朝北的，冬天火炉要添得勤些。Karl Kraus站在楼梯口没进屋，只替Klara看着箱子。
  _effects: cash -4 (当场付下定金和钥匙钱); residence_

- **1930.07** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/dentist-pull` tags: health, medical, family, money

  _effects: cash -3 (牙医拔牙费用); health 0.05999999999999999 (拔除病牙解除疼痛); mood -0.030000000000000027_

  Klara把Josefine Hofer从后窗接下来，藏进自家地窖。第二天，Josefine Hofer却在巡警面前说是Klara先点的火；Klara被带走问了一夜，回来时地窖门开着，里面的煤少了一半。门锁却没有撬痕，像是有人用钥匙开过。地窖的锁也换了。
  _effects: role; flag +betrayed_after_rescue; cash -6 (lost coal, a broken cellar lock and a night of questioning); knowledge “被救的人可能为了自保说火是你点的；问一夜放回来，地窖里的煤也会少一半。”_

- **1933.08** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.stove-and-line/stove-and-line.sheet-quarrel` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Klara发现自己搭在栏杆上的床单被人挪到了绳尾，角上沾了煤灰，一时火起，在楼梯间拦住Elisabeth Huber问是谁动的。Elisabeth Huber说是Lena嫌滴水，两人越说越响，最后各自回屋摔上门，炉子空着，谁也没再去烧水。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-67_

- **1933.09** `vienna-parish-aid.sick-call/sick-call.help-in` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Klara和Anna Bauer一起上楼，门没锁，屋里一股酸味。病人裹着毯子坐在床沿咳嗽，炉子只剩灰。Klara去楼下讨了几块煤核，蹲着把火生起来，又用带来的面粉搅了一小锅稀粥。药粉冲开，碗底沉着白渣，Klara先垫了药钱，说以后再说。
  _effects: food; cash -0.6 (替病人垫上药粉钱); flag +parish_sick_visitor_

- **1934.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Klara在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Klara把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.05 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:005951_

  主顾在契约上按了印，要求一把带黄铜镶边的胡桃木书柜。Klara带着Otto Moser做了四十天，刨花堆到窗台；交货那天，主顾把尾款数了两遍，又在工匠名册上添了Klara的名字。
  _effects: organization_found 师傅作坊 (household_firm); position 师傅 @workshop:person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:006663; state_add wc17-master-craftsperson state:world-vienna-1912:423; cash 4 (masterwork commission fee); skill; reputation 0.07999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-friedrich-hofer; flag +masterwork_accepted; knowledge “主顾把尾款数了两遍，并把我的名字写进了工匠名册。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-61; role; flag +master_craftsperson_

  _effects: historical development:initiative-taxi:person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3-evt:world-vienna-1912:000753-1927 as supporter; historical_influence; reputation 0.015000000000000013_

  Klara在楼梯下面接到一张折成四折的传单。纸上没有署名，只写着下一次集会的地址。Klara去了，在昏暗的屋里报上姓名，成为第二区夜班出租合作社的一员，并答应负责自己所在街区的联络。
  _effects: organization_membership; role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-68; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-68_

- **1934.09** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.letter-answer/burn-the-letter` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family
  等到第九天，Klara把没寄出的草稿和那封写了一半的信一起塞进炉子。纸卷起来，火苗发蓝，屋里一股焦味；Klara拿火钩把灰捅散，决定不再去问信的下落。炉门上的铁片烫手，那个“亲”字先没了。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; flag +gave_up_on_letter; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23_

- **1934.12** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-neighbors-together` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Klara把Elisabeth Huber和楼里另两户人家叫到楼道，把那张纸摆在窗台上传看。看门人在楼梯拐角听着，说房东礼拜三来收钱。几家人合计好，到那天一起等在门厅，先把克朗递过去，再把修补的事一件件摆出来。Klara把纸折进围裙口袋，觉得人凑齐了话也硬气些。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-67; mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +tenant_collective_

  Klara和Katharina Kral在咖啡馆后间成立了一个小小的政治俱乐部。桌子是借的，章程是手写的，但签名、印章和一间可以锁门的办公室是真的。Gustav Berg没有到场，只送来一句“先看看你们能撑多久”。
  _effects: cash -2 (一间借来的办公室); reputation 0.06; organization_found 权力俱乐部 (party); position 发起人 @org:r15-faction:evt:world-vienna-1912:007241_

  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:007241_

  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; relation colleague with person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912_

  工厂的人来问过两次，房东随后把Klara的箱子搬到楼梯口。Klara在车站的长椅上坐到天亮，把药瓶倒空，又一颗颗捡回来；清洁工给了半杯温水。清洁工把拖把靠在长椅边，没有催他走。
  _effects: move place-wien; cash -4 (lost room deposit and food); condition; role; knowledge “工厂来问过两次以后，房东就把箱子搬到了楼梯口；车站长椅可以过夜。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-86_

  Klara把第三幕撕成四份，塞进铁炉，火苗先舔到的是标题。屋子里有一股纸灰和胶水混在一起的味道。Ferdinand Berg在门外敲门催房租，Klara说再等两天，然后坐回桌前，开始写一个只有四页的短篇：一个男人在煤气灯下修一把永远修不好的椅子。
  _effects: state_add fa17-starving-artist state:world-vienna-1912:462; skill; mood -0.030000000000000027; cash -1 (买新纸和墨水的钱); flag +burned_manuscript_

  _effects: mood -0.029999999999999805; reputation -0.04999999999999999; flag +faction_split_

  走到半路雨点砸下来，山道立刻变得溜滑。Klara和Gustav Bauer躲进路边的酒棚檐下，商量了几句，决定改乘电车回家，车票六十赫勒。Klara把带去的面包掰开分给同车躲雨的人，自己站在车尾，湿透的裤脚贴着腿，一路打着哆嗦；远足没走完，倒记住了一车陌生人的谢意。

  _effects: cash -0.4 (一周丧葬互助会会费); relation acquaintance with person-hermann-kral; flag +funeral_society_member_

  Klara把牌一推，说今晚不借也不玩。Ferdinand Berg耸耸肩，把桌上的钱收回口袋。Klara走到街上，口袋里的硬币只够买一杯咖啡；但经过当铺时，Klara没有停。第二天上工，Klara的手指还在发痒，像少做了一件本该做的事。

  _effects: reputation -0.03999999999999998; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-73; skill_

- **1939.01** `lc19-talisman.never-leave::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-workshop-hides-the-tools` tags: talisman, apprentice, workshop, escape, coercion, position, dark, triumphant
  Klara收拾工具箱时，发现最顺手的刨子和那把旧角尺都不见了。门口挂着幸运牌的作坊说库房进了水，工具先收起来；Anna Bauer 3在门口低着头，鞋边露出角尺的黄铜头。Klara没有拆穿，只把自己的围裙叠好，放回椅背上。
  _effects: state_parameter lc19-workshop-talisman.reliance 45 -> 55; state_parameter lc19-workshop-talisman.dignity 52 -> 42; state_parameter lc19-workshop-talisman.protection 17 -> 22; flag +lc19_tools_hidden_

- **1939.01** `state_tick:wc17-master-craftsperson/workshop-year/commissions-arrive` tags: wc17-master-craftsperson, craft, work, master, workshop, ownership, reputation, state
  Klara的作坊又接了两单：一扇橡木门、一只修到一半的八音盒。Klara把工钱先付给学徒，自己留下最后一张钞票，用铅笔在账本背面记下欠料行的数目。
  _effects: state_parameter wc17-master-craftsperson.backlog 5 -> 7; cash 2 (workshop commissions); reputation 0.020000000000000018_

- **1939.01** `state_tick:lc19-workshop-talisman/talisman-year/the-workshop-leans` tags: lc19-workshop-talisman, workshop, craft, luck, talisman, reputation, dependence, exploitation
  一年过去，主顾进门先看Klara坐的那把椅子，再谈木料和价钱。Klara的工钱涨了一点，手上的活却少了；师傅说这叫“镇店”，Klara听见自己像一件被擦得很亮的旧家具。
  _effects: state_parameter lc19-workshop-talisman.reliance 55 -> 61; state_parameter lc19-workshop-talisman.dignity 42 -> 38; state_parameter lc19-workshop-talisman.debt 12 -> 15; cash 1 (a small raise for being the workshop's sign)_

- **1939.01** `agency:attend_meeting` tags: agency, agency:attend_meeting
  Klara走到Arbeiter-Zeitung im Vorwärts-Haus门口，看见便衣和拥挤的人群，最终没有进去。
  _effects: reputation -0.010000000000000009_

- **1939.04** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.letter-answer/mother-opened-letter` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family
  回信没有到Klara手里。Katharina Berg的母亲先拆了它，连信封上的邮戳一起摊在洗衣房的桌上，说有人写字来缠自家孩子。话传到院子里时已经添了油，Ferdinand Kral隔着栏杆把原话学了一遍，说得很难听；Katharina Berg再见到Klara时，眼睛先往母亲那边看。Klara站在水桶边，知道这封信已经被拆成了两家的脸面。
  _effects: reputation -0.06; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000453:child_

- **1939.06** `hs18-radio.rumor::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/turn-it-up` tags: radio, blackout, rumor, panic, neighbor, politics, vienna, urban
  停电后的第三个小时，Ferdinand Berg把收音机搬到楼道，电池快没电了。女播音员说“请市民保持镇静”，随后是一段军乐。Klara听见有人开始收拾行李；楼下的铁门被风撞了三次，像有人在敲。
  _effects: state_add hs18-blackout-rumor state:world-vienna-1912:576; flag +heard_radio_rumor_

- **1939.11** `p9-social-courtship-friendship.friend-intercession/intercession-too-far` tags: courtship, friendship, social, adulthood, reputation, leopoldstadt
  Klara听到Ferdinand Kral把工钱和家底夸过了头，脸沉下来，当晚就照地址找上门去解释。话到嘴边绕成一团，Ferdinand Weiss 3的母亲把网兜往桌上一搁，说年轻人有话该早说，不该让旁人来传。Klara站在门里，名声在面包店门口折了一道，Ferdinand Weiss 3始终没有出来。
  _effects: reputation -0.03999999999999998; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-98; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-98_

- **1940.01** `office16-hire.protege::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/hired-competent` tags: office, hire, patronage, bureaucracy, work, power, vienna, urban
  Klara把Friedrich Berg的名字写在编制表的空行上，又在旁边注了“试用三个月”。Mitzi Hofer提醒说预算只够半个位置，Klara还是把名字留下了。第二天，Friedrich Berg提前半小时到，把走廊里的旧档案按年份重新排了一遍。
  _effects: position 助理文书 @lc19-workshop:person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:008904; relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001604:child; reputation 0.02999999999999997; flag +hired_protege_

  又一年过去，Klara把房租、颜料和纸张的钱分开写在一张账单背面。作品没有卖出去几件，但手上的线条比去年稳；夜里，Klara把一盏煤气灯移到画架最近的地方，继续修改同一个角落。
  _effects: state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.craft 55 -> 59; state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.recognition 6 -> 9; state_parameter fa17-starving-artist.need 74 -> 72; cash 1 (一张小画的价钱); mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1940.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.cafe-newspaper/p9-social-daily-culture.cafe-newspaper.read-all` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:daily
  Klara把木架上的《新自由报》从头版读到讣告栏，中间续了两次水，第三杯 Melange 端上来时奶泡已经塌了，三杯一共六十赫勒。侍者把找零的铜板拍在桌上，没有催的意思；Klara折好报纸夹回木架，记住了议会辩论里的两个名字，出门时雨还没停。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (三杯 Melange 的咖啡钱); mood 0.050000000000000044; skill_

- **1940.03** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.stove-and-line/stove-and-line.move-to-line-end` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Klara把自己泡着的衣服端到绳子最末一段，把靠近炉子的位置让给Otto Bauer，条件是对方看着火，别让水烧干。Otto Bauer点头，往炉膛里添了一块煤，说中午以前一定把炉子空出来。两家的盆就挨着放在窗下。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1940.05** `oj18-zoo.night::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/broken-tooth` tags: zoo, animals, night, keeper, work, odd_job, suspense, vienna
  夜里十一点，母狮不肯碰桶里的马肉。Klara举着煤油灯绕到兽栏后面，看见它右槽牙断了一截，牙龈肿得发亮。Klara把肉切碎，又去敲Elisabeth Bauer 5的门；第二天兽医来了，Hermann Berg 2在晨会上第一次记住Klara的名字。
  _effects: organization_found 普拉特动物园夜班队 (other); position 夜班饲养员 @zoo:person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:010017; state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:599; skill; reputation 0.030000000000000027; flag +zoo_night_shift; knowledge “母狮的右槽牙断了，牙龈肿得发亮，夜里不肯吃马肉。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-83_

- **1940.12** `vienna-legacy-worklife.hours-ledger/hours-ledger.sign` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  老工人凑近看那张表，说这纸比工资袋还薄。Klara把拇指蘸进印泥，按下去时听见工头说了句“下一个”。学徒在旁边数少掉的铜板，数到四十赫勒时不出声了。
  _effects: cash -1 (星期六下午减少的计件工钱); mood 0.050000000000000044; flag +accepted_hours_reform; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-102_

  Klara在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Klara把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.05 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages)_

  第一次会议只来了三个人，其中一个是房东，来问房租。Klara把章程念完，没有人反对，也没有人加入。办公室的钥匙还在，但那间屋子比街上更安静。

  洗衣房里蒸汽弥漫，熨斗压在衬衫领子上咝咝作响。老板娘翻了翻Klara熨好的第一件，说领口还差些火候，手上倒还稳。两打熨完，三个克朗放在围裙口袋里，铜板还带着体温。
  _effects: cash 3 (洗衣房试工半天按件计酬); skill_

  Mitzi Bauer 3把Klara的证件翻了两遍，问这么晚去哪。Klara说回家；Mitzi Bauer 3看了看地址，又看了看Klara的鞋，把证件还回来，说“走大路”。Klara走出十几步，才发现自己一直屏着呼吸。
  _effects: mood 0.010000000000000009; flag +police_let_go; knowledge “警察看了地址，让走大路。”_

  _effects: cash -0.3 (缴纳丧葬互助会的月钱); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-68; reputation 0.03999999999999998_

  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018; reputation 0.03999999999999998; state_add stage-king state:world-vienna-1912:673; flag +mistaken_for_official; flag +stage_king; reputation 0.03999999999999998_

  兵营食堂的桌子都推到墙边，留声机放在一只弹药箱上。Gustav Bauer的军装袖口磨得发亮，请Klara跳一支，说下星期就开拔。Otto Bauer在门口催了三回；Klara把名字和部队番号写在糖纸上，塞进对方口袋。

- **1942.03** `r13-turn.taking-in-orphan::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::war-refugee-children/took-the-child-in` tags: family, orphan, care, household, scarcity, vienna, urban, crisis
  孩子是Ferdinand Weiss 2从车站领回来的，父母在轰炸里没了。Klara先量了量他的鞋，发现比自家孩子的脚小两指，就把旧鞋垫了两层布。晚上多添一瓢水进汤锅，锅立刻显得浅了。Klara说先住下，户口的事明天再想。
  _effects: cash -6 (门口多出来的一双鞋); food; flag +took_in_orphan; role; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:004457:child; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81; household; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81_

- **1942.06** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/drops-and-endure` tags: health, medical, family, money
  夜里疼醒时，Klara摸黑往棉球上又滴药水，手抖得洒在枕头上。Marie Huber第二天一早来敲门，看见肿脸就说别硬撑了，可Klara只是摆摆手，说再熬一天也许就好了。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (牙痛药水); condition_

- **1942.11** `bc18-stage.understudy-king::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/booed-off` tags: theatre, performance, stage, mistaken_identity, absurd, fame, humiliation, vienna
  Klara上场时踩到王袍，连人带椅摔进乐池，王冠滚到第一排一位老太太脚边。观众先愣，接着笑成一片；Ferdinand Weiss 3在后台把节目单撕了。第二天，小报写“王冠落地，剧院关门”，Klara在家里躺了三天，听见楼下孩子喊“国王”，也不敢开窗。
  _effects: cash -1 (摔坏王冠的赔偿); reputation -0.03999999999999998; state_add bc18-ridiculous-fame state:world-vienna-1912:674; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-98; condition; flag +fell_into_the_orchestra_pit; mood -0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-65; flag +ridiculously_famous; reputation 0.020000000000000018; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1943.01** `office16-corruption.inspection::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/fined` tags: office, corruption, audit, legal, power, dark, vienna, urban
  Mitzi Bauer 3在第三份合同上停住了：同一批纸张被报了两次价，第二次的签字是Klara。罚款单当场开出，金额不算大，但足够让下一季度的办公用品全部改成最便宜的牌子。Klara没有争辩，只在收据背面写了日期。
  _effects: reputation -0.04999999999999999; mood -0.020000000000000018; state_add office-under-siege state:world-vienna-1912:724; flag +fined_after_audit; knowledge “同一批纸张被报了两次价，第二次的签字是我的。”; flag +office_under_siege_

- **1943.01** `vienna-women-housework.coal-stove/coal-stove.buy-coal` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  炉门一开，黑烟先扑出来，Klara眯着眼把纸团往里塞。火苗舔着碎煤慢慢亮起来，Marie Huber隔着门喊当心一氧化碳。水壶开始咕噜作响，屋里才有点热乎气。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (购买一小筐碎煤); food_

- **1943.01** `state_tick:wc17-master-craftsperson/workshop-year/winter-thin` tags: wc17-master-craftsperson, craft, work, master, workshop, ownership, reputation, state
  冬天来得早，煤价涨了，主顾却要压价。Klara守着冷掉的胶锅，把最后一块好木料留给一单可能退掉的活；账本上的欠款又添了一行。
  _effects: state_parameter wc17-master-craftsperson.debt 5 -> 8; cash -1 (workshop winter costs)_

- **1943.01** `state_tick:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/strange-year/the-logbook-mislaid` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  巡查簿在换班时不见了，第二天却出现在煤筐底下，最后几页被水泡得发皱。管事的说签字不能算数，扣了Klara半个月的夜班钱；Klara没有争，只在新的簿子第一页把日期写得很大。

- **1943.01** `state_expire:stage-king` tags: stage-king, performance, theatre, status, irony, state, state_tick, state:stage-king

  _effects: state_remove stage-king state:world-vienna-1912:673; flag -stage_king_

- **1943.01** `state_tick:bc18-ridiculous-fame/ridiculous-fame-year/the-story-holds-another-year` tags: bc18-ridiculous-fame, fame, reputation, mistaken_identity, promotion, media, absurd, public
  又一年过去，Klara仍在被介绍成那个人物。邀请函从市郊的园艺会、女子学校和退伍军人酒馆寄来；Klara学会在签名后加一句模糊的谦辞，听起来像有许多往事不便细说。每一次应付都带来一点小钱，也让真正的解释更不合时宜。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.notoriety 20 -> 27; state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.invitations 0 -> 1; cash 1 (一场小型演讲的车马费); reputation 0.010000000000000009; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1943.01** `agency:attend_meeting` tags: agency, agency:attend_meeting
  Klara去了Arbeiter-Zeitung im Vorwärts-Haus，坐在后排听完了关于Sozialdemokratische Arbeiterpartei Österreich的争论；散会时带走一张油印提纲。
  _effects: historical development:initiative-org-sdap-1933 as participant; knowledge “我参加了Sozialdemokratische Arbeiterpartei Österreichs (SD。”; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1943.10** `hs18-queue.refugee::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/give-a-false-address` tags: refugee, queue, station, papers, official, history, vienna, urban
  Klara把隔壁空屋的门牌号写在表格上。Mitzi Bauer 3看了一眼，居然没有追问。Friedrich Berg千恩万谢，提着箱子往那条街走；Klara没有提醒那屋已经塌了半边，只把围巾拉高，转身去赶电车。
  _effects: knowledge “空屋的门牌号也能通过登记。”; flag +gave_false_refugee_address_

- **1943.12** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/corner-doctor` tags: health, medical, family, money
  药粉含在嘴里发苦，Klara就着糖浆咽下去，喉头一阵凉。医生拎包出门时，楼梯间的煤气灯刚好灭了，Katharina Berg摸黑送他到拐角，回来时手里攥着找回的几枚赫勒。
  _effects: cash -4 (医生出诊费和药费); health 0.05 (药粉和糖浆缓解症状); relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000453:child_

- **1944.01** `office16-office.lose-the-chair::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/term-ends-clean` tags: office, removal, loss, politics, power, transition, vienna, urban
  任期在十二月三十一日结束。Klara把印章擦干净，钥匙串上只剩自己家的那把；Mitzi Hofer 2来接手续，两个人把最后一份移交清单对了两遍。走出大门时，门房照旧叫了一声“先生”，没有加头衔。
  _effects: position_end 公共礼仪官 @org:absurd15-wish-office:evt:world-vienna-1912:005046; reputation 0.020000000000000018; mood -0.040000000000000036; condition; flag +left_office_in_order; knowledge “移交清单上最后一项是印章，钥匙串上只剩自己家的那把。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-117_

- **1944.08** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.remake-coat/remake-coat.crooked-sleeves` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, mending, clothing, household
  Klara的剪刀钝得咬布，一剪下去袖口走了斜线。她索性把两只袖子都裁短，袖边用灰线锁了一圈。锁边时线太短，接到第三段才够，右手食指被针顶出个红印，改完像件旧式的短褂。
  _effects: skill; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1944.09** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_no_damage` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Klara刚把锅端下灶，火苗就蹿了起来，Katharina Berg一脚踩灭地上的油布，顺手抄起沙箱里的沙子盖住灶口。两人都没受伤，但Katharina Berg的鞋底烧穿一个洞，那是唯一一双出门的鞋。
  _effects: relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000453:child; mood -0.04999999999999999_

- **1944.12** `ad20-loan::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/sign-here` tags: loan, debt, credit, everyday, obligation, money, vienna, urban
  Katharina Berg把借据铺在桌上，先让Klara看利息，又让Klara看还款日期。Klara用指头一行行划过去，在最后一行停住：若逾期，担保人承担。Katharina Berg说只是走个形式；Klara还是把担保人的名字问清楚，才签了字。
  _effects: cash 6 (a loan was signed with a private creditor); state_add ad20-private-bargain state:world-vienna-1912:764; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000453:child; flag +loan_signed; mood 0.010000000000000009; knowledge “借据上写着若逾期，担保人承担。”; flag +private_bargain_active_

- **1945.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/neighbor_yanks_clothes` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Katharina Berg嫌Klara的床单滴水滴到Katharina Berg家衬衫上，一把扯下床单扔在院子的石板上。Klara下楼捡起来时，床单角沾了泥，重洗又要费肥皂。两人在院子当中对喊，楼上有人推开窗看热闹。Klara把床单重新搭上绳子时，故意把水拧在Katharina Berg那侧。
  _effects: relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000453:child; cash -0.15 (重洗床单的肥皂钱)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:012283_

  _effects: move place-klosterneuburg-aunt-house; role; cash -5 (temporary shelter)_

- **1945.11** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-neighbors-together` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Klara把Grete Kral和楼里另两户人家叫到楼道，把那张纸摆在窗台上传看。看门人在楼梯拐角听着，说房东礼拜三来收钱。几家人合计好，到那天一起等在门厅，先把克朗递过去，再把修补的事一件件摆出来。Klara把纸折进围裙口袋，觉得人凑齐了话也硬气些。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-94; mood 0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-94_

- **1945.12** `vienna-life-overcrowded-flat/move-to-shared-tenement` tags: housing, home, health, family, household
  Klara把床架拆开，用绳子捆在借来的手推车上，搬进利奥波德城的合租房。押金和车钱花掉八克朗；新住处人多、墙薄，但至少每个人有一块能躺下的地方，Klara的腰在冷风里疼了一路，夜里却第一次不用侧着身睡。
  _effects: residence; cash -8 (deposit and cart)_

- **1946.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Klara在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Klara把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.05 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:012721_

  前任留下的账本太整齐了：每一笔都落在同一个日期，连墨水颜色都一样。Klara让Mitzi Bauer 3抽了三页去核对，发现其中两页的收据号码连续，却来自两家不同的商号。Mitzi Bauer 3合上本子，问要不要现在报上去。
  _effects: reputation -0.020000000000000018; state_add office-under-siege state:world-vienna-1912:835; flag +found_cooked_ledger; knowledge “前任账本里两页收据号码连续，却来自两家不同商号。”; flag +office_under_siege_

  水龙头滴滴答答地漏，两人都伸手去扶桶沿。Klara看见自家未洗的床单还堆着，胸口发闷，把桶让了半掌宽。晾绳上她的衬衣角被风掀起，正挡着邻家的窗户。

- **1946.01** `r14-spec.hoax-newspaper::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/pulled-before-the-print-run` tags: hoax, newspaper, media, humiliation, fraud, vienna, urban, postwar
  Klara在付印前把底稿抽回来，向Josef Lechner 2承认星图是假的。Josef Lechner 2盯着那张纸看了很久，问为什么。Klara说本来只想气一气Mitzi Lechner，没想到会印到头版。Mitzi Lechner在旁边笑了一声，说这年头，假星星比真新闻好卖；Josef Lechner 2扣下一克朗排版费，把稿子锁进抽屉，没有再提。
  _effects: cash -1 (整版登出的火星人); mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +spec14_hoax_confessed; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-112; knowledge “the editor keeps rejected hoaxes in the bottom drawer”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-112_

  _effects: health -0.04 (高烧硬熬伤身); condition; relation friend with person-otto-winter_

  _effects: cash 8 (追回的拖欠计件工资); reputation -0.030000000000000027; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-87; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-87_

  审计传票上，Klara的名字被Mitzi Hofer多写了一个字母，门房因此把信退回了收发室。等更正后的传票送到时，真正的采购单已经从另一只抽屉里露了出来；质询会延期两周，Klara在走廊里第一次听见有人替自己说话。Mitzi Hofer没有邀功，只把那张写错的传票夹进自己的抽屉。

  Klara上楼敲了Anna Bauer的门，说唱针刮在墙上，夜班的人睡不安稳。Anna Bauer把留声机从墙边挪到窗下，垫了一块毛毡，答应十点以后不再放。下楼时进行曲还在响，但闷了些，Klara能听见自家水壶的响声了。

  _effects: cash 0.75 (交了一整捆衬衫领子的计件工钱); mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1914.08** `statistical_infection`

- **1925.01** `r1-fam.schooling-decision::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/school.apprenticeship` tags: family, education, apprenticeship, poverty, future, reform, medical, schooling
  Luise Hofer把学徒合同摊在桌上，说三年管饭，出师后一天两克朗。Ferdinand Lechner 2说Mitzi的算术是班里最好的，再读两年可以考师范。Alois Lechner把合同推回去，说家里等不起两年；Mitzi看着窗外，操场上有人在踢一只破球。
  _effects: cash 2 (学徒合同还是课本); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-16; knowledge “老师认定这个学生能考上师范。”; education apprenticeship; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-16_

- **1925.01** `r13-edu.school-transfer::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/transfer-approved` tags: education, school, transfer, bureaucracy, family, reform, medical, schooling
  Mitzi的转学材料缺一张居住证明，Alois Hofer让Mitzi去区公所补。Mitzi跑了三趟，第三趟正赶上下雨，Alois Hofer看见Mitzi把湿透的纸一张张摊在柜台上，没再挑毛病，盖了章。Mitzi赶在开学前一天把名字写进新学校。那间教室在二楼，Mitzi第一天就找到了靠窗的座位，把旧课本摆好。
  _effects: flag +school_transfer_approved; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45_

- **1925.08** `r13-turn.school-leaving::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::reform-leaving-age/signed-apprenticeship` tags: education, school, work, family, transition, reform, medical, schooling
  毕业钟敲过三下，Grete Stein把成绩单按在讲台上，说Mitzi还能再读一年，只要家里肯出四克朗。家里人在走廊里把学徒契约展开，纸角还带着面粉。Mitzi签了名，第二天就站到刨床前，手指上多了第一道木刺。
  _effects: cash -2 (毕业钟响过以后); flag +left_school_for_apprenticeship; role; education apprenticeship_

- **1925.12** `r3-teen.parent-remarriage::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/conflict` tags: family, remarriage, stepfamily, grief, household, reform, medical, schooling
  Rosa Lechner把Mitzi生父的旧大衣拿去当铺，说家里不缺这件碍事的衣服。Mitzi追到巷口，把当票抢回来，撕成两半。Alois Lechner站在两人中间，最后谁也没有帮。
  _effects: mood -0.1; condition; flag +conflict_over_dead_fathers_coat; relation rival with person-rosa-lechner; relation rival with person-alois-lechner_

- **1926.01** `yf18-friend.two-tickets::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/confront-at-the-cloakroom` tags: first_love, friendship, betrayal, dance, tickets, reform, medical, schooling
  Mitzi在衣帽间堵住Anton，问那两张票怎么到了Klara手里。Anton说Mitzi从来没敢开口，自己只是替Mitzi把话说了；Mitzi把票根揉成一团，塞进Anton的西装口袋，转身时撞翻了侍者的托盘。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 60 -> 65; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 0 -> 10; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 5 -> 0; relation friend with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912; reputation -0.020000000000000004; mood -0.04999999999999999_

  _effects: mood -0.10000000000000003_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:002730_

  Mitzi把两件衬衫、一双袜子和小时候的洗礼证明卷进一块旧桌布，塞到床底下。Alois Lechner在厨房里数硬币，数了三遍都没有抬头；Mitzi躺在黑暗里，听见夜车从远处经过，铁轨的声音一直传到枕头下面。
  _effects: state_add yf18-runaway-plan state:world-vienna-1912:130; flag +yf18_bundle_hidden; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +yf18_runaway_plan_active_

- **1926.01** `state_tick:yf18-first-awakening/half-year-of-glances/rehearsed-conversation` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state
  Mitzi在厨房里对着镜子练了七遍开场白，第二天却在离Elisabeth Bauer三步远的地方拐进面包店。Mitzi买了一袋并不需要的盐，回家路上把开场白又想了一遍；这一次，连标点都更熟练了。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 65 -> 71; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 10 -> 13; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1926.01** `r13-fam.help-homework::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/tears-on-the-page` tags: task, family, school, homework, sibling, literacy, reform, medical
  Friedrich Lechner把铅笔芯写断了两次，第三遍还是算错，眼泪滴在本子上，把墨迹洇开。Mitzi把本子拿开，说再写一遍，孩子却把椅子往后一推。母亲在灶边喊别吵，Mitzi只好把豆子收进碗里，听见Friedrich Lechner在床上抽鼻子。
  _effects: mood -0.06; flag +homework_ended_in_tears; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000305:child; condition_

- **1926.01** `state_reaction:yf18-runaway-plan/a-family-question` tags: yf18-runaway-plan, escape, youth, plan, home, travel, risk, state
  饭桌上又为房租吵起来。Mitzi把面包掰成很小的块，听见父亲说“这个家养不起闲人”；Mitzi没有回嘴，只在心里把格拉茨的地址又背了一遍。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.suspicion 5 -> 13; state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.resolve 55 -> 53_

  Elisabeth Bauer 3先说试三个月，不给工钱，只管一顿午饭。家里同意了，因为多一张嘴比多一双手更难。Mitzi每天最早到，最晚走，三个月后师傅才把契约拿出来。
  _effects: food; mood -0.03999999999999998; state_add apprenticeship-contract state:world-vienna-1912:128; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-25; education apprenticeship_

  学校没有多余名额，只给Mitzi留了夜校的位置。白天在工厂站一天，晚上再去教室坐三个钟头；老师说先试试，撑不住也不算丢人。
  _effects: state_add night-school state:world-vienna-1912:129_

- **1927.01** `yf18-dare.bridge-rail::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/climb-back-down` tags: dare, bridge, river, friendship, risk, reform, medical, schooling
  Mitzi的手已经搭上栏杆，听见桥下有驳船拉笛，忽然把手收回来。Josef骂了一句，Rosa却把围巾解下来递给Mitzi；三个人在桥头站了一会儿，谁也没有再提这件事。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 71 -> 74; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 0 -> 0; relation friend with person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912; relation friend with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.010000000000000009_

  Mitzi在教堂门口或学校走廊里又遇见Elisabeth Bauer。这一次对方先点了头，Mitzi准备好的整段话全忘了，只把手里的书换到另一只手上；回家后Mitzi才发现，自己把书拿倒了。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 74 -> 79; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 0 -> 8; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.reciprocated false -> true; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  Rosa Winter种完痘把卡片交给老师，老师随手夹进点名册里。放学时点名册被带去办公室，Mitzi空着手回家，Rosa Lechner问有没有证明，她只能把袖子拉起来。
  _effects: health 0.04 (种痘室门口的袖子); mood -0.030000000000000027; condition; flag +vaccination_card_misplaced; knowledge “the school keeps the vaccination card in the class register”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-29_

- **1927.06** `yf18-performance.choir-solo::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/laugh-and-bow` tags: performance, choir, humiliation, school, voice, reform, medical, schooling
  Mitzi听见自己的声音劈开，索性停下来，对着教堂的穹顶笑了一声。Ferdinand Lechner 2在风琴后面瞪眼，Mitzi却把那句拉丁文降了八度重新唱了一遍，声音厚得像另一个人；台下有人先笑，随后跟着鼓掌，Emil Huber在最后一排把手套举起来挥了挥。
  _effects: reputation 0.03; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-40; flag +yf18_laughed_at_solo; mood 0.03999999999999998; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51_

  补考那天，Mitzi在考场门口等到钟响，监考说名单上没有名字。Maria Moser跑去教务处，回来时手里只有一张转学建议。Mitzi去了印刷厂当学徒，师傅说铅字比字母好认，至少不会骗人。
  _effects: condition; flag +left_school_after_exam_failure; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33; education apprenticeship; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33_

  教堂年历的订单催得紧，Magistrat der k. k. Reichshaupt- und Residenzstadt Wien把压印机的护板卸了，说这样进纸快一半。Mitzi看着那块铁板，说手会进去；Magistrat der k. k. Reichshaupt- und Residenzstadt Wien把工钱袋往桌上一扔，问Mitzi是不是不想出师了。Mitzi没有碰机器，那天下午的活由Berta Berg接手。
  _effects: relation employer with person-friedrich-hofer; state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.progress 8 -> 3; state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.failures 0 -> 1; flag +yf18_refused_unguarded_press; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-22_

  地理课上，Mitzi在地图册的空白处看见一行铅笔字：“多瑙河从维也纳流走，不从这儿。”Mitzi认出是Rosa的笔迹，趁Grete Stein转身，在下面添了一句：“那你往哪儿流？”下课铃响时，Mitzi把地图册塞进Rosa的课桌。
  _effects: state_add yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:188; relation friend with person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912; flag +yf18_atlas_note_

  Mitzi每晚给Rosa Bauer讲题，自己的作业只能留到深夜。期中考试，Mitzi的名次掉了十几位，Mitzi Weiss在家长会上点名。回家后，Mitzi看见Rosa Bauer趴在桌上睡着了，算术本还摊在胳膊下面，就没有叫醒。
  _effects: mood -0.05999999999999994; flag +neglected_own_studies_

  Mitzi在最后一支曲子前溜出礼堂，沿着教堂外墙走了一圈。窗子里传来手风琴声，Mitzi数到第三遍副歌才往家走；家里问舞会好不好，Mitzi说人太多，脚疼。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 55 -> 49; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 30 -> 27; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  _effects: cash -1 (寄给作者的信（其中1克朗记入债务）); mood -0.09999999999999998; condition; flag +hid_letter_in_bookshop; knowledge “the bookshop sold its old stock by weight”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-37; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41_

  德语作文题是《我的家乡》。Mitzi写了半页多瑙河，剩下半页写一个总在电车上看书的人。Maria Moser没有打分，只在页边写“感情太多，论据太少”，把作文折好放进自己的公文包；放学后，Mitzi看见Emil在门口等，忽然不敢走过去。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 49 -> 55; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 0 -> 3; skill; reputation -0.009999999999999995; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  _effects: reputation 0.020000000000000004; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33; flag +yf18_denounced_teacher; mood -0.050000000000000044_

  他们终于摸到公证处的后窗，撬开的抽屉里却没有遗嘱，只有一叠慈善捐款收据，数目和报纸上公布的对不上。Mitzi把收据塞进外套，找到一位律师，换回七克朗；律师说，这不是盗窃，是取证。Otto Winter问那遗嘱怎么办，Mitzi说，先让死人再等一等。
  _effects: cash 7 (把慈善收据交给律师的酬劳); knowledge “公证处抽屉里的慈善捐款收据与报纸公布的数目对不上。”; relation friend with person-otto-winter; condition; flag +stole_charity_receipts; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  _effects: relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10; cash -0.15 (重洗床单的肥皂钱（其中0.15克朗记入债务）)_

  Grete Winter在门口堵了三天，第四天把Mitzi的辞职信全文登了出来。信只有四行，最后一行是“为避免机构继续为我的判断付出代价”。Mitzi把办公室的钥匙交给Luise Hofer 3，没有带走任何文件。
  _effects: position_end 助理文书 @zoo:person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2-evt:world-vienna-1912:002321; reputation -0.08000000000000002; condition; flag +resigned_over_scandal; knowledge “辞职信被全文刊登，接任者当天就搬进了办公室。”_

- **1941.01** `r13-work.strike-fund::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/collected-forty-kroner` tags: work, union, strike, mutual_aid, food, vienna, urban, crisis
  Mitzi拿着锡罐在市场和厂门口转了一天。肉铺老板放了两枚克朗，面包房给了一袋隔夜面包，Anna Bauer 2把准备交房租的钱数出三分之一。天黑时锡罐里有四十多克朗，Mitzi把每一笔都写在包装纸上，连半赫勒也没漏。
  _effects: cash -2 (罢工第七天的汤锅); food; flag +work13_strike_fund_collected; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-46; organization_funds; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-86_

- **1941.02** `vienna-adult-social.burial-fund/burial-fund.paid` tags: adulthood, social, work, family
  车间里管互助会的Gustav Bauer 3在午休时把本子摊在Mitzi的工具箱上，说这个月还差最后一份月钱。Mitzi从马甲内袋摸出二十个克罗伊策，Gustav Bauer 3当着他的面在本子上画了钩。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (缴纳丧葬互助会的月钱); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-107; reputation 0.03999999999999998_

- **1941.04** `vienna-legacy-worklife.apprentice-wage/apprentice-wage.hand-to-family` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  家里那间屋子朝北，下午的光只够照亮桌角。Mitzi把车钱塞进外套内袋，听见母亲在数硬币时嘴里念念有词。师兄说过别到处讲，Mitzi就没讲，连妹妹问起也只说“够用”。
  _effects: cash 3 (按新规定多出的学徒工资交给家里); food; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1_

  Alois Kral把入籍证书推到Mitzi面前，让Mitzi念一段誓词，再签上新的姓名。Rosa Bauer站在旁边，手里拿着一面小旗；Alois Winter替Mitzi纠正了两个发音。签完字，Alois Kral把笔收回去，说这支笔要留给下一个人。
  _effects: mood 0.15000000000000002; flag +granted_citizenship; role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-116; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000467:child; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-115; knowledge “the oath is read aloud before the certificate is signed”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-116; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-115_

- **1942.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Mitzi在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Mitzi把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.050000000000000044 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:010832_

  Mitzi在预算表上签了字，把冬季煤房的款项从三笔并成一笔，又给夜班守卫加了半份津贴。Katharina Berg算出省下的钱刚好够修屋顶；没有人鼓掌，但一月的会议室不再需要穿大衣。
  _effects: reputation 0.03999999999999998; skill; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +signed_balanced_budget; knowledge “把冬季煤房的款项并成一笔后，省下的钱够修会议室屋顶。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-69_

- **1942.01** `vienna-historical-social-movement.district-list/demand-open-books` tags: history, historical_process, social_movement, organization, politics
  负责人说这是不信任，Mitzi回说数目对得上才叫信任。Ferdinand Moser把卷角的账页压平，指着一行重复的支出，屋里安静得能听见煤气灯嘶声。最后决定下次集会再报账。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-119; reputation 0.03999999999999998; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-119_

- **1942.01** `r13-civ.clinic-first-visit::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/chronic-cough` tags: health, clinic, queue, diagnosis, institution, vienna, urban, crisis
  Mitzi在诊所走廊的第三张长凳上坐到中午，膝上压着挂号纸和一块湿手帕。Ferdinand Kral把听诊器贴上来不到半分钟，说是潮气和烟，开了褐色止咳糖浆，又让下周再来。药费从当天的菜钱里扣走，回家时篮子里只剩半棵卷心菜。
  _effects: cash -3 (诊所走廊的第三张长凳); health -0.030000000000000027 (诊所走廊的第三张长凳); condition; flag +clinic_first_visit; knowledge “the clinic doctor called the cough chronic bronchitis”_

  Mitzi算了一夜账，第二天去敲楼下退休护士的门。看护每天下午来两小时，工钱按周付；Alois Lechner嫌贵，Mitzi说“总比我辞工强”。第一周结束时，药盒上的时间第一次没有写错。
  _effects: state_add lc17-caregiver state:world-vienna-1912:676; cash -3 (the first week of paid care); relation kin with person-alois-lechner; flag +paid_for_parent_care_

- **1943.01** `office16-corruption.inspection::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/fined` tags: office, corruption, audit, legal, power, dark, vienna, urban
  Mitzi Hofer在第三份合同上停住了：同一批纸张被报了两次价，第二次的签字是Mitzi。罚款单当场开出，金额不算大，但足够让下一季度的办公用品全部改成最便宜的牌子。Mitzi没有争辩，只在收据背面写了日期。
  _effects: reputation -0.04999999999999999; mood -0.05999999999999994; state_add office-under-siege state:world-vienna-1912:725; flag +fined_after_audit; knowledge “同一批纸张被报了两次价，第二次的签字是我的。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4; flag +office_under_siege_

  Helene Berg敲门想借半块煤给发烧的孩子烧热水，Mitzi那天刚和Helene Berg为走廊堆东西吵过嘴，没开门，隔着门板说自家也不够用。后半夜听见Helene Berg抱着孩子下楼去敲药房的门。第二天在走廊碰见，Helene Berg低头绕过去，手里的铜壶碰在栏杆上，响了一声。
  _effects: relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001844:child_

- **1945.11** `r1-civ.cellar-press::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/distributed-via-church-box` tags: politics, press, underground, print, risk, vienna, urban, crisis
  Mitzi Bauer 3没有按时来。Mitzi抱着纸包在电车站等到收班，最后把纸塞进教堂门口的意见箱。第二天，纸上的一段话被抄在面包店的包装纸上，没有人知道是谁印的，Mitzi却认出了自己的错字。
  _effects: cash -0.5 (地窖里的脚踏印刷机); mood 0.050000000000000044; reputation 0.030000000000000027; flag +distributed_sheet_via_church_box; knowledge “the church suggestion box is emptied only once a week”_

- **1946.01** `office16-admin.quiet-ledger::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/balanced` tags: office, administration, budget, competence, quiet, power, vienna, urban
  Mitzi用三个月把账本上的零碎支出并成四类，又停掉两份没有人看的简报。年底结账时，Luise Hofer 3发现不仅没有赤字，还剩下够换一批档案柜的钱；没有剪彩，只有仓库里新柜子的木头味。
  _effects: organization_funds; reputation 0.03999999999999998; skill; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +balanced_the_books; knowledge “停掉两份简报、合并零碎支出后，年底还剩一笔够换档案柜的钱。”_

  Mitzi又撑过一年。药盒按星期分好，账本上多了一栏“看护”，工钱少了一些，但还没有断；Alois Lechner今年记得住Mitzi的名字，偶尔也记得住昨天吃了什么。
  _effects: state_parameter lc17-caregiver.burden 40 -> 36; state_parameter lc17-caregiver.resentment 25 -> 23; health -0.010000000000000009 (a year of broken nights); mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1946.07** `bc18-enemy.accidental-help::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/wrong-coal-delivery` tags: enemy, mistake, work, reputation, absurd, luck, social, vienna
  Emil把二十袋“劣质煤”送到Mitzi门口，货到付款，想看Mitzi拿不出钱。卸货时，煤块黑得发亮，是上好的无烟煤；Mitzi付了钱，转手卖给面包房，净赚五克朗。Emil来打听煤的去向，Mitzi说已经烧了，还谢谢他记得自己怕冷。
  _effects: cash 5 (转卖无烟煤的差价); relation rival with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; condition; flag +enemy_coal_was_anthracite; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1946.08** `r3-war.victory-empty-chair::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::victory-anniversary/sold-bag-from-uniform` tags: war, victory, celebration, grief, community, vienna, urban, postwar
  Mitzi在庆祝会上摆了一个小摊，卖用旧军装改的布袋。Mitzi Hofer来买，问能不能便宜点；Mitzi说“这是你男人的衣服改的”，Mitzi Hofer愣住，把钱放下就走了。Mitzi把布袋收进箱子，再没拿出来。
  _effects: cash 2 (广场上的空椅子); mood -0.07999999999999996; flag +sold_bag_made_from_dead_husbands_uniform; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1_

  Luise Hofer 3每天晚上九点进电梯，只说“九层”。那座楼没有九层的办公室，只有一扇钉死的防火门；Mitzi把电梯停稳，等他敲三下门再开。月底，Luise Hofer 3把一只信封塞进电梯操作盘后面，里面是两张钞票。
  _effects: cash 2 (payment for the ninth-floor errand); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-95; knowledge “九层没有办公室，只有一扇钉死的防火门，{official}每晚九点上去敲三下。”; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 20 -> 25; mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-105_

  _effects: health -0.07999999999999996 (门闩从里面插上); condition; flag +stayed_with_abusive_partner; relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2; knowledge “the partner’s remorse lasts only until the next quarrel”_

  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 1 -> 2; flag +windfall_claim_by_relative; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  _effects: flag +hiring_list_marked; reputation -0.02999999999999997; mood -0.04000000000000015; knowledge “店主把面试者的名字写在纸背面，并说要让下一个雇主去问。”_

  Otto Winter在楼道里拦住Mitzi，问家里那张盖红章的纸是不是出了事。Mitzi说只是旧房租收据，回家却把纸从柜底翻出来，和Josef Lechner一起塞进米缸夹层。两人把米重新铺平，Josef Lechner的手一直在抖；Mitzi说这事到此为止，谁也别再提。

  Mitzi Bauer 3说拘留室满了，把Mitzi送上了一辆去乡下的卡车。车上还有三个人，谁也不说话。Mitzi被分到甜菜地，晚上睡在谷仓，工钱直接记在拘留所的账上。Helene 2后来告诉Mitzi的妹妹，说“他去外地找活了”。
  _effects: condition; flag +sent_to_the_workhouse; role; knowledge “workhouse wages are credited to the police station”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-85_

  _effects: cash -1 (借手推车买烟熨谢邻居的小开销); health -0.050000000000000044 (三趟推车扛家具累得肩臂酸痛); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-83_

  Mitzi Lechner收下了锅，却没有让Mitzi进门，说东西留下，人先回去。Mitzi在门口站了一会儿，把锅盖掀开又盖上；第二天，空锅被放在门槛上，里面压着一张写着“收到了”的纸条。

  _effects: relation friend with person-mitzi-lechner; relation friend with person-mitzi-lechner; mood -0.02_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-bread-with-friend` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  两个孩子在院子两头跑，一个递，一个夹，床单在风里鼓起来。最后一条挂好，Josefine Hofer从厨房拿出面包，先给了自家孩子，再掰一块给Josef。Josef把面包塞进围裙口袋，回家后放在家里人的碗边，说自己在外面吃过了。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-josefine-hofer; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-both-families` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  Franz Moser把Josef拽到队伍前头，往自己身前塞，说“孩子占不了多大地儿”。窗口一次递出两包面包，Josef把邻居那包夹在胳肢窝，自己这包抱在胸前。到Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang门口，Josef蹲下把两包并排，按Franz Moser说的份量分开，连找零的铜板也分成两小堆。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5_

- **1919.10** `vienna-childhood-street.market-errand/errand-ok` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  Josef在土豆摊前蹲了很久，挑了一磅没有芽眼的。付完钱，手里只剩几个铜板，又去买了盐。围裙兜太浅，Josef用下巴压住布边，走两步就低头看一眼。到家Josef Bauer把土豆倒进盆里，盐包放在灶台边。
  _effects: food; cash -0.15 (市场花销); relation kin with person-josef-bauer_

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-dragged-docked-pay` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  “你看看这角。”Josef Bauer把床单拎起来，煤渣簌簌往下掉。Josef伸手去拍，反而把灰按进布纹里。Josef Bauer从工钱里数回两枚，只留一赫勒在Josef手心，让重洗那角。晚饭前Josef一直蹲在井台边，换了两盆水，指甲缝里全是黑泥。
  _effects: cash 0.01 (被扣后只剩一赫勒); mood -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-josef-bauer; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11_

- **1920.09** `vienna-childhood-street.cigarette-card/card-gift` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  烟草店的门铃响一次，Josef就往柜台边挪一步。Emil终于从柜台底下抽出一张风景卡，用手掌抹了抹，还是皱的。“拿去吧。”Emil说完就去招呼别的客人。Josef把卡举到鼻子前闻，一股烟草味，回家路上把多出来的一张卡分给Emil看。
  _effects: mood 0.06; reputation 0.02; relation friend with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912_

- **1921.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-sold-out-potatoes` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  “卖完了。”三个字从窗口里传出来，Josef后面的队伍立刻散了一半。Josef没走，趴在窗台上看伙计扫面屑，直到人家挥手赶人。旁边摊子的土豆带着泥，Josef用围裙兜住，一枚一枚数铜板，回家报账时连找零都说清了。
  _effects: food; mood -0.03; skill_

- **1922.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-three-heller` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  “拽平，别留褶。”Rosa Winter在绳子那头喊。Josef踮着脚把床单拉直，碱水把指缝泡得发白。收工时天已经黑了，Rosa Winter数了三赫勒，又在围裙上擦干手才递过来。Josef把钱塞进袜筒，回家路上一直攥着，虎口的裂口沾了汗，火辣辣地疼。
  _effects: cash 0.03 (帮邻居晾床单挣的三赫勒); health -0.030000000000000027 (碱水泡手，虎口裂开); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3_

- **1923.01** `vienna-childhood-street.cigarette-card/card-trade` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  “三张换一张。”Lena把皇帝像在Josef眼前晃了晃。Josef数出三张攒了半年的烟画，一张张递过去。皇帝像到手还没捂热，Lena已经推开杂货铺的门，用同一张卡换了糖。Josef把卡举到光下看，觉得皇帝的脸有点歪。
  _effects: mood 0.04; relation friend with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912_

- **1923.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-half-flour` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  面粉袋压在胸口，越抱越沉。Josef前面的大婶一直往后靠，Josef就把膝盖顶住对方的篮子，硬留出半步。买到半袋面粉后，Josef的鞋跟留在人堆里，找回来时鞋面全是泥。回家路上布袋换了两次肩，赤脚踩过石板时脚底发麻。
  _effects: food; health -0.020000000000000018 (赤脚走石板路磨伤脚底); skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-21; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10_

- **1923.12** `p9-childhood-household.name-day-gift/old-shoes` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors, injury
  报纸窸窸窣窣打开，露出一双旧鞋。Josef认出鞋帮上的补丁，是弟妹穿剩下的。Josef把脚伸进去，脚后跟还空着一指，脚趾却挤在前头。Josef Bauer看了看，说垫层布就好了，起身去柜子里翻布头。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000004; relation kin with person-josef-bauer; health -0.020000000000000018 (鞋挤脚走路发疼)_

- **1924.01** `state_tick:wish-craft/craft-pressure/put-away` tags: wish-craft, wish, wish:craft, craft, work, mastery, state, state_tick
  Josef暂时把那件事收进抽屉；日子还有别的东西要应付。
  _effects: mood -0.019999999999999997_

- **1925.01** `r13-edu.school-transfer::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/transfer-approved` tags: education, school, transfer, bureaucracy, family, reform, medical, schooling
  Josef的转学材料缺一张居住证明，Mitzi Hofer 2让Josef去区公所补。Josef跑了三趟，第三趟正赶上下雨，Mitzi Hofer 2看见Josef把湿透的纸一张张摊在柜台上，没再挑毛病，盖了章。Josef赶在开学前一天把名字写进新学校。那间教室在二楼，Josef第一天就找到了靠窗的座位，把旧课本摆好。
  _effects: flag +school_transfer_approved; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-22_

- **1925.02** `r13-turn.exam-failure::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::reform-open-secondary/name-missing` tags: education, exam, failure, school, work, reform, medical, schooling
  补考那天，Josef在考场门口等到钟响，监考说名单上没有名字。Grete Stein跑去教务处，回来时手里只有一张转学建议。Josef去了印刷厂当学徒，师傅说铅字比字母好认，至少不会骗人。
  _effects: condition; flag +left_school_after_exam_failure; role; relation colleague with person-grete-stein; education apprenticeship_

- **1925.03** `yf18-firstlook.tram-glove::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/return-it-at-the-gate` tags: first_love, love, tram, glove, youth, courtship, reform, medical
  下一站，Josef追下车，把那只手套递还给Grete Moser。Grete Moser说了一声谢谢，手指在毛线手套里缩了一下；Josef回到车上才发现自己坐过了两站，只好沿着环城路走回家。
  _effects: state_add yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:82; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52; mood 0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52; flag +yf18_first_awakening_active_

- **1925.04** `r15-work.master-takes-on::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/signed-contract` tags: work, craft, apprentice, trade, poverty, reform, medical, schooling
  Luise Hofer把学徒契约摊在桌上，指给Josef看期限、工钱和出师考试。Josef签了名，第二天站到刨床前，手指上多了第一道木刺。
  _effects: relation employer with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-16; state_add apprenticeship-contract state:world-vienna-1912:81; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-16; education apprenticeship_

- **1925.05** `vienna-adolescence.workshop-apprentice/apprenticeship-accepted` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  作坊里全是松木味。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne把一块方木夹进台钳，让Josef推刨子。Josef推了十分钟，木花卷到脚背上，刨面总算平了。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne用拇指摸了摸，收下Josef，从钱盒里数出三克朗，说是头一周的饭钱。Josef Bauer在门口把围裙攥得很紧。
  _effects: cash 3 ({subject}学徒首周的膳食预付款); education apprenticeship; reputation 0.1; relation kin with person-josef-bauer_

- **1926.01** `yf18-dare.bridge-rail::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/walk-the-rail` tags: dare, bridge, river, friendship, risk, reform, medical, schooling
  多瑙河上的铁桥在夜里只剩两排灯。Josef翻过栏杆，鞋底踩上结霜的钢梁，走了七步；Grete Moser在桥头喊了一声，风把声音吹散。Josef翻回来时，Mitzi把一支烟塞进Josef嘴里，手抖得点不着火。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 45 -> 55; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 25 -> 40; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 0 -> 5; reputation 0.020000000000000004; flag +yf18_walked_bridge_rail; mood 0.05_

  _effects: state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.failures 0 -> 1; cash -1 (wasted material); mood -0.04_

- **1926.01** `state_tick:yf18-first-awakening/half-year-of-glances/answered-glance` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state
  Josef在教堂门口或学校走廊里又遇见Grete Moser。这一次对方先点了头，Josef准备好的整段话全忘了，只把手里的书换到另一只手上；回家后Josef才发现，自己把书拿倒了。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 55 -> 60; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 40 -> 48; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.reciprocated false -> true; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52; mood 0.029999999999999992_

- **1926.01** `p9-childhood-youth-aspiration.join-a-club/joined-and-borrowed` tags: adolescence, education, aspiration, school, books, club, exam, task
  Josef跟着Katharina Kral走进印刷厂后面的屋子，交了十赫勒会费，在借书簿上按了手印。他挑了一本讲蒸汽机的册子，封面烫金已经磨掉；管理员说下月十赫勒要按时交，书才能续借。
  _effects: cash -0.1 (工人青年会月费); skill; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23; flag +workers_youth_member; mood 0.04000000000000001; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23_

  Friedrich Hofer把Josef的名字写进一本硬皮簿子，问一句写一句，最后让他按手印。Josef Bauer赶来时，Friedrich Hofer说带回去好好管教，这次不立案。走出门口，Josef Bauer的手一直攥着Josef的袖子。
  _effects: reputation -0.04000000000000001; flag +police_caution_recorded; relation guardian with person-josef-bauer; knowledge “the police opened a caution file in the subject’s name”_

- **1926.09** `r13-turn.school-leaving::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::reform-leaving-age/signed-apprenticeship` tags: education, school, work, family, transition, reform, medical, schooling
  毕业钟敲过三下，Mitzi Weiss把成绩单按在讲台上，说Josef还能再读一年，只要家里肯出四克朗。家里人在走廊里把学徒契约展开，纸角还带着面粉。Josef签了名，第二天就站到刨床前，手指上多了第一道木刺。
  _effects: cash -2 (毕业钟响过以后); flag +left_school_for_apprenticeship; education apprenticeship_

- **1926.11** `yf18-performance.choir-solo::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/laugh-and-bow` tags: performance, choir, humiliation, school, voice, reform, medical, schooling
  Josef听见自己的声音劈开，索性停下来，对着教堂的穹顶笑了一声。Mitzi Weiss在风琴后面瞪眼，Josef却把那句拉丁文降了八度重新唱了一遍，声音厚得像另一个人；台下有人先笑，随后跟着鼓掌，Anton在最后一排把手套举起来挥了挥。
  _effects: reputation 0.03; relation friend with person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-29; flag +yf18_laughed_at_solo; mood 0.039999999999999994; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-29_

- **1927.01** `yf18-firstlook.parish-dance::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/the-second-dance` tags: first_love, dance, church, courtship, jealousy, reform, medical, schooling
  舞卡上第一支曲子被Elisabeth Bauer写走了。Josef在教会礼堂的木地板上踩错了两步，Elisabeth Bauer说没关系，把手放回Josef背后；第三支曲子响起时，Anton在门口冲Josef比了个手势，Josef假装没看见。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 60 -> 68; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 48 -> 58; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.reciprocated true -> true; mood 0.04000000000000001_

  _effects: flag +left_domestic_service_for_factory; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2; move place-wien-printshop-winter; employment Arbeiterin @ org-winter-print; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2_

  早上醒来，Josef的手指还是麻的。Josef跟Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne说想走，Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne把账本翻到最后一页，扣了午饭钱，把剩下的铜币推过来，少了半克朗。Josef抱着围裙过街，Luise Hofer在门口说，另一家老板脾气急，但从不扣工钱，窗户也一直开着。

  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23; mood -0.03_

- **1928.10** `vienna-adolescence.workshop-apprentice/apprenticeship-rejected` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne捏了捏Josef的虎口，又让Josef张开手指，说骨节还没长开，推硬木会伤手。Josef Bauer想再问一句，Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne已经转身去搬木料。Josef退到门槛外，听见刨子重新响起来，木花从门缝里卷到脚边。
  _effects: mood -0.08; reputation -0.05; relation kin with person-josef-bauer_

- **1929.01** `state_transition:apprenticeship-contract/expelled` tags: apprenticeship-contract, education, craft, apprentice, work, state, state_tick, failed-apprentice
  Josef从「学徒契约」转入另一段处境：the master ended the contract
  _effects: state_transition apprenticeship-contract -> failed-apprentice; flag +failed_apprenticeship; mood -0.08; role_

- **1929.05** `vienna-youth-threshold.sibling-departure/quarrel-who-leaves` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, transition
  车票只够一个人走，晚饭桌上话越说越大声。Elisabeth Bauer说该自己走，Josef说自己也快够岁数了。母亲把汤勺往锅沿一磕，屋里静下来，最后Josef摔门出去在楼梯上坐了半宿，直到楼上有人喊冷。
  _effects: relation kin with person-elisabeth-bauer; mood -0.07_

- **1929.07** `vienna-adolescence.brothers-torn-jacket/sew-it-yourself` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  外套腋下裂了一道口子，里布翻出来。Josef把袖口套在桌角上撑开，穿针时试了三次才引上线。针脚一针长一针短，像蜈蚣。Josef Bauer端着汤进来，站在桌边看了一会儿，把碗往Josef那边推了推，自己只留了半碗。
  _effects: skill; food; relation kin with person-josef-bauer_

- **1930.01** `r13-work.interview-prep::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/borrowed-collar` tags: work, interview, shop, preparation, class, reform, medical, schooling
  Marie Huber把自己结婚时的硬领借给Josef，又用湿布把领口擦了一遍。镜子只有巴掌大，Josef练了三遍怎么进门、怎么把帽子拿在手里。Elisabeth Bauer 4的柜台在两条街外，招的是会写字的人；Josef把地址抄在纸片上，睡前又看了一遍。
  _effects: cash -1 (借来的硬领); flag +work13_prepared_for_interview; skill; relation friend with person-marie-huber; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34_

- **1930.05** `ad20-apprenticeship-review::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/kept-on` tags: craft, apprentice, work, review, everyday, stigma, rural, agriculture
  Magistrat der k. k. Reichshaupt- und Residenzstadt Wien把Josef做的榫头拿在手里，对着光看了很久，又用指甲刮了刮接缝。Josef站在旁边，听见自己的心跳。Magistrat der k. k. Reichshaupt- und Residenzstadt Wien最后把榫头放回工作台，说“明天开始，你自己接活”。
  _effects: skill; flag +apprenticeship_passed; mood 0.039999999999999994; cash 1 (the first independent job)_

- **1930.09** `ad20-police-stop::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/the-name-is-not-written` tags: police, legal, street, everyday, obligation, risk, rural, agriculture
  Friedrich Hofer把Josef带到街角，说今天的事可以不记在册子上，但下回需要Josef帮个小忙。Josef问什么忙；Friedrich Hofer说“到时候会有人找你”。他走后，Josef在路灯下站了很久，觉得自己的名字比刚才轻了一点。
  _effects: state_add ad20-private-bargain state:world-vienna-1912:264; flag +police_bargain; relation acquaintance with person-friedrich-hofer; mood -0.06; knowledge “警察说今天的事可以不记在册子上，但以后会有人来找。”; flag +private_bargain_active_

- **1930.11** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/hospital-night` tags: health, medical, family, money
  电车在环城大道上哐当响，Franz Bauer 2攥着Josef的胳膊，怕人从座位上滑下去。急诊厅的煤气灯发黄，长椅上全是咳嗽声，押金窗口的铜盘递出两克朗，换来一张硬纸号牌。
  _effects: cash -2 (医院急诊押金); health 0.08 (急诊观察和冰敷退烧); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-58; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-58_

- **1930.11** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.family-table/leave-before-dessert` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, partnership
  问到后来，Josef Bauer提起别家有个更稳当的差事。Katharina Kral把餐巾往桌上一放，说吃好了，先走。两人在楼梯间站住，Katharina Kral说不是Josef的错，可下次再来还得一样坐这张桌子；Josef心里松了一点，也知道这顿饭没吃完，事情并没有过去。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23; relation kin with person-josef-bauer; mood 0.03_

- **1931.01** `state_tick:ad20-private-bargain/the-favor-ages/called-in` tags: ad20-private-bargain, private, bargain, favor, debt, trust, leverage, exit
  对方在一个不合时宜的时刻提起旧事，语气很客气，意思却不客气。Josef听见自己答应得太快，挂断以后才发现手心有一层薄汗。
  _effects: state_parameter ad20-private-bargain.leverage 60 -> 68; state_parameter ad20-private-bargain.debt 50 -> 54; state_parameter ad20-private-bargain.trust 40 -> 36; mood -0.03_

- **1931.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.last-standing-ticket/p9-social-daily-culture.last-standing-ticket.buy-for-elder` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:culture
  前面的老人掏零钱时手抖得厉害，数了三遍也没凑够。Josef替老人把四十赫勒递进窗口，老人连声道谢，硬塞回一枚十赫勒铜板。等Josef自己买票时，窗口已经挂出“售罄”的牌子，只好站在剧院门廊下听里面隐约的乐声，脚底冰凉；这一趟净少三十赫勒，却让队伍里的人都记住了这份体面。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (替排队的老人垫付站票钱); cash 0.1 (老人硬塞回的铜板); reputation 0.04000000000000001; mood -0.03_

- **1931.02** `p10-work-career.first-position/p10-work-career.first-position.turned-away` tags: adulthood, work, career, wages, injury, pension, employment, first_job
  介绍所的柜台前还有几个人在等，Gustav Berg把一张印好的通知推过来，用铅笔在“星期三”下面画了一道。他把纸条折成四折，塞进上衣内袋。
  _effects: flag +seeking_work; knowledge “下星期三早上七点，另一家机器厂在职业介绍所登记招上油工”; mood -0.05999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-40_

- **1931.08** `vienna-legacy-worklife.apprentice-wage/apprentice-wage.ask-master` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  掌柜的木尺敲了两下柜台，声音短促。Josef把问题又问了一遍，掌柜才说师兄是“老规矩进来的”。师兄这时转过身，笑了一下，笑得不太自然，说“没事，你问得对”。
  _effects: reputation -0.04; relation colleague with person-gustav-berg; skill_

- **1931.09** `oj18-zoo.night::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/child-at-the-bear-pit` tags: zoo, animals, night, keeper, work, odd_job, suspense, reform
  一个男孩翻过熊池的矮栏去捡帽子。Josef没有喊，怕惊动母熊，只提着装苹果的桶绕到下风处，把桶敲得咚咚响。母熊转身时，孩子被Elisabeth Huber一把拽了上来；Josef的腿在后来的很多年里还会在梦里发软。
  _effects: reputation 0.05999999999999999; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-67; cash 2 (reward from the child's parents); state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:314; mood -0.01999999999999999; flag +zoo_night_shift; flag +saved_child_at_zoo; knowledge “母熊被苹果桶引开时，孩子已经挂在{keeper}的胳膊上了。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-67; role; flag +odd_job_apprentice_

- **1931.12** `vienna-parish-aid.sick-call/sick-call.with-priest` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Josef觉得一个人去不合适，绕到教堂请了修女一同上楼。修女看过病人，留下药包和一小瓶圣水，说会从教区账上出钱请医生来看一趟。Otto Bauer在楼下等着，说这样妥当，病人家里没男人，多个见证人心里安稳。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10; mood 0.04; flag +parish_sick_visitor_

- **1932.01** `state_reaction:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/the-thing-in-the-basement` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  夜班结束前，Josef听见地下室传来拖拽声。下去时，门开着，地上只有一道湿拖把的痕迹；第二天，所有人都说那间屋子锁了十年。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 18 -> 26; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 38 -> 35; mood -0.03; knowledge “地下室那扇门后面有东西被搬动过，但巡查簿上没有记录。”_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:005441_

- **1932.01** `vienna-family-divorce/separate-under-one-roof` tags: family, marriage, divorce, housing, lifecycle, separation
  Josef和Katharina Kral把床铺分开，米和煤也各算各的。两人仍住在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang，因为谁都没有钱再租一间房；孩子在两床之间来回，三克朗买了隔开的锅碗和煤油，家里从此有两本账，夜里两盏灯先后熄灭。
  _effects: relation_end partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23; mood -0.07999999999999999; mood -0.07999999999999999; cash -3 (separate household goods)_

- **1932.01** `oj18-zoo.escape::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/hid-the-chimp` tags: zoo, animals, escape, night, blame, work, odd_job, reform
  马戏班老板Anna Bauer在普拉特门口拦住Josef，说愿意出三十克朗买那只黑猩猩，还缺一个懂它脾气的驯养人。Josef没有答应，也没有报告；夜里，Josef把卡斯帕藏进废弃的鸟舍，每天多带一份面包。
  _effects: cash -1 (bread for the hidden chimpanzee); relation acquaintance with person-anna-bauer; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 26 -> 34; state_add oj18-workplace-legend state:world-vienna-1912:349; mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +hid_chimp; knowledge “卡斯帕藏在废弃鸟舍里，听见面包纸响就会从梁上下来。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-42; role; flag +workplace_legend_

- **1932.01** `state_expire:ad20-private-bargain` tags: ad20-private-bargain, private, bargain, favor, debt, trust, leverage, exit
  Josef的「私下的账」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove ad20-private-bargain state:world-vienna-1912:264; flag -private_bargain_active_

- **1932.01** `state_tick:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/strange-year/the-logbook-mislaid` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  巡查簿在换班时不见了，第二天却出现在煤筐底下，最后几页被水泡得发皱。管事的说签字不能算数，扣了Josef半个月的夜班钱；Josef没有争，只在新的簿子第一页把日期写得很大。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 35 -> 19; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.reliability 65 -> 55; mood -0.05000000000000002; flag +odd_job_scare_

- **1932.01** `state_tick:wish-craft/craft-pressure/press-on` tags: wish-craft, wish, wish:craft, craft, work, mastery, state, state_tick
  Josef又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。
  _effects: mood 0.01999999999999999_

- **1932.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.lending-library/p9-social-daily-culture.lending-library.volksbibliothek` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:knowledge
  Josef听Otto Bauer 2报出押金数目，把伸进内袋的手又抽了回来。问清免费阅览处的位置后，Josef穿过两条街，在 Volksbibliothek 的阅览室门口登记了姓名，坐在长桌边读了一下午；一个赫勒没花，只是不能把书带回家，天黑前就得还回去。
  _effects: mood 0.03; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-39; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-39_

- **1932.04** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.letter-answer/reply-at-bakery` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, partnership
  第四天，Elisabeth Kral的回信从门缝底下塞进来，纸边被门槛磨毛了。Elisabeth Kral约Josef星期六早上在街角面包店门口见，说想买一个两赫勒的小圆面包，两个人分。Maria Kral在楼道里撞见Josef读信，笑着问是不是好消息；Josef把信折回四折，塞进内袋贴着胸口。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-78; relation acquaintance with person-maria-kral; mood 0.07; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-78_

- **1932.06** `df18-mark.first-appearance::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/fever-hand` tags: body, mark, omen, illness, rumor, supernatural, eerie, reform
  一连三天的高烧退下去以后，Josef的手腕上多了一块暗褐色的印子，边缘像五根很短的手指。诊所的医生说只是皮下色素，Josefine Hofer却在院子里看了很久，说这形状不是随便长的。Josef把袖子拉下来，回家路上一直用另一只手按着那块皮。
  _effects: cash -1 (去诊所问这块印子); state_add df18-uncanny-mark state:world-vienna-1912:350; flag +df18_mark_appeared_after_fever; mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36; flag +df18_mark_noticed; knowledge “我身上有一处说不清的痕迹，别人对它各有各的说法。”_

- **1933.01** `df18-mark.reading-at-market::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/read-as-curse` tags: body, mark, omen, rumor, superstition, market, reform, medical
  肉摊的Rosa Lechner看见Josef挽袖子，刀停在半空，说这种印子像吊死的人留下的手印。旁边买菜的人安静了一瞬，又各自低头挑菜。Josef想笑，却发现自己的手在抖；那天回家，Josef绕开了平时走的巷子。
  _effects: state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.attention 25 -> 40; state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.claimed illness -> curse; reputation -0.04000000000000001; relation acquaintance with person-rosa-lechner; flag +df18_mark_read_as_curse; mood -0.03_

- **1933.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_burns_curtain` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Josef在公用灶台上热猪油，油星子溅到旁边晾着的床单上，火苗顺着床单爬上木隔板。Katharina Kral拎起水桶泼过去，火灭了，但床单烧掉半幅，隔板熏黑一片。房东扣了那户人家两克朗押金，那家的女人在楼道里哭了一下午。
  _effects: cash -2 (赔给房东的隔板熏黑损失); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23_

- **1933.01** `state_expire:failed-apprentice` tags: failed-apprentice, craft, work, failure, stigma, state, state_tick, state:failed-apprentice
  Josef的「学徒契约终止」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove failed-apprentice state:world-vienna-1912:234_

- **1933.01** `state_tick:oj18-workplace-legend/story-year/story-grows-teeth` tags: oj18-workplace-legend, work, legend, odd_job, rumor, identity, night, state
  故事传到第三个街区时，已经多出一只会说话的猫和两具没有头的尸体。Josef想纠正，发现自己越解释，听的人越兴奋；酒馆老板干脆把最离谱的版本写在黑板上，下面画了一个箭头：本店常客。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-workplace-legend.legend 20 -> 28; state_parameter oj18-workplace-legend.truth 55 -> 50; state_parameter oj18-workplace-legend.toldCount 0 -> 2; mood -0.01999999999999999; flag +legend_exaggerated_

- **1933.01** `state_tick:df18-uncanny-mark/mark-year/kept-small` tags: df18-uncanny-mark, body, mark, omen, superstition, reputation, mystery, faith
  又过了一年，Josef学会了在洗澡时不去盯那块印子。它还在，边缘有时发红，有时像普通的旧疤；街坊有了新的闲话，Josef的袖子也换了更长的款式。
  _effects: state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.attention 40 -> 34; state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.pain 5 -> 1; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1933.01** `r13-civ.enter-hospital::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/admitted-third-class` tags: health, hospital, admission, deposit, institution, reform, medical, schooling
  Friedrich Hofer把押金收据和住院单钉在一起，让Josef去三楼东侧等床。走廊里全是折叠床，Franz Weiss给Josef一条洗得发硬的毯子，说今晚先别喝水。Josef把鞋塞在枕头下面，听着隔壁的呻吟睡了过去。
  _effects: cash -8 (住院处窗口的押金); health 0.05999999999999999 (住院处窗口的押金); condition; flag +hospitalized_third_class; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-65; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-65_

- **1933.07** `vienna-legacy-worklife.sick-fund/sick-fund.fill-form` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  表格有四栏，第四栏要写“工种及所属行会”。Josef问工友平时怎么称呼自己的活，工友靠在机器旁边说“就是上油”。办事员的印章是木柄的，按下去时咔的一声。
  _effects: relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5; skill_

- **1933.10** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.help-wash` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Josef喝完汤没走，留下来帮修女把大锅刷净，又把木碗按大小摞好。厨房后头有半块面包和一碗剩汤，修女说是帮忙人的午饭。Friedrich Lechner在门口等着一起回去，说下回还来，洗锅的活总有人要干。
  _effects: food; skill; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000305:child; mood 0.01999999999999999_

- **1934.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/compromise_split_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Josef把洗好的床单搭上院子里的公用晾衣绳，占了整条。Katharina Kral抱着一盆湿衣服下来，说自家孩子明天要穿干净衬衫上学。两人在绳子两头扯了一阵，最后Josef把床单对折，腾出一半，Katharina Kral用木夹子把衬衫一件件排上去。谁也没道歉，但第二天见面都点了头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23; mood 0.01999999999999999_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:006111_

- **1934.01** `df18-mark.reading-at-market::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/read-as-blessing` tags: body, mark, omen, rumor, superstition, market, vienna, urban
  市场尽头的Ferdinand Lechner 2会看手相。Josef把铜币放在桌上，Ferdinand Lechner 2捏着手腕看了半天，说这不是病，是“有人替你许过愿”，还送了一条红绳。Josef把红绳系在袖口里，走出棚子时，觉得路上的人都在看自己的手。
  _effects: cash -1 (看手相的铜币); state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.attention 34 -> 44; state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.claimed curse -> blessing; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-40; flag +df18_mark_read_as_blessing; mood 0.01999999999999999_

- **1934.01** `state_reaction:df18-uncanny-mark/mark-public` tags: df18-uncanny-mark, body, mark, omen, superstition, reputation, mystery, faith
  Josef在人群里挽了一下袖子，第二天就有人来问那块印子是不是“会动的”。Josef说不会，问的人却已经把自己的解释说完了。
  _effects: state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.attention 44 -> 52; mood -0.01999999999999999; reputation -0.009999999999999995_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:006658_

- **1934.01** `state_tick:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/strange-year/blamed-for-the-noise` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  楼上住户投诉夜里有人拖椅子，管事的把巡查簿翻到Josef的那一页，说签字太潦草，看不出巡过几趟。Josef想辩解，却想起那晚确实在楼梯间多站了一刻钟；扣掉的工钱刚好够买一双不漏水的鞋。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 19 -> 3; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.reliability 55 -> 45; mood -0.05000000000000002_

- **1934.01** `state_expire:oj18-workplace-legend` tags: oj18-workplace-legend, work, legend, odd_job, rumor, identity, night, state
  Josef的「职场传说」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove oj18-workplace-legend state:world-vienna-1912:349; role; flag -workplace_legend_

- **1934.01** `state_tick:df18-uncanny-mark/mark-year/story-grows` tags: df18-uncanny-mark, body, mark, omen, superstition, reputation, mystery, faith
  入冬后，Josef在集市上听见一个陌生女人向别人描述“那个有手印的人”。故事已经比原来多了一个雨夜和一声尖叫。Josef低头挑菜，发现自己的手正按着袖口。
  _effects: state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.attention 52 -> 62; state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.pain 1 -> 7; state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.claimed blessing -> curse; reputation -0.020000000000000004; mood -0.02999999999999997_

- **1934.01** `r13-fam.write-missing-relative::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/letter-returned` tags: task, family, letter, missing, war, literacy, vienna, urban
  信过了三个星期被退回来，信封上盖着“查无此人”，边角已经磨毛。Grete Berg不认得那四个字，让Josef念；Josef念成“还在路上”，把信折好放进抽屉。Grete Berg点点头，第二天又问有没有回信。
  _effects: cash -1 (没有地址的信); mood -0.12; condition; flag +letter_returned_undeliverable; relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-6; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-6_

- **1934.02** `r1-cul.secular-ritual::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/unconsecrated-ground` tags: faith, secular, funeral, ritual, institution, vienna, urban, crisis
  墓地管理处说没有教会证明不能入土。Josef带着Rosa跑了三趟，最后在城郊找到一块不属于任何教区的地。下葬那天只有五个人，Josef用木匠的折尺在土上画了一个十字，又用靴子抹掉。
  _effects: cash -3 (没有十字架的葬礼); condition; flag +buried_without_church; role; relation friend with person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912; knowledge “the plot belongs to no parish”_

- **1934.06** `r1-hc.building-collapse::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/attic-floor-gives` tags: home, collapse, housing, landlord, inspection, vienna, urban, crisis
  房东在阁楼上又隔出一间，横梁是旧的，钉子直接钉进砖缝。夜里Josef被一声闷响惊醒，天花板塌下一角，石灰落在脸上；隔壁的床已经掉到下一层，被褥挂在梁上。
  _effects: cash -10 (阁楼加出来的那间房); condition; flag +attic_floor_collapsed; role; knowledge “the new partition was nailed into a rotten beam”_

- **1934.07** `ad20-shop-opening::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-street-watches` tags: shop, business, opening, everyday, public, obligation, vienna, urban
  Josef把新店的招牌挂上去时，街对面已经站了几个人。第一位客人买了一包针，第二位问能不能赊账，第三位什么也没买，只说“看看”。到傍晚，Josef发现自己记住了每一张脸，却忘了算今天的账。
  _effects: state_add ad20-public-attention state:world-vienna-1912:422; cash 3 (the first day’s takings); reputation 0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41; flag +shop_opened; mood 0.04000000000000001; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-46; flag +public_attention_active_

- **1935.01** `state_expire:oj18-odd-apprenticeship` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  Josef的「奇职学徒」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:314; role; flag -odd_job_apprentice_

- **1935.01** `state_tick:df18-uncanny-mark/mark-year/kept-small` tags: df18-uncanny-mark, body, mark, omen, superstition, reputation, mystery, faith
  又过了一年，Josef学会了在洗澡时不去盯那块印子。它还在，边缘有时发红，有时像普通的旧疤；街坊有了新的闲话，Josef的袖子也换了更长的款式。
  _effects: state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.attention 62 -> 56; state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.pain 7 -> 3; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1935.01** `state_tick:ad20-public-attention/the-attention-cycle/warms` tags: ad20-public-attention, public, attention, reputation, media, everyday, risk, opportunity
  Josef在街角被人认出来，对方没有要求什么，只说“上次那件事做得不错”。这句客气话被旁边的人听见，下午便有两个陌生人朝Josef点头。关注没有减少，但空气松了一点。
  _effects: state_parameter ad20-public-attention.attention 40 -> 36; state_parameter ad20-public-attention.sympathy 45 -> 51; state_parameter ad20-public-attention.opportunity 50 -> 54; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1935.01** `task-vienna-bourgeois-youth.concert-outing/balcony-bread` tags: bourgeois, education, culture, salon, university, family, task, task:culture
  Josef买了两张最便宜的楼座票，一共四克朗。Elisabeth Bauer家里起初不准，说星期天要回家吃饭，后来松了口。幕间两人分了一块带来的面包，音乐厅的暖气把外套烘得发烫。
  _effects: cash -4 (两张楼座票); mood 0.09; skill_

- **1935.04** `r3-mig.border-village::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/crossed-under-the-hay` tags: migration, border, village, smuggling, risk, vienna, urban, crisis
  Otto Winter把Josef藏在干草下面，赶着马车走田埂。经过界碑时，Mitzi Hofer 2抬手拦了一下，Otto Winter说车上是喂牛的草，Mitzi Hofer 2用刺刀往草里扎了两下，没扎到底。马车走出半里地，Otto Winter才让Josef出来换气。
  _effects: cash -6 (教堂钟声两边都听得见); condition; flag +crossed_under_hay; relation friend with person-otto-winter; knowledge “the patrol probes hay with a bayonet but not to the bottom”_

- **1935.08** `vienna-parish-aid.sick-call/sick-call.help-in` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Josef和Hermann Bauer一起上楼，门没锁，屋里一股酸味。病人裹着毯子坐在床沿咳嗽，炉子只剩灰。Josef去楼下讨了几块煤核，蹲着把火生起来，又用带来的面粉搅了一小锅稀粥。药粉冲开，碗底沉着白渣，Josef先垫了药钱，说以后再说。
  _effects: food; cash -0.6 (替病人垫上药粉钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7_

- **1935.10** `hs18-cinema.newsreel::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/clap-with-the-crowd` tags: cinema, newsreel, neighbor, recognition, media, history, vienna, urban
  新闻片放到新修的桥，观众跟着鼓掌。Josef也拍了两下手，掌心却停在半空——画面角落里那家面包店，昨天还排着另一条队。Emil说下回还来，Josef说票价太贵。
  _effects: cash -1 (two cinema tickets); mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +clapped_at_the_newsreel_

  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Luise Bauer这周主动擦了黑板。Josef把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。

  介绍时把两边都夸过了头，说老友是印厂里最靠得住的人，说女工的手艺整条街都找不出第二个。话太多，两人反倒拘谨起来，各自看窗外。咖啡凉了，坐了不到一个钟头就散了，出门时谁也没提再见面。

  腌鲱鱼切成两段，一段今天吃，一段留着。黑面包硬得要用刀锯。Mitzi Hofer来借盐，眼睛落在鱼上，说可以用半棵卷心菜换一块。Josef切了一指宽递过去。
  _effects: cash -2 (腌鲱鱼和黑面包); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1_

- **1936.06** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/barber-pull` tags: health, medical, family, money
  理发铺里还坐着等刮脸的老主顾，肥皂味和烟味混在一起。弗朗茨把围布往Josef胸前一系，说忍着点。钳子第一次滑脱，第二次才夹稳，牙出来时带着一小块肉，他用明矾水按住了。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (理发师拔牙收费); health 0.03 (病牙被拔除但手法粗糙); mood -0.01999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-55_

  Josef没有立刻答话，先去敲Mitzi Bauer 3的门。两人一起数落墙上的水渍和门框里灌进来的风，楼梯间的窗户修好了，自家墙皮还在剥落。Mitzi Bauer 3提议一起把话带给看门人：先修好漏水的墙角，涨租的事再谈。Josef把纸重新折好，请他把话带到门外。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-72; mood 0.03; flag +rent_dispute; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-72_

  警察局的桌子后面的人把哥哥的名字写进名册，撕下一张编号条。Josef在走廊里碰到一个女人，她找丈夫，手里也捏着一张条子。两人跑了两处收容所，登记簿翻到最后一页也没有。
  _effects: mood -0.09999999999999998; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; flag +brother_missing_registered_

- **1937.03** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/sweat-it-out` tags: health, medical, family, money
  Josef说胡话时念着车间的排班表，Friedrich Hofer一句句应着，拿小勺往嘴里送温水。床头那半瓶退烧药早见了底，她没舍得倒最后一滴，留着天亮再说。
  _effects: condition; relation friend with person-friedrich-hofer_

- **1937.04** `p9-gap-adult-unemployed.dock-morning/dock-picked-coal` tags: adulthood, unemployment, casual_labour, poverty, daily, dock
  多瑙河码头的雾还没散，工头捏着名单念了Josef的名字。煤灰落在汗湿的脖颈上，Josef Lechner在铁门边看着，说这活儿一天顶三天，可肩膀得疼上一个礼拜。
  _effects: cash 4.2 (卸煤半日的工钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33_

- **1937.08** `vienna-life-overcrowded-flat/take-in-a-lodger` tags: housing, home, health, family
  Josef把厨房角落隔出一块，租给一个在附近上工的学徒。每月多四克朗，但煤、水和厕所都要排队；夜里有人咳嗽，Josef睡不踏实，第二天眼下发青，孩子也在床上翻来覆去，四克朗换来一屋子醒着的人。
  _effects: cash 4 (lodger rent); condition_

- **1937.10** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.quit` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Josef说互助会先退了，这笔钱要留给煤和面包。Elisabeth Huber没争辩，在蓝皮本上把名字划了一道，问清楚是不是全家都退，然后把划掉的那行折进本子夹缝。她说以后想再进，要重新排三个月。门关上，Josef听见铅笔在楼梯上轻轻敲了两下栏杆。
  _effects: cash 0.4 (省下本周会费留作煤钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-67; flag +funeral_society_left_

  _effects: condition; role; cash -5 (fine and lost work)_
