  介绍时把两边都夸过了头，说老友是印厂里最靠得住的人，说女工的手艺整条街都找不出第二个。话太多，两人反倒拘谨起来，各自看窗外。咖啡凉了，坐了不到一个钟头就散了，出门时谁也没提再见面。
  _effects: cash -0.9 (三杯咖啡和点心); relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000957:child; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; mood -0.04999999999999999_

- **1938.06** `vienna-medical-treatment.sanatorium/ignore-diagnosis` tags: health, medical, family, money
  下午的活是搬纱锭，Josef蹲下去再站起来眼前发黑，扶墙站了一会儿。工友老约瑟夫把自己的水杯推过来，说去诊所开个假条吧，她摇头，说假条要扣钱。
  _effects: condition; mood -0.03999999999999998_

- **1938.10** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/compromise_split_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Josef把洗好的床单搭上院子里的公用晾衣绳，占了整条。Katharina Hofer抱着一盆湿衣服下来，说自家孩子明天要穿干净衬衫上学。两人在绳子两头扯了一阵，最后Josef把床单对折，腾出一半，Katharina Hofer用木夹子把衬衫一件件排上去。谁也没道歉，但第二天见面都点了头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1938.12** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.window-argument/window-argument.peek` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, neighbor, overheard
  Josef下楼倒水，在楼梯口看见院子里站着一对夫妻，男人的围裙上还沾着面粉，女的攥着一张工钱条。她没插话，提着壶又上楼，关门时听见那女人说“一星期八克朗怎么够”。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; skill_

- **1939.01** `vienna-life-wwii-conscription/wounded` tags: war, conscription, adulthood
  Josef在前线被弹片击中腿部，被送回维也纳的医院。Friedrich Hofer在站台上念了名字，Josef拄着拐杖下车，裤管空了一截。
  _effects: condition; role; cash 12 (military pension)_

- **1939.01** `r14-soc.club-election::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/won-by-three-votes` tags: task, club_election, club, status, community, vienna, urban, crisis
  合唱团在酒馆后厅改选，票箱是一只装饼干的铁盒。唱票唱到第三遍，Josef比Franz Weiss多三票。Franz Weiss把账本推过来，说“你接”。散会后，两人在门口抽完同一支烟，谁也没有先提那三票。账本第一页记着上次义卖的收入，数字旁边有一道被擦过的铅笔痕。
  _effects: mood 0.12; reputation 0.08; flag +won_club_election; role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-65_

- **1939.02** `major-crisis.rent-increase/tenant-meeting` tags: crisis, housing, rent, eviction, family, poverty
  巷口洗衣房的老板娘把几张椅子摆开，叫来半条街的租户。Josef把涨租单摊在桌上，逐条念出上面的数字；Franz Winter站在门边，既没走，也没替房东说话。大家决定先集体迟交一个月，签名纸传到Josef手里时，纸上已经有一块油渍。
  _effects: cash -2 (paper, ink and tram fare for the tenants meeting); role; flag +rent_strike; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001103:child; reputation 0.03_

- **1939.03** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_found_by_neighbor` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Josef傍晚从面粉厂下工回来，发现挂在腰带上的房门钥匙不见了，在楼梯间来回找了四趟。Mitzi Hofer在二楼拐角的煤堆旁捡到那把铜钥匙，钥匙上缠着红线，一眼就认出来。Mitzi Hofer敲开Josef的门，把钥匙拍在桌上，说自己眼神好使。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; mood 0.04999999999999999_

- **1939.06** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.river-walk/river-walk.turned-back` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, walk, river
  Josef走到半路风忽然大起来，围巾抽在脸上生疼，她掉头往回走。路过面包店时橱窗里还剩几个昨天的圆面包，四赫勒一个，她买了两个，揣在怀里带回去当晚饭。
  _effects: food; cash -0.08 (两个剩面包四赫勒一个)_

- **1940.01** `ad20-child-teacher::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-teacher-has-a-question` tags: family, child, school, teacher, everyday, public, vienna, urban
  Ferdinand Lechner 2问Josef，Luise Bauer最近为什么总在课上打瞌睡。Josef说家里晚上在赶一批活；Ferdinand Lechner 2点点头，说“别让孩子熬太晚”。Josef应了一声，心里却在算那批活还能不能按时交。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-40; state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 28 -> 30; mood 0.01999999999999999; flag +teacher_asked_homework_

- **1940.01** `kernel-crisis.arrest-questioning::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/questioning-informs` tags: crisis, police, questioning, repression, vienna, urban, war, industrial
  讯问的人把一张名单推到Josef面前，说只要指出一个名字就能回家。Josef看了很久，指了Anton Bauer；第二天那人被带走问话，Josef拿到五克朗“车马费”，却再没敢走那条街。名单上的人名他认得一半，另一半是后来添的。
  _effects: role; flag +informed_on_neighbor; cash 5 (five crowns of “travel money” after the statement); knowledge “讯问桌上有一张名单，指出一个名字就能回家；车马费是五克朗，邻居第二天被带走。”_

- **1940.01** `state_tick:wish-craft/craft-pressure/press-on` tags: wish-craft, wish, wish:craft, craft, work, mastery, state, state_tick
  Josef又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。
  _effects: mood 0.01999999999999999_

- **1940.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.lending-library/p9-social-daily-culture.lending-library.read-in-aisle` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:knowledge
  Josef站在书架前抽出一本厚小说，就着门口的光读了半章，Luise Bauer 2在柜台后抬起眼皮看了一眼，没说话。读到要紧处，Josef摸出铅笔头把页码记在车票背面，把书插回原处，说下次带够押金再来；白读半章，管理员的眼神却凉了一点。
  _effects: skill; mood 0.01999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-85; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-85_

- **1940.05** `r2-law.savings-passbook::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/passbook-pledged` tags: finance, bank, savings, household, contract, vienna, urban, crisis
  Elisabeth Kral没有商量，把存折拿去给Josef的兄弟做担保。Josef发现时，存折上多了一行陌生人的名字和一枚蓝章。Elisabeth Kral说只是借三个月；Rosa Weiss 2在柜台后听见，低头把算盘珠子拨回去，没有抬头。
  _effects: cash 10 (存折上的蓝格子); condition; flag +passbook_used_as_collateral; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-78; knowledge “the passbook now guarantees a stranger’s loan”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66_

- **1940.08** `lc17-care.offers-to-care::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/moves-the-bed-in` tags: care, family, aging, health, household, duty, vienna, urban
  Josef Bauer在楼梯口喘了两次才上到三楼。Josef没有开会，也没有和谁商量，晚上就把折叠床搬进走廊尽头的小房间，把闹钟拨到凌晨两点。第二天，Josef在药盒上贴了第一张写着时间的纸。
  _effects: state_add lc17-caregiver state:world-vienna-1912:598; relation kin with person-josef-bauer; mood 0.01999999999999999; flag +moved_parent_into_the_flat_

- **1941.01** `r15-fam.school-registration::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/enrolls` tags: family, child, school, education, school_child, vienna, urban, crisis
  Josef带着Luise Bauer去学校登记。孩子的名字被写在名册最后一行，墨水还没有干；回家的路上，Luise Bauer一直捏着那张课程表，Josef则在算这个月还能剩下多少煤钱。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 51 -> 45; state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 38 -> 20; state_parameter raising-child.childConfidence 42 -> 55; state_parameter raising-child.careDebt 4 -> 0; state_parameter raising-child.schoolEnrolled false -> true; state_parameter raising-child.leftHome false -> false; cash -1 (the first school books and a slate); mood 0.03_

- **1941.01** `state_tick:lc17-caregiver/care-year/worn-thin` tags: lc17-caregiver, care, family, illness, elder, duty, household, state
  Josef Bauer今年摔了两次，第二次把胯骨摔裂了。Josef在医院走廊的硬椅上睡了四夜，回家以后发现厂里已经把位置给了别人；药费单、住院单和一张停工的证明一起压在桌玻璃下面，边角每天都被翻卷一点。
  _effects: state_parameter lc17-caregiver.burden 30 -> 38; state_parameter lc17-caregiver.resentment 10 -> 16; cash -2 (medicine and hospital fees); mood -0.04000000000000001_

- **1941.01** `kernel-family.child-school::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::child-school-crisis/child-school.market-hours` tags: task, task:education, family, school, childhood, class, vienna, urban
  Luise Bauer每天清晨去市场替摊主搬菜，下午才到学校，Elisabeth Kral让他站在最后一排。月底，Josef用孩子挣的五克朗买了面粉和煤，Luise Bauer的作业本仍停在第一页。老师把作业本合上，说下学期再看。
  _effects: cash 5 (the child’s wages from morning market work); education primary; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-78; role; flag +missed_school_for_work_

- **1941.02** `bc18-crime.inept-blackmail::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/photographer-sells-print` tags: crime, blackmail, photograph, mistake, humiliation, absurd, reputation, vienna
  Elisabeth Kral把那张照片卖给了《晨报》，配文写“送别者的疲惫”。Josef收到两克朗模特费和一份报纸；照片里的Josef歪着头，嘴半张，确实像哭过。邻居开始叫Josef“那个在葬礼上睡着的人”，语气不算尊敬，也不完全是嘲笑。
  _effects: cash 2 (报纸给的照片使用费); reputation 0.01999999999999999; state_add bc18-ridiculous-fame state:world-vienna-1912:627; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-78; flag +funeral_photo_in_the_paper; mood 0.009999999999999995; flag +ridiculously_famous; reputation 0.01999999999999999; mood 0.020000000000000004_

- **1941.02** `state_reaction:wish-craft/craft-humiliation` tags: wish-craft, wish, wish:craft, craft, work, mastery, state, state_reaction
  Josef把那句轻视记了很久。它没有变成一个决定，但也没有消失。
  _effects: mood -0.04000000000000001_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:010554_

- **1941.08** `r2-law.inflation-savings::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/bought-foreign-currency` tags: finance, inflation, savings, scarcity, market, vienna, urban, crisis
  Franz Kral在巷口收本币，按黑市价换外币。Josef换了三张皱巴巴的美元，藏在鞋垫下面。Franz Weiss看见交易，低声说这是违法的；Franz Kral说违法的事多了，面包不违法，可你买不起。Josef回家路上一直觉得鞋里有石头。
  _effects: cash -20 (一麻袋钞票买一块面包); condition; flag +converted_savings_to_foreign_currency; knowledge “the black-market dollar rate is four times the official rate”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-87_

- **1941.09** `ad20-apprenticeship-review::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/tools-returned` tags: craft, apprentice, work, review, everyday, stigma, vienna, urban
  Wiener Psychoanalytische Vereinigung把Josef的工具一件件摆在桌上，凿子、刨子、尺，摆得很整齐。Josef知道这是什么意思。Wiener Psychoanalytische Vereinigung说不是手艺不好，是“店里养不起”。Josef把工具收进布袋，布袋比来时轻，因为有一把凿子已经磨短了。
  _effects: state_add failed-apprentice state:world-vienna-1912:628; relation employer with person-sigmund-freud; flag +apprenticeship_failed; mood -0.06; cash -1 (tools repaired before returning them)_

- **1942.01** `pc20-apprentice.second-workshop::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/sweep-up` tags: craft, apprentice, failure, workshop, second_chance, tools, patronage, vienna
  Josef把工作台擦干净，把刨花扫进麻袋，最后把钥匙还给Maria Moser。Maria Moser没有挽留，只让Josef把门口那块旧木牌摘下来；Josef照做了，木牌背面还留着当年没刻完的名字。
  _effects: state_add pc20-second-chance state:world-vienna-1912:672; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33; cash -1 (the unpaid rent for the bench); flag +swept_the_bench_and_left; knowledge “旧木牌背面还有当年没刻完的名字，{subject}没有把它翻过来。”; mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +second_chance_open_

- **1942.01** `state_tick:lc17-caregiver/care-year/carried` tags: lc17-caregiver, care, family, illness, elder, duty, household, state
  Josef又撑过一年。药盒按星期分好，账本上多了一栏“看护”，工钱少了一些，但还没有断；Josef Bauer今年记得住Josef的名字，偶尔也记得住昨天吃了什么。
  _effects: state_parameter lc17-caregiver.burden 38 -> 34; state_parameter lc17-caregiver.resentment 16 -> 14; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1942.01** `state_tick:bc18-ridiculous-fame/ridiculous-fame-year/the-audience-gets-bored` tags: bc18-ridiculous-fame, fame, reputation, mistaken_identity, promotion, media, absurd, public
  观众开始厌倦同一个故事。园艺会请了更年轻的发明家，女子学校把Josef的名字从海报上撤下；Josef在咖啡馆听见有人说“那个人还在讲火柴”。同一个月，一张报纸把旧照片配上新标题，问Josef是否已经“过气”。纠正的冲动又回来，但没有人再提问。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.legitimacy 15 -> 7; state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.boredom 8 -> 20; state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.invitations 0 -> 0; reputation -0.01999999999999999; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1942.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.lending-library/p9-social-daily-culture.lending-library.volksbibliothek` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:knowledge
  Josef听Hermann Bauer报出押金数目，把伸进内袋的手又抽了回来。问清免费阅览处的位置后，Josef穿过两条街，在 Volksbibliothek 的阅览室门口登记了姓名，坐在长桌边读了一下午；一个赫勒没花，只是不能把书带回家，天黑前就得还回去。
  _effects: mood 0.03; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7_

- **1942.04** `ad20-public-mistake::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-wrong-person-apologizes` tags: public, mistake, shame, everyday, reputation, comic, vienna, urban
  一个路过的男人以为是自己撞了Josef，连声道歉，还帮Josef把纸包重新系好。Josef说不是他；男人说“那就当我替别人道歉”。他走后，Josef站在街口，忽然觉得这件事比刚才轻了一些。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018; reputation -0.010000000000000009; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002328:child; flag +wrong_apology_

- **1942.12** `vienna-market-household.friday-fish/friday-fish.share` tags: home, market, food, social
  鱼还滴着水，Josef在走廊里分成两份。一份拿进自家，一份搁在Anton Bauer的门口。傍晚Anton Bauer来还盘子，盘子里是一小袋面粉。
  _effects: cash -6 (买鱼); food_

- **1942.12** `vienna-life-overcrowded-flat/apply-for-a-municipal-flat` tags: housing, home, health, family
  Josef去市政办事处登记申请更大的市营住宅。办事员收下表格和两克朗手续费，说前面还有几百户，至少要等两年；Josef把回执折好，回家路上看见新盖的市营楼房，窗户亮着，门却还进不去。
  _effects: cash -2 (municipal housing application); role_

- **1943.01** `ad20-school-meeting::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/teacher-listens` tags: family, school, child, meeting, everyday, public, vienna, urban
  Elisabeth Kral把Luise Bauer的作业本推到Josef面前，说这孩子最近总在算术格子里画窗户。Josef没有急着解释，只问窗户外头有什么；Elisabeth Kral愣了一下，说“大概是操场”。两个人把本子翻到最后，发现每一扇窗里都站着一个很小的人。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-78; state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 28 -> 32; flag +teacher_listened; mood 0.01999999999999999_

- **1943.01** `state_tick:lc17-caregiver/care-year/worn-thin` tags: lc17-caregiver, care, family, illness, elder, duty, household, state
  Josef Bauer今年摔了两次，第二次把胯骨摔裂了。Josef在医院走廊的硬椅上睡了四夜，回家以后发现厂里已经把位置给了别人；药费单、住院单和一张停工的证明一起压在桌玻璃下面，边角每天都被翻卷一点。
  _effects: state_parameter lc17-caregiver.burden 34 -> 42; state_parameter lc17-caregiver.resentment 14 -> 20; cash -2 (medicine and hospital fees); mood -0.03999999999999998_

- **1943.01** `state_expire:bc18-ridiculous-fame` tags: bc18-ridiculous-fame, fame, reputation, mistaken_identity, promotion, media, absurd, public
  Josef的「荒唐的名声」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove bc18-ridiculous-fame state:world-vienna-1912:627; flag -ridiculously_famous_

- **1943.01** `state_tick:pc20-second-chance/kept-word/kept-word` tags: pc20-second-chance, second_chance, reconciliation, return, failure, trust, debt, hope
  Josef把答应过的事又做了一遍：按时出现，把欠下的小钱还掉，没有在旧话上添新的解释。对方没有鼓掌，只把杯子往Josef这边推了推；这一次的沉默比上次轻。
  _effects: state_parameter pc20-second-chance.credibility 30 -> 36; state_parameter pc20-second-chance.suspicion 50 -> 45; state_parameter pc20-second-chance.hope 25 -> 29; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1943.01** `r13-fam.care-sick-child::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::child-care-war/milk-watered` tags: task, care, health, child, fever, night, vienna, urban
  配给站给的奶已经掺了水，Josef把它煮开，撇掉上面那层薄薄的沫，还是喂给了Luise Bauer。孩子喝一半就推开碗，说苦；Josef尝了一口，才发现锅底糊了。那一夜，Josef把窗子开了又关，怕煤烟，也怕穿堂风。
  _effects: cash -3 (第三遍换湿布); flag +watered_milk_fed; condition_

- **1943.08** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.sick-parent-night/hospital-deposit` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Josef跑了一趟医院，把Josef Bauer送进病房：押金三克朗，搪瓷杯和旧毛巾一克朗二十赫勒，头三天的药费三克朗八十赫勒，一共八克朗。护士收走便盆，换上带刻度的玻璃壶。Josef回到空了的床边，把塌下去的椅子扶正，才发现自己一整天只喝了一杯咖啡。
  _effects: cash -8 (住院押金和住院用品); health 0.05999999999999999 (父亲住进医院得到治疗); mood -0.06; flag +parent_hospitalized_

- **1943.12** `curse15-betrayal.ledger-share::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/sold-the-share` tags: betrayal, money, family, trust, curse, secret, vienna, urban
  Anton Bauer把Josef寄放在杂货铺后间的木箱搬走了，里面是两个人合买的缝纫机零件和一小袋银币。Josef追到码头时，Anton Bauer已经上了船，只留下一张写着“算我借的”的纸条。一个月后，Josef在旧货摊上看见那台缝纫机，摊主说是从当铺收来的。
  _effects: cash -4 (被卷走的合买本钱); relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000957:child; flag +betrayed_over_money; knowledge “我的合伙人带着合买的零件和银币上了船。”; condition; mood -0.08000000000000002_

- **1943.12** `r3-mig.transit-camp::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/registered-by-nationality` tags: migration, refugees, camp, family, papers, vienna, urban, crisis
  Luise Hofer 3按国籍把人分成三队，Josef被推到“无国籍”那一队。登记卡上只写了姓、年龄和一双鞋的尺码。第三排的草垫已经被人占了，Franz Bauer挪出半个位置，说“夜里冷，别脱鞋”。
  _effects: condition; flag +registered_in_transit_camp; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002328:child; knowledge “the camp sorts people by nationality, not by family”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-95_

- **1944.01** `ml18-family.fortune-split::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-sibling-takes-it` tags: family, money, windfall, inheritance, conflict, dark, vienna, urban
  晚饭后的第三天，Josef去邮局取钱，发现存折上少了三十克朗。Emil在母亲的旧照片前承认拿了钱，说这些年照顾母亲的情分总该折成数目。Elisabeth Kral去叫了警察；Josef站在两个最熟悉的人中间，听见楼下有人把留声机开得很响。
  _effects: cash -30 (the sibling took thirty crowns from the savings book); state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.principal 45 -> 15; state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 5 -> 8; relation rival with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; condition; flag +family_fortune_stolen; knowledge “存折上少了三十克朗，取款单上的签名是模仿的。”; mood -0.09999999999999998_

- **1944.01** `vienna-historical-social-movement.relief-network/allocate-by-need` tags: history, historical_process, social_movement, organization, politics
  煤块在秤盘上碰出闷响，Josef用粉笔在麻袋上写门牌号，Franz Bauer挨户叫名。轮到放空碗那家时，Josef把面包掰成两半，一半放进碗里，一半留在自己手中，谁也没说话。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002328:child; mood 0.03999999999999998_

- **1944.01** `state_tick:lc17-caregiver/care-year/carried` tags: lc17-caregiver, care, family, illness, elder, duty, household, state
  Josef又撑过一年。药盒按星期分好，账本上多了一栏“看护”，工钱少了一些，但还没有断；Josef Bauer今年记得住Josef的名字，偶尔也记得住昨天吃了什么。
  _effects: state_parameter lc17-caregiver.burden 42 -> 38; state_parameter lc17-caregiver.resentment 20 -> 18; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1944.01** `state_tick:pc20-second-chance/kept-word/kept-word` tags: pc20-second-chance, second_chance, reconciliation, return, failure, trust, debt, hope
  Josef把答应过的事又做了一遍：按时出现，把欠下的小钱还掉，没有在旧话上添新的解释。对方没有鼓掌，只把杯子往Josef这边推了推；这一次的沉默比上次轻。
  _effects: state_parameter pc20-second-chance.credibility 36 -> 42; state_parameter pc20-second-chance.suspicion 45 -> 40; state_parameter pc20-second-chance.hope 29 -> 33; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1944.01** `state_tick:wish-craft/craft-pressure/put-away` tags: wish-craft, wish, wish:craft, craft, work, mastery, state, state_tick
  Josef暂时把那件事收进抽屉；日子还有别的东西要应付。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1944.01** `r13-civ.recover-at-home::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/kin-nursed` tags: health, recovery, care, household, food, vienna, urban, crisis
  Elisabeth Kral把粥熬得很稀，一勺一勺吹凉，托着Josef的后颈喂下去。窗帘拉开半尺，太阳从床脚移到墙上，Josef第一次觉得退烧后的骨头是轻的。Elisabeth Kral把药钱记在日历背面，说下个月少买两次肉。
  _effects: cash -2 (退烧后的第一碗粥); food; health 0.08 (退烧后的第一碗粥); flag +recovered_with_kin_care; relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-78_

- **1944.05** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/sweat-it-out` tags: health, medical, family, money
  Josef说胡话时念着车间的排班表，Anton Bauer一句句应着，拿小勺往嘴里送温水。床头那半瓶退烧药早见了底，她没舍得倒最后一滴，留着天亮再说。
  _effects: relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000957:child_

- **1944.11** `ad20-stranger-request::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/refuse-and-walk-on` tags: stranger, envelope, request, everyday, obligation, risk, vienna, urban
  Josef把信封推回去，说今天不方便。Anton Bauer没有生气，只把信封收回口袋，说“那就算了”。Josef走出巷口，回头看了一眼，Anton Bauer还站在原地，像在等另一个人。
  _effects: mood 0.010000000000000009; flag +envelope_refused_

- **1945.01** `vienna-life-1945-liberation/postwar-illness` tags: war, liberation, 1945
  Josef在战后第二个月病倒，医院没有足够的药，医生只给了退烧粉。邻居每天送来一碗汤，放在门口就走。
  _effects: cash -4 (medicine); condition_

- **1945.01** `pc20-apprentice.master-return::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/shared-bench` tags: craft, apprentice, mentor, return, credit, second_chance, rivalry, vienna
  Luise Bauer 2带着一只旧工具箱站在门口，说不是来收徒，是来找一个能一起接活的人。Josef让他进屋，两个人把工具箱里的凿子按大小排开；Rosa Kral 2在楼下等活，看见窗户亮了很久。
  _effects: state_add pc20-second-chance state:world-vienna-1912:811; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-85; flag +shared_bench_with_master; cash 1 (the first joint commission); knowledge “老师傅带来的不是判决，是一只旧工具箱和一张两个人的工作台。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-69_

- **1945.01** `state_tick:lc17-caregiver/care-year/worn-thin` tags: lc17-caregiver, care, family, illness, elder, duty, household, state
  Josef Bauer今年摔了两次，第二次把胯骨摔裂了。Josef在医院走廊的硬椅上睡了四夜，回家以后发现厂里已经把位置给了别人；药费单、住院单和一张停工的证明一起压在桌玻璃下面，边角每天都被翻卷一点。
  _effects: state_parameter lc17-caregiver.burden 38 -> 46; state_parameter lc17-caregiver.resentment 18 -> 24; cash -2 (medicine and hospital fees); mood -0.040000000000000036_

- **1945.01** `state_expire:pc20-second-chance` tags: pc20-second-chance, second_chance, reconciliation, return, failure, trust, debt, hope
  Josef的「第二次机会」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove pc20-second-chance state:world-vienna-1912:672; flag -second_chance_open_

- **1945.01** `task-vienna-family-care.care-sick-parent/neighbor-helps` tags: family, care, courtship, siblings, inheritance, housing, task, task:family
  天没亮，Josef就敲开Mitzi Bauer 3的门，说好白天请她过来照看，喂药、添煤、给孩子们热汤，回来结钱。Mitzi Bauer 3要了六十赫勒，Josef点头应下，赶去上工。晚上回来时药按时吃了，炉子也旺，只是孩子们把粥洒了一桌。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (付给邻居白天的照看钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-72; health 0.03 (有人按时喂药，病情稍稳)_

- **1945.06** `r15-curse.betrayal-at-table::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/took-the-room` tags: betrayal, social, family, trust, secret, vienna, urban, crisis
  Anton Bauer趁Josef不在，把租约上的名字改成了自己。Josef回家时，门口多了一只不属于自己的箱子；Anton Bauer站在门里，说房东已经同意。
  _effects: mood -0.14; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000957:child; flag +home_taken_by_betrayal; state_add grief state:world-vienna-1912:813_

- **1945.06** `state_reaction:wish-craft/craft-humiliation` tags: wish-craft, wish, wish:craft, craft, work, mastery, state, state_reaction
  Josef把那句轻视记了很久。它没有变成一个决定，但也没有消失。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:012896_

- **1945.10** `ad20-public-mistake::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/everyone-saw` tags: public, mistake, shame, everyday, reputation, comic, vienna, urban
  Josef在街口绊了一下，手里的纸包散开，里面的纽扣滚了一地。有人笑，有人弯腰帮忙，一个孩子把纽扣含进嘴里又吐出来。Josef蹲在地上捡，越捡越觉得所有人都在看自己的后脑勺。
  _effects: state_add ad20-public-attention state:world-vienna-1912:812; reputation -0.04000000000000001; mood -0.04999999999999999; flag +public_mistake; knowledge “纽扣滚了一地，整条街都看见了。”; flag +public_attention_active_

- **1946.01** `state_expire:lc17-caregiver` tags: lc17-caregiver, care, family, illness, elder, duty, household, state
  Josef的「照料一个衰弱的亲人」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove lc17-caregiver state:world-vienna-1912:598_

  一篇没有署名的短文把Josef的旧事重新翻出来，添了几句谁也没说过的话。Josef走在街上，听见有人压低声音提到自己的名字；回头时，那两个人已经转进巷子。
  _effects: state_parameter ad20-public-attention.attention 65 -> 75; state_parameter ad20-public-attention.danger 35 -> 45; state_parameter ad20-public-attention.sympathy 30 -> 22; reputation -0.03; flag +public_attention_turned_

- **1946.01** `state_tick:grief/anniversary/carried` tags: grief, death, family, mind, state, state_tick
  Josef又过了一年。那个人的名字不再每天出现，但在某些气味和街道上仍会回来。
  _effects: state_parameter grief.years 0 -> 1; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1946.01** `r13-civ.donate-clothes::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/coat-donated` tags: social, charity, clothing, poverty, household, vienna, urban, postwar
  Josef把父亲留下的厚呢外套从箱底翻出来，掸掉樟脑味，叠好交给Anna Bauer 3。Anna Bauer 3摸了摸袖口，说这料子还能穿三年，就在收据上写了“男式冬衣一件”。回家时，箱子空出一角。
  _effects: reputation 0.06; flag +donated_winter_coat; knowledge “the coat was logged as a man’s winter coat”_

- **1946.08** `vienna-medical-treatment.sanatorium/sanatorium-go` tags: health, medical, family, money
  安娜把钥匙拴在门后的钉子上，又反过来压了压窗栓。车票是硬纸的，打孔时咔嗒一声。Josef在车厢里把诊断书摊在膝盖上，窗外最后几排厂房退远了，松树的味道从窗缝钻进来。
  _effects: cash -12 (疗养院车票和住宿押金); health 0.12000000000000001 (疗养院松林空气和正规休养); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-98; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-98_

- **1946.11** `vienna-parish-aid.rent-box/rent-box.accept-all` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  煤袋落在台阶上，扬起细细一层黑末。Alois Lechner用铅笔头在蓝格册上写门牌号，问房东姓什么，又叮嘱别把面包票弄丢。灶上水壶开始叫，Josef才想起屋里连只干净的杯子都没有。
  _effects: cash 6 (互助会补上本月房租差额); food; flag +parish_registered_

- **1946.12** `p9-gap-adult-unemployed.moving-job/moving-damaged-deducted` tags: adulthood, unemployment, casual_labour, poverty, daily, moving, neighbor, injury
  柜脚磕在石阶边缘那一声，Maria Hofer 3在楼上就听见了。下来摸着裂缝，说这柜子跟了十几年，你们抬东西也太毛躁。Josef把手在裤子上蹭了蹭，血印子蹭出一条。
  _effects: cash 1.2 (搬家磕坏柜脚被扣工钱); health -0.020000000000000004 (搬柜子时手背划伤); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-121_

- **1947.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Josef在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Josef把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.05 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:013493_

  Josef在《新维也纳日报》的失物栏里登了三行小字，花了四十个铜板。第二天，一个卖旧货的人说在电车终点站见过那样的铜牌。Luise 4放学后跟着Josef去找，一路上把书包带绕在手指上，绕得发白。
  _effects: cash -1 (在报纸上登失物启事); state_add sm18-missing-object state:world-vienna-1912:869; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:010546:child; mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +advertised_for_the_childs_medal; flag +something_is_missing_

- **1947.01** `state_tick:grief/anniversary/overwhelmed` tags: grief, death, family, mind, state, state_tick
  Josef在整理旧衣服时突然停下来。那天剩下的活没有做完。
  _effects: mood -0.05999999999999994; state_parameter grief.years 1 -> 2_

- **1947.01** `p9-social-relationship-initiative.cut-the-guarantee/settle-at-the-tavern` tags: courtship, friendship, family, social, initiative, housing, task, task:social
  没有签字，陪对方去酒馆把账当面谈清。掌柜的把欠条一张张摆在桌上，一共二十八克朗。有人替着说了几句好话，讲明利息怎么算、按月还多少，请掌柜的宽限到月底。谈下来先还五克朗，余下的立了新据，担保人一栏空着，掌柜的临走时瞪了一眼。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (酒馆里替对方先垫的第一笔款子和两杯酒); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-134; reputation -0.01999999999999999; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-134_

- **1947.03** `df18-mark.first-appearance::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/burn-bird` tags: body, mark, omen, illness, rumor, supernatural, eerie, vienna
  印刷机的滚筒咬住Josef的袖口时，谁都以为只擦破了一层皮。伤口结痂后，却留下一只张开翅膀的鸟形烫痕。Elisabeth Kral给了Josef两枚银币，说别去报工伤，厂里正查安全。Josef把钱收下，回到排字房，觉得那只鸟在袖子里发烫。
  _effects: cash 2 (工头给的封口费); health -0.04 (滚筒造成的烫伤); state_add df18-uncanny-mark state:world-vienna-1912:871; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-78; flag +df18_mark_appeared_after_injury; mood -0.030000000000000027; knowledge “我身上有一处说不清的痕迹，别人对它各有各的说法。”_

- **1947.03** `state_reaction:sm18-missing-object/a-rumor-hardens` tags: sm18-missing-object, mystery, object, loss, memory, search, suspicion, everyday
  Josef在院子里听见两个人在说“那家的东西”，话头在看见Josef时停住。晚上，Josef把首饰盒从五斗柜挪到床边，又觉得这样更像在防谁。
  _effects: state_parameter sm18-missing-object.suspicion 5 -> 15; reputation -0.01999999999999999; mood -0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:014140_

- **1947.04** `ld18-bathhouse.vow::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/a-vow-in-the-steam` tags: love, queer, men, intimacy, secret, vow, secret_intimacy, vienna
  Josef和Hermann Bauer在澡堂最里面的隔间交换了戒指，蒸汽把两个人的脸都熏红了。没有证婚人，只有一只漏水的铜龙头在滴答响；Hermann Bauer说这声音像教堂的钟，Josef说那就当它是。
  _effects: state_add ld18-impossible-attachment state:world-vienna-1912:870; cash -1 (two rings and the bathhouse fee); mood 0.050000000000000044; flag +vow_in_the_steam; flag +impossible_attachment_active_

- **1947.09** `r3-crime.crime-scene::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/second-trail-to-the-cellar` tags: crime, police, evidence, street, mistake, vienna, urban, postwar
  Josef在院墙下面发现另一串鞋印，比死者的鞋小两号，一直通到Elisabeth 2家的煤窖。Katharina Berg让人把窖门打开，里面只有一排腌菜缸。Elisabeth 2站在门口说“你们随便看”，手却一直攥着围裙角。
  _effects: condition; flag +found_a_second_trail; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000925:child; knowledge “a second set of footprints leads to the neighbor’s coal cell”_

- **1948.01** `vienna-postwar-housing-1950s/private-sublet` tags: adulthood, postwar, housing, family, household
  Josef从报纸小广告上找到一间转租屋，付了三十先令“钥匙钱”。墙皮一碰就掉，冬天窗框漏风；Rosa Lechner 2说这一带都这样，先住下再说。
  _effects: residence; cash -30 (key money and rent in advance); condition_

- **1948.01** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.help-wash` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Josef喝完汤没走，留下来帮修女把大锅刷净，又把木碗按大小摞好。厨房后头有半块面包和一碗剩汤，修女说是帮忙人的午饭。Mitzi Bauer在门口等着一起回去，说下回还来，洗锅的活总有人要干。
  _effects: food; skill; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000558:child; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1948.01** `state_tick:pc20-second-chance/kept-word/small-relapse` tags: pc20-second-chance, second_chance, reconciliation, return, failure, trust, debt, hope
  Josef晚了半个钟头，进门时先解释路上的电车，又解释口袋里的收据。对方没有追问，只把已经凉掉的汤端回厨房；Josef听见锅盖碰在灶台上，知道这次不是天气的问题。
  _effects: state_parameter pc20-second-chance.credibility 72 -> 65; state_parameter pc20-second-chance.suspicion 25 -> 33; state_parameter pc20-second-chance.debt 30 -> 33; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1948.01** `state_tick:grief/anniversary/carried` tags: grief, death, family, mind, state, state_tick
  Josef又过了一年。那个人的名字不再每天出现，但在某些气味和街道上仍会回来。
  _effects: state_parameter grief.years 2 -> 3; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1948.01** `state_tick:sm18-missing-object/another-drawer/the-same-question` tags: sm18-missing-object, mystery, object, loss, memory, search, suspicion, everyday
  Josef把同一只抽屉拉开又推上，里面的东西已经按原来的位置摆了三遍。楼下有人搬煤，铁锹刮过石阶；Josef忽然想不起那件东西最后一次被谁拿在手里，只觉得每个人的脸都有一点可疑。
  _effects: state_parameter sm18-missing-object.suspicion 15 -> 23; state_parameter sm18-missing-object.leads 25 -> 23; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1948.01** `state_tick:ld18-impossible-attachment/invented-anniversary/a-form-with-an-empty-line` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  户籍窗口换了一张新表格，配偶一栏不能空着。Josef最后写了“同住人”，办事员抬头看了一眼，说这不作数。回家后，Josef把表格折成很小的一块，塞进针线盒；伴侣问今天顺不顺利，Josef说和平时一样。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 25 -> 31; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 40 -> 37; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1948.01** `state_tick:df18-uncanny-mark/mark-year/story-grows` tags: df18-uncanny-mark, body, mark, omen, superstition, reputation, mystery, faith
  入冬后，Josef在集市上听见一个陌生女人向别人描述“那个有手印的人”。故事已经比原来多了一个雨夜和一声尖叫。Josef低头挑菜，发现自己的手正按着袖口。
  _effects: state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.attention 35 -> 45; state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.pain 20 -> 26; state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.claimed unknown -> curse; reputation -0.020000000000000004; mood -0.030000000000000027_

  Josef暂时把那件事收进抽屉；日子还有别的东西要应付。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1948.01** `p9-work-jobsearch.railway-depot/railway-depot.day-labor` tags: work, job_search, negotiation, wages, employment, task, railway, depot
  货场的煤烟落在肩上。Luise Huber帮他扛第一袋时教他把腰沉下去。站务员在名册边上记了日期，说干得好才有正式名分，Josef点头，把袋子往肩上一顶。
  _effects: employment 货场临时装卸工 @ morgue:person-berta-weiss-evt:world-vienna-1912:003856; condition; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-95; mood 0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44_

- **1948.08** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/dentist-pull` tags: health, medical, family, money
  钳子夹住牙根时，Josef听见自己太阳穴里咯的一声。医生往牙槽里塞了团浸药棉，叫咬住别松。下楼时Maria Hofer扶着胳膊，说嘴里的血味比啤酒苦多了。
  _effects: cash -3 (牙医拔牙费用); health 0.05999999999999999 (拔除病牙解除疼痛); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1948.08** `vienna-housing-move.sublet-room/sublet-daylight-only` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Josef和Ferdinand Lechner 4谈妥只出租白天，夜里不留宿，收两克朗。床仍归Josef家，钥匙只在白天给。Ferdinand Lechner 4说不碍事，他住的工棚不许带人，白天有个落脚的地方就够。Anna Berg听了点头，说这样少些闲话；到了傍晚，Josef把床铺重新收好，屋里照旧几口人。
  _effects: cash 2 (白天出租床位的钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-101; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-101; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-77_

- **1949.01** `state_reaction:pc20-second-chance/someone-vouches` tags: pc20-second-chance, second_chance, reconciliation, return, failure, trust, debt, hope
  一个不算亲近的人替Josef说了句话：不是保证Josef已经变好，只说那天Josef确实在场、确实没有躲。话传回来时，Josef正在补一只旧口袋，针脚第一次没有歪。
  _effects: state_parameter pc20-second-chance.credibility 65 -> 72; state_parameter pc20-second-chance.hope 73 -> 78; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Josef把顶针给了Klara。Klara的孩子发烧，戴上顶针的当晚退了一点。Klara说这是母亲留下的福气，Josef笑着点头，回家后却把自己那只旧针盒翻了三遍。
  _effects: relation kin with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912; knowledge “姐姐的孩子戴上母亲的顶针后退烧了。”; flag +df18_gave_thimble_away; mood -0.010000000000000009; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-140_

- **1949.01** `r13-edu.literacy-class::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/signs-own-name` tags: education, literacy, adult_education, class, belonging, vienna, urban, postwar
  Josef在石板上写自己的名字，写到第三遍才把最后一笔收住。Elisabeth Kral把Josef的手按在粉笔上，说慢慢来。放学时，Josef把石板抱在怀里，经过肉铺时第一次看懂招牌上写的是“今日有肝”。那天晚上，Josef在账本封皮上又写了一遍，写到最后一笔时手没有抖。
  _effects: mood 0.07999999999999996; flag +learned_to_sign_name; skill_

- **1949.03** `pc20-family.parent-mistake::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/cover` tags: parent, child, mistake, second_chance, family, school, repetition, intervention
  Josef替Luise Bauer向Alois Winter请了病假，又把那件可能惹麻烦的东西藏进自己的工具箱。Alois Winter看了Josef一眼，说病假可以补，别的东西补不了；Luise Bauer在里屋听见，门没有关严。
  _effects: state_add pc20-second-chance state:world-vienna-1912:945; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-115; flag +covered_child_mistake; knowledge “病假可以补，别的东西补不了。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-115; flag +second_chance_open_

- **1949.04** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_stolen_by_stranger` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Josef记得出门时钥匙还在，回来却没了。Mitzi Huber 3说下午看见一个穿灰外套的男人在楼道里转悠，手里晃着一把铜钥匙。两人下楼查看，房门虚掩，抽屉被拉开，攒着买煤的六克朗不见了。Mitzi Huber 3陪着Josef去街角警察岗亭报了案，警察记下灰外套，说会留意。
  _effects: cash -6 (被盗的买煤钱); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-140; mood -0.09999999999999998_

- **1949.06** `vienna-church-community.tenement-prayer/rent-prayer-help` tags: faith, social, family, home
  楼梯间只点一根蜡烛。Elisabeth Bauer凑过来低声说，楼下老寡妇的喘病又重了，教区明早才能派修女来。Josef把汤碗接过去，碗底还温着。
  _effects: condition; food_

- **1950.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/wound-infection` tags: health, work, family
  Josef的伤口在几天后肿起来，手腕发热。医生说要切开引流，费用比上次更高；Josef看着账单，先问能不能分两次付。
  _effects: cash -14 (drainage and medicine); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:015340_

- **1950.01** `kernel-health.task-pharmacy-hunt::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/pharmacy-last-bottle` tags: health, task, pharmacy, shortage, black_market, vienna, urban, postwar
  Josef跑了四家药房，最后在火车站附近的小店找到最后一瓶奎宁。掌柜先问给谁用，又看了看Josef的鞋，才把瓶子从柜台底下拿出来；价格是官价的三倍。他把瓶子塞进内袋，路上每隔一会儿摸一下。
  _effects: cash -25 (last bottle of quinine at three times the official price); health 0.04 (quinine obtained and taken); condition; role; knowledge “奎宁这类药会藏在柜台底下，先问给谁用，再看买药人的鞋；价格是官价三倍。”_

- **1950.02** `r3-crime.crime-scene::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/kept-the-crowd-back` tags: crime, police, evidence, street, mistake, vienna, urban, postwar
  Josef把围观的人挡在粉笔线外，又用一只木箱扣住那半枚鞋印。Mitzi Bauer 3来的时候，先看了看地上的血，再看了看Josef，说“你倒懂得不少”。Mitzi Bauer在人群里喊“我家孩子还没回来”，Katharina Berg让她先登记。
  _effects: reputation 0.06; flag +preserved_the_crime_scene; role; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-72; knowledge “the footprint was covered before the crowd arrived”_

- **1950.10** `ld18-raid.private-club::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/names-in-a-notebook` tags: queer, raid, police, public, scandal, nightlife, secret_intimacy, vienna

  _effects: state_add ld18-public-desire state:world-vienna-1912:988; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 37 -> 57; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 34 -> 19; reputation -0.1; cash -2 (a fine and a broken shoe); flag +name_in_police_notebook; flag +public_desire_active_

  办公室有人问Josef，为什么总有一个女人在楼下等她。Josef说那是表妹；对方笑了一下，说现在的表妹真体贴。下午，Josef把桌上的相框扣倒，又在没人时翻回来，用袖子擦了擦玻璃上的灰。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 57 -> 65; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 19 -> 15; reputation -0.020000000000000004_

  Josef和Otto Winter一起上楼，门没锁，屋里一股酸味。病人裹着毯子坐在床沿咳嗽，炉子只剩灰。Josef去楼下讨了几块煤核，蹲着把火生起来，又用带来的面粉搅了一小锅稀粥。药粉冲开，碗底沉着白渣，Josef先垫了药钱，说以后再说。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (替病人垫上药粉钱); relation acquaintance with person-otto-winter_

  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 72 -> 78; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 10 -> 7; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1951.01** `state_tick:df18-uncanny-mark/mark-year/kept-small` tags: df18-uncanny-mark, body, mark, omen, superstition, reputation, mystery, faith
  又过了一年，Josef学会了在洗澡时不去盯那块印子。它还在，边缘有时发红，有时像普通的旧疤；街坊有了新的闲话，Josef的袖子也换了更长的款式。
  _effects: state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.attention 57 -> 51; state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.pain 28 -> 24; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1951.01** `state_tick:pc20-second-chance/kept-word/small-relapse` tags: pc20-second-chance, second_chance, reconciliation, return, failure, trust, debt, hope
  Josef晚了半个钟头，进门时先解释路上的电车，又解释口袋里的收据。对方没有追问，只把已经凉掉的汤端回厨房；Josef听见锅盖碰在灶台上，知道这次不是天气的问题。
  _effects: state_parameter pc20-second-chance.credibility 33 -> 26; state_parameter pc20-second-chance.suspicion 66 -> 74; state_parameter pc20-second-chance.debt 43 -> 46; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1951.01** `state_tick:ld18-public-desire/the-story-changes-hands/strangers-know-best` tags: ld18-public-desire, desire, public, scandal, reputation, body, rumor, notoriety
  一个陌生女人在电车上叫住Josef，说自己认识她的“那位”，还劝她趁年轻找个正经人家。Josef没有回答，提前一站下了车；走在回家的路上，她发现自己的步子越来越快，像在躲一个并不存在的人。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.rumorHeat 50 -> 58; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.shame 40 -> 44; reputation -0.03; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1951.01** `r13-fam.collect-funeral-money::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/collection-short` tags: task, social, funeral, neighbor, charity, grief, vienna, urban
  走到第四家，Josef的帽子还是轻的。有人说明天再给，有人说自家也刚办过丧事。Josef把帽子里的钱倒在Grete Berg的桌上，数了两遍，还差一口棺材的钱；Grete Berg说算了，用旧门板钉一个也行。
  _effects: cash -1 (端着一顶帽子走楼道); flag +funeral_collection_short; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-6; knowledge “a plain coffin costs more than the building can raise”; condition_

- **1951.05** `r3-mig.xenophobic-landlord::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/landlord-called-police` tags: migration, housing, hostility, landlord, poverty, vienna, urban, postwar
  Josef拒绝加租，Otto Bauer第二天带着警察来，说屋里有六个人登记，实际住了九个。Hermann Bauer站在楼梯口，说愿意替Josef作证只住了四个。警察数了数床垫，把数字写在本子上，房东在旁边补充“他们晚上还烧煤油炉”。
  _effects: condition; flag +landlord_reported_overcrowding; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7; knowledge “the landlord counts mattresses for the police”_

- **1951.06** `ad20-loan::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/loan-refused` tags: loan, debt, credit, everyday, obligation, money, vienna, urban
  Helene Berg把Josef的收入证明翻来覆去看了两遍，说数目不够。Josef问能不能少借一点；Helene Berg说“少借也要还得起”。走出门时，Josef把证明信重新叠好，发现上面有一道自己刚才捏出的折痕。
  _effects: flag +loan_refused; mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001844:child; cash -1 (paperwork and a wasted morning)_

- **1951.11** `ml18-lottery.same-numbers::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-partner-bought-it-secretly` tags: lottery, luck, money, family, regret, windfall, vienna, urban
  Elisabeth Kral在开奖前偷偷去了烟草铺，用买菜剩下的钱买了那组号码，把票藏在糖罐后面。号码中了，Elisabeth Kral把票放在餐桌上，说本来想等Josef生日再讲。Josef先是不信，随后笑得把椅子往后推了一截；两个人坐在厨房里数奖金，数到一半又停下来烧水。
  _effects: cash 70 (the lottery prize bought with the grocery money); state_add ml18-sudden-fortune state:world-vienna-1912:1020; relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-78; reputation 0.03; flag +partner_bought_the_ticket; mood 0.09999999999999998; flag +sudden_fortune; reputation 0.02; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1952.01** `ld18-admirer.gift::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-gift-is-returned` tags: admirer, gift, public_desire, jealousy, danger, secret_intimacy, vienna, urban
  Josef在剧院后门把钥匙还给Mitzi Huber 3，说自己不接没有名字的礼物。Mitzi Huber 3把钥匙收回掌心，说名字不重要，重要的是她肯不肯来。Josef说肯不肯都不去；第二天，后台的花篮少了一只。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-140; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.protection 0 -> 10; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.spectacle 55 -> 50; reputation 0.02; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1952.01** `state_reaction:df18-uncanny-mark/mark-public` tags: df18-uncanny-mark, body, mark, omen, superstition, reputation, mystery, faith
  Josef在人群里挽了一下袖子，第二天就有人来问那块印子是不是“会动的”。Josef说不会，问的人却已经把自己的解释说完了。
  _effects: state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.attention 51 -> 59; reputation -0.009999999999999998_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:017318_

- **1952.01** `state_reaction:ld18-public-desire/a-moral-society-letter` tags: ld18-public-desire, desire, public, scandal, reputation, body, rumor, notoriety
  道德协会寄来一封措辞客气的信，请Josef去“谈谈自己的生活方式”。她把信读完，折好，垫在桌脚下；桌子还是晃。第二天，她照常去上班，只是把头发剪短了一截，短到所有人都能看出她不在乎。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.protection 10 -> 5; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.defiance 20 -> 28_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:017318_

- **1952.01** `state_expire:ld18-impossible-attachment` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  Josef的「无法登记的伴侣」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove ld18-impossible-attachment state:world-vienna-1912:870; flag -impossible_attachment_active_

- **1952.01** `state_tick:df18-uncanny-mark/mark-year/story-grows` tags: df18-uncanny-mark, body, mark, omen, superstition, reputation, mystery, faith
  入冬后，Josef在集市上听见一个陌生女人向别人描述“那个有手印的人”。故事已经比原来多了一个雨夜和一声尖叫。Josef低头挑菜，发现自己的手正按着袖口。
  _effects: state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.attention 59 -> 69; state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.pain 24 -> 30; state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.claimed curse -> curse; reputation -0.02_

  Josef把答应过的事又做了一遍：按时出现，把欠下的小钱还掉，没有在旧话上添新的解释。对方没有鼓掌，只把杯子往Josef这边推了推；这一次的沉默比上次轻。
  _effects: state_parameter pc20-second-chance.credibility 26 -> 32; state_parameter pc20-second-chance.suspicion 74 -> 69; state_parameter pc20-second-chance.hope 40 -> 44; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  一个陌生女人在电车上叫住Josef，说自己认识她的“那位”，还劝她趁年轻找个正经人家。Josef没有回答，提前一站下了车；走在回家的路上，她发现自己的步子越来越快，像在躲一个并不存在的人。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.rumorHeat 58 -> 66; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.shame 44 -> 48; reputation -0.03; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1952.01** `state_tick:wish-craft/craft-pressure/press-on` tags: wish-craft, wish, wish:craft, craft, work, mastery, state, state_tick
  Josef又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1952.01** `kernel-health.task-pharmacy-hunt::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/pharmacy-last-bottle` tags: health, task, pharmacy, shortage, black_market, vienna, urban, postwar
  Josef跑了四家药房，最后在火车站附近的小店找到最后一瓶奎宁。掌柜先问给谁用，又看了看Josef的鞋，才把瓶子从柜台底下拿出来；价格是官价的三倍。他把瓶子塞进内袋，路上每隔一会儿摸一下。
  _effects: cash -25 (last bottle of quinine at three times the official price); health 0.04 (quinine obtained and taken); knowledge “奎宁这类药会藏在柜台底下，先问给谁用，再看买药人的鞋；价格是官价三倍。”_

- **1952.05** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_lost_landlady` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Josef把钥匙丢在了回家路上的有轨电车轨道旁，天黑也没找着。房东太太说换锁芯要一克朗五十赫勒，从下月房租里扣。Rosa借给Josef一把旧挂锁，但锁鼻和门扣对不上，只能用铁丝拧着凑合。那夜Josef睡不踏实，总觉得有人在拧门。
  _effects: cash -1.5 (换锁芯费用); relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912_

- **1952.08** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.defer` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Josef把抽屉拉开又推上，铜币在木板上滚了半圈。Luise Bauer 2合上本子说，拖欠的人不少，可规矩就是规矩。她走下两级台阶又回头提醒，发工钱那天她会在教堂侧门等着。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-85_

- **1952.12** `ad20-landlord-visit::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-window-stays-broken` tags: housing, rent, landlord, everyday, repair, public, vienna, urban
  Gustav Hofer说修窗不在租约里，若嫌冷可以搬走。Josef问押金怎么算，Gustav Hofer把钥匙在手里转了一圈，说“到时候再说”。那天夜里风从缝里进来，Josef把椅子搬到炉边睡，第二天在门上贴了一张自己写的修窗单。
  _effects: cash -1 (coal for a room with a broken window); relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-142; flag +eviction_threatened; mood -0.05999999999999994; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-142_

- **1953.01** `state_reaction:wish-craft/craft-humiliation` tags: wish-craft, wish, wish:craft, craft, work, mastery, state, state_reaction
  Josef把那句轻视记了很久。它没有变成一个决定，但也没有消失。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:017815_

- **1953.01** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.misdelivered-letter/misdelivered-letter.upstairs` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, letter
  Josef拿着信上了四层楼，敲了两户都不是，门牌是七号还是十七号已看不清。第三户的老太太说那人去年就搬去法沃里滕了。她把信交回门房，门房在登记簿上写下一个赫勒的墨水钱。
  _effects: mood 0.030000000000000027; reputation 0.02_

- **1953.01** `vienna-market-household.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.keep` tags: home, market, food, social
  扁豆汤的香味从门缝飘出去，Luise Bauer敲了门。Josef掀开锅盖又盖上，说只够自家吃。Luise Bauer什么也没说就走了。夜里Josef听见隔壁有人用勺子刮碗底。
  _effects: food; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1953.01** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/corner-doctor` tags: health, medical, family, money
  施特劳斯医生用酒精棉擦手，把药粉倒进小纸袋，折口时指甲掐出印子。他说夜里别再喝醋水，那东西烧胃。Katharina Kral把炉火拨旺，在灶上坐了一壶水，答应第二天替Josef去铺子里告假。
  _effects: cash -4 (医生出诊费和药费); health 0.05 (药粉和糖浆缓解症状); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23_

  Josef的「第二次机会」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove pc20-second-chance state:world-vienna-1912:945; flag -second_chance_open_

  Josef的「被全城观看的欲望」到期；相关安排结束。

- **1953.01** `r13-fam.look-for-lost-relative::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/no-record` tags: task, family, missing, official, war, search, vienna, urban
  Katharina Berg把三本名册都翻完，说没有这个人，也许登记时用了别的写法。Luise Bauer 2不肯走，又把名字念了一遍，念到第三遍时声音低下去。Josef扶她到走廊的长椅上坐下，替她把围巾重新系好；窗口后面已经叫了下一个号。
  _effects: cash -1 (念错的名字); flag +missing_relative_no_record; relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-85; knowledge “there is no record under the name the aunt remembers”_

- **1953.10** `vienna-legacy-worklife.hours-ledger/hours-ledger.refuse` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  学徒在旁边小声说，拒绝按手印的人名字会被单独抄在一张纸上。Josef没接话，把八克朗换成一克朗的硬币，数给家里时硬币在桌上滚了两圈才停。屋里的煤油灯芯剪得太短，光只够照亮手边的桌面。
  _effects: cash 8 (追回的拖欠计件工资); reputation -0.03_

- **1954.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.pictures-or-concert/p9-social-daily-culture.pictures-or-concert.skip-both` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:culture
  Josef在影戏棚和音乐厅之间走了两趟，最后哪处都没进。Josef拐进常去的酒馆，花三十赫勒要了半升啤酒，听角落里有人拉琴，拉得不准但很响。Elisabeth Bauer找到Josef时，桌上的杯子已经空了一半，省下的票钱还在口袋里叮当响；这一晚没看成戏，倒把半升啤酒喝出了几分自在。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (酒馆半升啤酒); mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1954.05** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_burns_curtain` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Josef在公用灶台上热猪油，油星子溅到旁边晾着的床单上，火苗顺着床单爬上木隔板。Alois Hofer 2拎起水桶泼过去，火灭了，但床单烧掉半幅，隔板熏黑一片。房东扣了那户人家两克朗押金，那家的女人在楼道里哭了一下午。
  _effects: cash -2 (赔给房东的隔板熏黑损失); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-154; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-154_

- **1954.10** `vienna-parish-aid.rent-box/rent-box.accept-all` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  煤袋落在台阶上，扬起细细一层黑末。Ferdinand Weiss 2用铅笔头在蓝格册上写门牌号，问房东姓什么，又叮嘱别把面包票弄丢。灶上水壶开始叫，Josef才想起屋里连只干净的杯子都没有。
  _effects: cash 6 (互助会补上本月房租差额); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81_

- **1954.11** `bc18-fame.wrong-bust::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/widow-corrects` tags: fame, mistaken_identity, museum, public, invention, absurd, status, humiliation
  揭幕到一半，穿黑纱的Gustav Bauer从第二排站起来，说那根火柴是她丈夫一八九三年在布尔诺登记的，专利号还刻在家里一只锡盒上。Josef Bauer 3请她进办公室核对文件，Josef被留在展厅里，面对一队已经买票的小学生。第二天，铭牌换成了“无名热心市民”，Josef的名字被刮掉，只剩一道白痕。
  _effects: reputation -0.039999999999999994; state_add bc18-ridiculous-fame state:world-vienna-1912:1134; cash -1 (退掉车马费后的车钱); relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000904:child; condition; flag +plaque_scraped_clean; mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-145; flag +ridiculously_famous; reputation 0.02; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1955.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/neighbor_yanks_clothes` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Alois Hofer 2嫌Josef的床单滴水滴到Alois Hofer 2家衬衫上，一把扯下床单扔在院子的石板上。Josef下楼捡起来时，床单角沾了泥，重洗又要费肥皂。两人在院子当中对喊，楼上有人推开窗看热闹。Josef把床单重新搭上绳子时，故意把水拧在Alois Hofer 2那侧。
  _effects: relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-154; cash -0.15 (重洗床单的肥皂钱)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:018661_

- **1955.01** `bc18-office.new-director-speech::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/speech-misread` tags: office, promotion, speech, bureaucracy, public, absurd, comic, vienna
  第一次训话，Josef把预算备忘录拿倒了，从头念到尾。底下的人先是发愣，随后有人小声说，主任是在用倒着的账提醒大家：钱不够。散会后，各科主动减了文具和加班费，Josef没有解释，Katharina Berg在门口递上一杯咖啡，说这招比拍桌子管用。
  _effects: cash 1 (各科省下来的办公费分成); state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.legitimacy 5 -> 10; state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.notoriety 22 -> 27; relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000453:child; skill; flag +budget_read_backwards; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1955.01** `state_reaction:bc18-ridiculous-fame/reprinted-wrong` tags: bc18-ridiculous-fame, fame, reputation, mistaken_identity, promotion, media, absurd, public
  报纸的周末副刊又把Josef的旧照片翻出来，配上一个更整齐的故事：发明、失败、谦逊的晚年。Josef写信去更正，编辑回了一张便条，说读者喜欢现在这个版本。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.notoriety 27 -> 33; state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.absurdity 45 -> 50; state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.legitimacy 10 -> 6; flag +story_reprinted_wrong; mood -0.010000000000000009_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:019346_

- **1955.01** `state_tick:bc18-ridiculous-fame/ridiculous-fame-year/the-audience-gets-bored` tags: bc18-ridiculous-fame, fame, reputation, mistaken_identity, promotion, media, absurd, public
  观众开始厌倦同一个故事。园艺会请了更年轻的发明家，女子学校把Josef的名字从海报上撤下；Josef在咖啡馆听见有人说“那个人还在讲火柴”。同一个月，一张报纸把旧照片配上新标题，问Josef是否已经“过气”。纠正的冲动又回来，但没有人再提问。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.legitimacy 6 -> 0; state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.boredom 20 -> 32; state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.invitations 0 -> 0; reputation -0.020000000000000004; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1955.01** `task-vienna-family-care.reconcile-siblings/meet-at-church-door` tags: family, care, courtship, siblings, inheritance, housing, task, task:family
  两人谁也没提柜子，饭吃得客气而生分。临走时Josef提议：和不和好另说，每年忌日就在教堂门口见一面，站十分钟也好。Klara想了一下，说行，但别指望他进屋。Josef把这话记在心上，回去路上把没送出去的半包烟丝塞回口袋。
  _effects: relation kin with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912; flag +truce_with_sibling_

  _effects: cash -2 (按月多付两克朗房租); mood -0.030000000000000027_

  _effects: health 0.05 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages)_

### 316 subject events

- **1913.12** `vienna-life-infant-feeding/fed-cows-milk` tags: infancy, food, household
  家里有人从街角奶店带回一小罐牛奶，Rosa Lechner兑水煮开，用小勺喂给Rosa。罐底有一层灰，奶店老板说这已经是最好的。
  _effects: cash -3 (cow milk); condition_

- **1914.12** `iw18-infancy.first-word::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-tenement/a-word-for-bread` tags: infancy, first_word, memory, family, wonder, eerie, vienna, urban
  Rosa指着桌上的面包说出一个短词，声音轻得像在试音。Rosa Lechner把面包掰下一角，泡进热水里喂给孩子；Rosa吃完又指了一次，这次说得更清楚。厨房里的人都笑了，连灶上的水汽也像在往上冒。
  _effects: food; mood 0.03; flag +first_word_bread_

- **1916.11** `p9-childhood-household.name-day-gift/passed-gift-to-younger` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors
  木马在Rosa手里还没捂热，就转到了弟妹怀里。Rosa从纸包底下抽出那本图画书，封面卷了角，边上有人用铅笔写过字。Alois Lechner看看木马，又看看书，把报纸叠好收进抽屉。那晚Rosa把书压在枕头下，借着窗缝的光翻了三页。
  _effects: relation kin with person-alois-lechner; mood 0.04; flag +likes_reading; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.mind-sibling/sibling-shelter-missed-river` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, sibling, care
  河水那边传来同伴的笑声，Rosa站在院门口听了听，还是回去把木盆扶正。Friedrich Lechner睡醒哭了一声，Rosa把最后半块面包掰碎泡进温水，一勺一勺喂。Rosa Lechner回来时，Rosa正蹲在盆边打瞌睡，手里还攥着喂饭的木勺。
  _effects: mood -0.02; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000305:child; relation kin with person-rosa-lechner; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10_

- **1918.05** `p9-childhood-household.fever-night/called-corner-doctor` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors
  楼梯上传来皮包磕碰的声音，Hermann Kral把医生领进门。医生看了看Rosa的舌苔，又听了听胸口，说先把热度压下去。药粉包在旧纸里，苦味隔着纸都能闻到。Alois Lechner付了两克朗，把那张饮食单压在盐罐底下。
  _effects: cash -2 (出诊费和药粉（其中2克朗记入债务）); health 0.06 (服药后热退了些); relation acquaintance with person-hermann-kral_

- **1918.06** `vienna-early-childhood.stairwell-fall/arm-bruise` tags: childhood, family, home, street, injury
  楼梯间的灯罩空着，第三级踏板比别处高。Rosa一脚踩空，滚到平台，右小臂磕在铁扶手的弯头上。Rosa Lechner听见哭声跑出来，把Rosa抱回屋，用冷水毛巾敷那块青紫，再用旧布条缠了两圈。夜里Rosa哭醒两次。
  _effects: health -0.03999999999999998 (从楼梯滚下，小臂挫伤); mood -0.05000000000000002_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-sold-out-potatoes` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  “卖完了。”三个字从窗口里传出来，Rosa后面的队伍立刻散了一半。Rosa没走，趴在窗台上看伙计扫面屑，直到人家挥手赶人。旁边摊子的土豆带着泥，Rosa用围裙兜住，一枚一枚数铜板，回家报账时连找零都说清了。
  _effects: food; mood -0.03; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5_

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.mind-sibling/sibling-carried-afternoon` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, sibling, care
  Friedrich Lechner哭得打嗝，手指一直朝井台方向指。Rosa蹲下把Friedrich Lechner驮上背，沿着墙根走，数砖缝给Friedrich Lechner听：一、二、三……数到第四圈，背上的呼吸才匀了。天黑时Rosa的胳膊麻得抬不过肩，Alois Lechner回来接过孩子，Rosa扶着门框甩了好一会儿手。
  _effects: skill; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000305:child_

  _effects: relation kin with person-rosa-lechner; mood 0.04; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-6_

- **1921.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.fetch-coal/coal-neighbor-speaks` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, coal, errand
  掌柜不肯赊，Katharina Kral从队尾挤过来，说这户人家的男人在印刷所做了十年，欠不了一筐煤。掌柜把“两赫勒”写在门牌号旁边，煤只装了小半筐。Rosa抱着筐经过Katharina Kral身边，Katharina Kral替Rosa把绳结紧了紧。
  _effects: cash -0.02 (赊账添的两赫勒利息); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23_

- **1921.11** `vienna-childhood-friends.courtyard-race/won-the-race-lost-a-button` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  Rosa跑到第二圈时鞋带松了，索性踩着鞋后跟跑。最后一圈，Rosa从床单底下钻过去，先拍到墙。Ferdinand Berg喘着气，把两颗弹珠拍进Rosa手心。Rosa低头才发现外套少了一颗扣子；窗口的Ferdinand Berg扔下一根别针，说先别着。
  _effects: mood 0.05; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4_

- **1922.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.mind-sibling/sibling-falls-at-well` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, sibling, care
  土豆滚进墙角的煤灰里，Rosa弯腰去追，再抬头时Josef Lechner已经坐在井台边，裤腿湿了一片，哭声一抽一抽。Grete Stein把孩子抱进灶间，Rosa跟在后面，手指抠着门框。Rosa Lechner回来没问经过，只把Rosa的碗收走一半，说“看个人都看不住”。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (在井台边摔了一跤); mood -0.06; relation kin with person-rosa-lechner_

- **1922.05** `vienna-childhood-street.market-errand/errand-bullied` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  市场拱廊下晒不到太阳，Rosa却被堵出一头汗。带子越挣越紧，肩膀在柱子上磕了一下。Lena从人群里挤过来，先看了看Rosa的肩，又看了看柱子上的结，拿出小刀割断。两个大孩子骂了一句，Lena把Rosa拉到菜篮后面。
  _effects: relation friend with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.05; condition_

- **1922.08** `vienna-childhood-friends.hidden-book/book-borrowed-and-returned` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  书在Rosa的书包里藏了三天，课间才敢翻两页。还书那天，Rosa把书角淋皱的事先说了，又把书页一张张抚平。Maria Moser翻到最后一页，看了看书脊，说下次别带进院子，书却还是借给了Rosa。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33_

- **1923.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-three-heller` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  “拽平，别留褶。”Grete Stein在绳子那头喊。Rosa踮着脚把床单拉直，碱水把指缝泡得发白。收工时天已经黑了，Grete Stein数了三赫勒，又在围裙上擦干手才递过来。Rosa把钱塞进袜筒，回家路上一直攥着，虎口的裂口沾了汗，火辣辣地疼。
  _effects: cash 0.03 (帮邻居晾床单挣的三赫勒); relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein_

- **1923.06** `p9-childhood-household.name-day-gift/passed-gift-to-younger` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors
  Rosa把木马推给弟妹，说“这个给你”，自己翻开那本旧图画书。纸页发黄，角上留着别人的铅笔字。Rosa Lechner问真的不要木马吗，Rosa点头，眼睛没离开书页。此后几天，Rosa一有空就翻那本书，连书脊都摸得发亮。
  _effects: relation kin with person-rosa-lechner; mood 0.04_

- **1924.01** `p9-childhood-apprenticeship.ask-for-trial/too-young-refused` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, labour, training, family, task, task:work
  “开春再来。”Otto Winter说完就转身去接纸车。Rosa站在门槛外，把这句话在心里复述了一遍，弯腰捡起地上的纸边。两个赫勒落在掌心时还带着围裙的体温。回家那条街上有三家亮着灯的作坊，Rosa一家一家看过去，把位置记住。
  _effects: cash 0.2 (替作坊捡纸边得到的两个赫勒); mood -0.04; flag +seeking_work; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17_

- **1924.02** `vienna-youth-threshold.sibling-departure/coins-into-box` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, transition
  Mitzi Lechner的箱子摊在床板上，里面是两件衬衫、一双旧靴和母亲塞的一小袋面粉。Rosa把攒了半年的零钱数到第三遍，一共一克朗二十赫勒，全塞进箱角的袜子里。Mitzi Lechner看见了，没说话，只把箱子扣上，铜扣响了一声。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (把攒下的零钱塞给离家的兄姐); relation kin with person-mitzi-lechner; mood -0.03_

- **1924.06** `vienna-adolescence.brothers-torn-jacket/sew-it-yourself` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  Rosa在厨房找到一截粗线，颜色和外套差了一截。缝到一半，线打结，Rosa用牙咬断，重新来。Rosa Lechner坐在对面，没问外套的事，只把汤碗推过来，说“先吃”。Rosa把最后一针打完，汤面已经结了一层薄皮。
  _effects: skill; food; relation kin with person-rosa-lechner_

- **1925.01** `r13-edu.music-lesson::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/broken-string` tags: education, music, lesson, craft, culture, reform, medical, schooling
  Rosa换把时用力过猛，弦轴崩了，琴弦抽在手背上。Mitzi Weiss没有责备，只让Rosa先学三个月空弦。Rosa每天对着镜子拉同一个音，拉到最后，连邻居都不再敲墙，只是经过门口时会放慢脚步。第二天Rosa照常来，手上缠着布。弦上还有血。
  _effects: cash -2 (借来的小提琴（其中2克朗记入债务）); skill_

- **1925.03** `yf18-firstlook.tram-glove::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/keep-it-in-the-satchel` tags: first_love, love, tram, glove, youth, courtship, reform, medical
  Rosa把手套塞进书包最里层，回家后放在枕头下面。第二天上学，Rosa每隔一会儿就摸一下书包带，像在确认一件不能给家里看见的东西；手套上还留着电车木座的气味。
  _effects: state_add yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:86; flag +yf18_kept_glove; mood 0.020000000000000004; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52; flag +yf18_first_awakening_active_

- **1925.03** `r13-turn.school-leaving::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::reform-leaving-age/straight-to-factory` tags: education, school, work, family, transition, reform, medical, schooling
  Mitzi Weiss把Rosa的名字从升学名单上划掉，说厂里正缺人手。家里没有人争，只把午饭里的半块面包塞给Rosa。Rosa进了纺织厂，工牌上写着“临时”，每天站十二个钟头，月底工钱比学徒多两克朗。
  _effects: cash 2 (毕业钟响过以后); condition; flag +left_school_for_factory; role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-29_

- **1925.07** `r15-work.master-takes-on::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/signed-contract` tags: work, craft, apprentice, trade, poverty, reform, medical, schooling
  Alois Hofer把学徒契约摊在桌上，指给Rosa看期限、工钱和出师考试。Rosa签了名，第二天站到刨床前，手指上多了第一道木刺。
  _effects: relation employer with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45; state_add apprenticeship-contract state:world-vienna-1912:87; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45; education apprenticeship; role_

- **1925.10** `vienna-adolescence.stolen-roll/share-the-roll` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  Rosa蹲在面包房后门的台阶上，手指抠着筐沿。Berta Weiss提着篮子过来，脚步很轻，掰下半个黑面包塞进Rosa手里，说“别让里面的人看见”。Rosa咬了两口，把剩下的包进纸里，回家放在桌上。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-berta-weiss_

- **1926.01** `yf18-friend.two-tickets::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/give-them-away` tags: first_love, friendship, betrayal, dance, tickets, reform, medical, schooling
  Rosa把两张票送给门口卖花的小女孩，自己在普拉特大街上看橱窗。橱窗里摆着一双和Hedwig一样的白手套；Rosa站到商店关门，回家时说票弄丢了，谁也没有再问。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 60 -> 52; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.secret true -> true; flag +yf18_gave_tickets_away; mood -0.020000000000000004_

  Rosa在教堂门口或学校走廊里又遇见Emil。这一次对方先点了头，Rosa准备好的整段话全忘了，只把手里的书换到另一只手上；回家后Rosa才发现，自己把书拿倒了。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 52 -> 57; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 5 -> 13; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.reciprocated false -> true; relation friend with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.03_

  _effects: state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.progress 10 -> 30; skill; mood 0.02_

- **1926.01** `r13-civ.vaccination::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::r13-civ.vaccination-reform/reform-class-vaccinated` tags: health, vaccination, school, parent, public_health, reform, medical, schooling
  Grete Stein让全班按座位排队，Anna Bauer 3在讲台边摆开酒精瓶和针盒。轮到Rosa时，Grete Stein替她把袖子卷好，说别怕，去年他自己也种过。下课后，黑板上还留着一行没擦掉的日期。
  _effects: health 0.05 (种痘室门口的袖子); condition; flag +vaccinated_in_school_campaign; relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-55_

  Rosa趁Rosa Lechner去上夜班，把储钱罐砸在窗台上。硬币滚进床缝，Rosa趴在地上摸到最后一枚，跑去南站买了一张最便宜的单程票；售票员问要不要往返，Rosa说不用，声音比自己想象中平静。
  _effects: state_add yf18-runaway-plan state:world-vienna-1912:131; cash -3 (the savings jar was broken for a ticket（其中0.2克朗记入债务）); flag +yf18_ticket_bought; mood 0.04; flag +yf18_runaway_plan_active_

  Mitzi Weiss把半年前的作业本找出来，纸边已经发黄。Rosa坐在最后一排，铅笔短得握不住，Emil Winter从桌下递来一截新的。放学后，Mitzi Weiss留下她补算术，黑板上的粉笔字擦了又写。
  _effects: flag +returned_to_school_after_dropout; skill; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-29; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-56; education secondary; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-56_

  _effects: state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.suspicion 15 -> 23; state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.resolve 70 -> 68_

  _effects: education apprenticeship; employment Schriftsetzerlehrling @ org-winter-print; cash 6 (apprentice advance)_

  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 57 -> 51; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 13 -> 10; mood -0.02_

  Rosa藏在糖罐后面的硬币少了一小叠。父亲说家里这个月煤钱不够，先拿去用了；Rosa看着空出来的那圈灰，什么也没说。那天晚上，Rosa第一次认真算了一笔账：从维也纳到格拉茨，到底要攒几个冬天。

  Rosa把工钱袋推回去，说夜班课已交过费。白天他接零活，给面包房送两趟货，工钱比正式工少了一半。晚上坐在教室里，手指还带着面粉味，握笔时总打滑。

  Rosa在学校的煤炉边把油印纸烧了，灰烬和煤渣混在一起。Ferdinand Lechner 2第二天问第三十四页读得怎么样，Rosa说译文看不出来；Ferdinand Lechner 2点点头，把课本收回去，从此没有再借给Rosa任何书。
  _effects: mood -0.01999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-40; flag +yf18_burned_pamphlet; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-40; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-22_

  Lena说入伙要交二十个铜板买蜡烛，Rosa掏遍口袋只有九个。他们让她在楼梯口等，等到天黑也没人下来。楼上传来笑声，像隔着一层厚被子。Rosa把九个铜板在楼梯上排成一排，又一个个收进口袋。
  _effects: mood -0.12; flag +excluded_from_friend_group; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50_

  工会的人在厂门口拦住Rosa，说学徒工也该有工票和保险。Rosa每月交一克朗会费，把会员证塞进上衣内袋。
  _effects: role; cash -1 (union dues（其中1克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-alois-lechner_

- **1928.01** `yf18-firstlook.essay-exam::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/the-teacher-keeps-it` tags: first_love, exam, essay, school, humiliation, reform, medical, schooling
  德语作文题是《我的家乡》。Rosa写了半页多瑙河，剩下半页写一个总在电车上看书的人。Mitzi Weiss没有打分，只在页边写“感情太多，论据太少”，把作文折好放进自己的公文包；放学后，Rosa看见Elisabeth Bauer在门口等，忽然不敢走过去。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 56 -> 62; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 0 -> 3; skill; reputation -0.01; mood 0.009999999999999995_

  Rosa在厨房里对着镜子练了七遍开场白，第二天却在离Emil三步远的地方拐进面包店。Rosa买了一袋并不需要的盐，回家路上把开场白又想了一遍；这一次，连标点都更熟练了。

  Rosa回到学校，发现同班同学已经毕业，新班级里没人认识Rosa。Rosa在操场上站了一下午，最后没有进教室，跟着父亲去了工地。晚上Rosa把课本放进箱底，没舍得扔。工地的工头看Rosa年纪小，只让Rosa搬轻的。课本压在最底下。

  _effects: state_add ld18-impossible-attachment state:world-vienna-1912:189; mood 0.05; flag +letter_in_the_schoolbook; flag +impossible_attachment_active_

- **1928.12** `vienna-youth-threshold.night-class/enroll-night-class` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, transition
  Maria Moser在黑板上写下课程表和书本费：一学期三克朗，另加六十赫勒杂费。Rosa下工后啃着冷土豆赶到教室，坐到公共长桌最靠煤气灯的一头。手指上还留着白天的油污，握笔时在本子上蹭出灰印，但座位号已经登记在册。
  _effects: cash -3.6 (夜校学费与书本杂费（其中3.6克朗记入债务）); education secondary; skill_

  Rosa说自己也等钱交房租，把口袋翻给Grete Moser看。Grete Moser没有再求，转身去敲另一扇门；第二天，楼道里有人说Rosa见死不救，Rosa听见了，只把煤筐往门里挪了挪。那天夜里，两家都早早关了灯。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52; role; reputation -0.03; flag +loan_refused; knowledge “邻居转身去敲了别人的门，楼道里开始议论这件事。”_

- **1930.10** `vienna-adult-social.burial-fund/burial-fund.paid` tags: adulthood, social, work, family
  车间里管互助会的Emil Winter在午休时把本子摊在Rosa的工具箱上，说这个月还差最后一份月钱。Rosa从马甲内袋摸出二十个克罗伊策，Emil Winter当着他的面在本子上画了钩。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (缴纳丧葬互助会的月钱); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-56; reputation 0.04000000000000001_

- **1930.12** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.evicted-neighbours/evicted-neighbours.only-hot-water` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Rosa没有开门，只从门缝递出一壶热水，说自家也挤不下。走廊里的箱子到半夜还堆着，孩子的哭声隔着门板一阵一阵。Rosa坐在桌边没有点灯，第二天在楼梯间遇见时避开了目光。
  _effects: mood -0.06; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912_

- **1931.01** `state_tick:ld18-impossible-attachment/invented-anniversary/someone-asks-for-the-papers` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  房东要续租，必须出示结婚证或亲属关系证明。Rosa说伴侣是表妹，房东却问为什么表妹的姓和籍贯都对不上。那天晚上，两个人把箱子重新打包，又把已经叠好的衣服一件件拿出来；谁也没有说下个月住哪里。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 27 -> 39; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 49 -> 41; reputation -0.03; mood -0.04000000000000001_

- **1931.01** `p9-history-cultural-contact.join-circle/circle-rejects` tags: culture, knowledge, letter, lecture, editor, training, circle, task
  有人上下打量Rosa的衣领，低声说了句什么，桌边笑起来。Rosa把杯子放下，咖啡还剩一半。Maria Hofer送他到门口，说“别在意”，没有提下星期四。
  _effects: cash -1 (咖啡钱); mood -0.08000000000000002; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2_

- **1931.02** `vienna-women-housework.piecework-bundle/piecework.overnight` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  Rosa点着煤油灯缝到后半夜，把一捆领子全做完了。针脚密是密，可天亮时她看东西重影，手指被针扎了三回。星期六交活，Emil Winter量了量尺寸，收下整捆，数出七十五赫勒推过桌面。
  _effects: cash 0.75 (交了一整捆衬衫领子的计件工钱); mood 0.020000000000000004_

- **1931.04** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/sweat-it-out` tags: health, medical, family, money
  醋水的酸味混着汗气，屋子小得转不开身。Hermann Kral把布拧到不滴水，敷上去时Rosa打了个激灵。窗外送奶车吱呀碾过石板，她才想起自己家的炉子还灭着。
  _effects: condition; relation friend with person-hermann-kral_

- **1931.05** `vienna-illness-household.child-fever/call-doctor` tags: health, family, home
  夜里Katharina Lechner哭醒，Rosa独自用湿布敷额，熬到街上有人扫雪才去请医生。医生进门时靴底带着雪泥，听完胸口说无大碍；那三克朗花得心疼，却换回一条稳下来的呼吸。
  _effects: cash -3 (支付医生诊费和药粉); mood -0.05_

- **1931.10** `vienna-legacy-worklife.sick-fund/sick-fund.keep-quiet` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life, injury
  止咳药水是褐色的，瓶口塞着软木塞，摇一摇会起沫。Rosa看着工友把表折成小块，折了三折才塞进帽檐。墙上的规章还贴在那里，纸角被风掀起，没人去按。
  _effects: mood -0.05; health -0.04 (没有正规病假，只能靠药水撑着); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-68; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-68_

- **1932.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Rosa在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Rosa把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.05 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages（其中8克朗记入债务）); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:005059_

- **1932.01** `vienna-life-work-injury/crushed-hand` tags: adulthood, labour, injury
  Rosa在换纸时左手被压板夹住。工友关掉机器，工头拿来急救箱，把伤手包起来送去医院；厂里先垫了十克朗医药费，事故报告写的是“操作不慎”。Rosa的左手从此握不紧拳，指尖总是凉的，天阴时骨缝里发疼。
  _effects: health -0.05 (crushed left hand); condition; cash 10 (employer medical advance)_

- **1932.01** `oj18-radio.repair::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/cold-joint` tags: radio, repair, craft, customer, work, odd_job, suspense, reform
  Rosa Winter的收音机只发出炒豆子一样的杂音。Rosa把后板拆开，发现一根接地线被老鼠咬断，焊点冷得像一颗坏牙。修好以后，Rosa Winter留Rosa听完了整点新闻；出门时，Rosa在巷口还听见那台机器在响。
  _effects: organization_found 屋顶无线电修理铺 (household_firm); position 无线电修理匠 @radio:person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:005588; state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:352; cash 2 (radio repair fee); skill; reputation 0.03; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3; flag +radio_repairer; knowledge “收音机的接地线被老鼠咬断，焊点冷得像一颗坏牙。”; role; flag +odd_job_apprentice_

  Rosa和伴侣把认识的那一天当作纪念日。没有登记，没有证人，只有一块从旧货摊买来的蛋糕和两杯兑了水的酒；Rosa在日历上画了一个别人看不懂的记号，第二年又画在同一个格子里。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.devotion 45 -> 49; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 39 -> 36; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1932.01** `agency:attend_meeting` tags: agency, agency:attend_meeting

  _effects: mood -0.03999999999999998; reputation -0.009999999999999995_

- **1932.10** `vienna-medical-treatment.sanatorium/ignore-diagnosis` tags: health, medical, family, money
  下午的活是搬纱锭，Rosa蹲下去再站起来眼前发黑，扶墙站了一会儿。工友老约瑟夫把自己的水杯推过来，说去诊所开个假条吧，她摇头，说假条要扣钱。
  _effects: condition; mood -0.03999999999999998_

- **1932.11** `vienna-illness-household.mother-falls/hire-care` tags: health, family, home
  Rosa托人请了巷尾的看护妇，每天来两回给Rosa Lechner擦洗换药，六克朗花得清清楚楚。看护费不便宜，Rosa晚上多接了一份缝补的活，手指被针扎了几回；Rosa Lechner的腿伤有人料理，Rosa却连着几夜睡不够。
  _effects: cash -6 (支付看护妇费用); health 0.08 (看护妇料理后伤势好转)_

- **1933.01** `vienna-life-depression/short-time` tags: adulthood, depression, work
  Rosa改成每周上三天班，工钱按日结算。家里的面包从每天一条变成隔天一条，Rosa开始把午饭省下来带回家。
  _effects: cash -3 (short-time work（其中3克朗记入债务）); condition_

- **1933.01** `ld18-intimacy.hairwash::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/a-knock-at-the-door` tags: love, intimacy, gentle, everyday, queer, body, secret_intimacy, reform
  头发刚擦到一半，Hermann Kral来敲门收煤气钱。Rosa把Ferdinand Lechner 2推进壁橱，自己披上外套去开门；床上的湿毛巾还没来得及收。Hermann Kral看了一眼，说下次记得把水倒了，楼梯会滑。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 36 -> 44; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 41 -> 46; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-40; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  户籍窗口换了一张新表格，配偶一栏不能空着。Rosa最后写了“同住人”，办事员抬头看了一眼，说这不作数。回家后，Rosa把表格折成很小的一块，塞进针线盒；伴侣问今天顺不顺利，Rosa说和平时一样。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 44 -> 50; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 46 -> 43; mood -0.019999999999999962_

  _effects: cash 1 (母亲洗衣挣的零钱); mood -0.050000000000000044_

- **1935.12** `vienna-housing-move.sublet-room/sublet-declined` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Rosa正要把空床许给Elisabeth Stein，Rosa Winter却在楼梯上拦住了，说这条街上去年有人招了生人合住，失过钥匙，惹出些麻烦。Rosa回家把铺盖重新叠好，回绝了Elisabeth Stein，只让他白天来喝口热水。空床仍空着，房租还是从自己的工钱里补。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30_

- **1936.01** `wc17-sweatshop.the-big-order::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-order-is-taken` tags: sweatshop, ownership, work, factory, order, apprentice, profit, craft
  旅馆老板Hermann Berg 2把一张订单摊在刨床上：四十张床架，月底交货。Rosa算了一遍工时，按现在的做法只能保本；第二天，Rosa把Franz Weiss的午饭时间缩短，又把工钱改成按件计。Franz Weiss没有反对，只在木料上多画了一道线。
  _effects: state_transition wc17-master-craftsperson -> wc17-sweatshop-owner; cash 4 (hotel order deposit); relation employer with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-65; reputation -0.02; flag +took_the_big_order; mood -0.010000000000000009; knowledge “四十张床架的利润来自缩短午饭时间和按件压价。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-86; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-65; role; flag -master_craftsperson; role; flag +sweatshop_owner_

- **1936.01** `state_tick:ld18-impossible-attachment/invented-anniversary/a-form-with-an-empty-line` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  户籍窗口换了一张新表格，配偶一栏不能空着。Rosa最后写了“同住人”，办事员抬头看了一眼，说这不作数。回家后，Rosa把表格折成很小的一块，塞进针线盒；伴侣问今天顺不顺利，Rosa说和平时一样。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 54 -> 60; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 43 -> 40; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1936.01** `r13-civ.recover-at-home::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/neighbor-soup` tags: health, recovery, care, household, food, vienna, urban, crisis
  Karl Kraus端来一碗兑了水的牛奶，顺手把Rosa家的炉子捅旺。Rosa靠在床头，听Karl Kraus讲楼下谁也被传染了，谁家把病人送去了医院。人走后，屋里还留着一点煤烟和奶味。
  _effects: cash -1 (退烧后的第一碗粥); health 0.04000000000000001 (退烧后的第一碗粥); flag +recovered_with_neighbor_help; relation friend with person-karl-kraus_

- **1936.03** `vienna-women-housework.wash-day/wash-day.rush` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family, injury
  木盆边的水渍映着天光，Rosa跪在石阶上揉搓床单，肘弯发酸。晾绳上的湿布滴答作响，Rosa Lechner探身把自家被单也挂上去，两人没多说话。
  _effects: health -0.020000000000000004 (长时间浸在碱水里，手部皮肤开裂); relation acquaintance with person-rosa-lechner_

- **1936.03** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/hospital-night` tags: health, medical, family, money
  电车在环城大道上哐当响，Emil Hofer攥着Rosa的胳膊，怕人从座位上滑下去。急诊厅的煤气灯发黄，长椅上全是咳嗽声，押金窗口的铜盘递出两克朗，换来一张硬纸号牌。
  _effects: cash -2 (医院急诊押金); health 0.07999999999999999 (急诊观察和冰敷退烧); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71_

- **1936.08** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_scald_hand` tags: home, social, family, housing, injury
  Rosa在合租房走廊的公用灶台上煮土豆，转身取盐时袖子扫翻了铁锅，滚水浇在左手背上。Hermann Lechner从隔壁冲出来，扯下自己的围裙裹住烫伤处，又跑下楼叫来住在二楼的接生婆。接生婆用蛋清和旧亚麻布包扎，嘱咐三天不能沾水。
  _effects: health -0.05 (左手背烫伤); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59_

- **1936.08** `state_reaction:wc17-sweatshop-owner/blood-on-the-cloth` tags: wc17-sweatshop-owner, work, factory, ownership, profit, guilt, labor, sweatshop
  一个学徒的手指被卷进皮带，血滴在麻布上。Rosa让人把她送去诊所，又吩咐把染红的布裁掉；晚上算账时，赔偿金和那批布的钱写在同一页。
  _effects: state_parameter wc17-sweatshop-owner.guilt 10 -> 20; state_parameter wc17-sweatshop-owner.profit 55 -> 53; cash -2 (worker compensation)_

- **1937.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/compromise_split_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Rosa把洗好的床单搭上院子里的公用晾衣绳，占了整条。Hermann Lechner抱着一盆湿衣服下来，说自家孩子明天要穿干净衬衫上学。两人在绳子两头扯了一阵，最后Rosa把床单对折，腾出一半，Hermann Lechner用木夹子把衬衫一件件排上去。谁也没道歉，但第二天见面都点了头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:007795_

  Rosa没有去诊所，只用热水和旧布洗了伤口。第二天伤口周围发红，家里人把仅剩的干净床单撕成条。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (untreated wound); cash -2 (clean cloth); condition_

  _effects: health -0.12 (beaten in police custody); condition; role; cash -5 (fine and lost work)_

  检查员在楼梯口闻到了煤烟和汗味，数了数窗边的床位。Rosa把罚单折进账本，转身催工头把剩下的活赶完；这一批货的利润刚好抵掉罚款。
  _effects: state_parameter wc17-sweatshop-owner.inspectionRisk 45 -> 60; state_parameter wc17-sweatshop-owner.profit 64 -> 61; cash -1 (inspection fine); mood -0.040000000000000036_

  两年过去，Rosa已经能闭着眼说出哪块地板会响、哪盏煤气灯该在几点关。师傅把备用钥匙交下来，没有解释为什么第三把钥匙磨得最亮；Rosa也没有问，只在巡查簿最后一页画了一个小小的记号。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 14 -> 24; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 20 -> 30; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.nightsWorked 0 -> 24; cash 2 (night-shift wages); skill_

  _effects: knowledge “我读到Rosa关于当前争论的文字。”; knowledge “我读到Rosa关于当前争论的文字。”; reputation 0.03_

  _effects: cash -2 (一张床与两条被子); flag +hid_lodger_from_landlord; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-112; knowledge “the landlord does not know about the second lodger”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-112_

  Mitzi Hofer问Rosa国歌第二句是什么，又让她说出本区议员的名字。Rosa答对了一半，Mitzi Hofer在表格上写“基本合格”，让下周来宣誓。Mitzi Hofer 2在门外等着，手里拿着一面小旗；Rosa出来时，Mitzi Hofer又在后面补了一句：别迟到。
  _effects: cash -8 (入籍宣誓前的口试); flag +citizenship_oath_scheduled; role; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-22; knowledge “the oath ceremony is held every second Tuesday”_

  Rosa把自家煤箱里仅剩的四块煤分了两块给Marie Huber，自己家那一夜没生火，裹着大衣睡的。孩子烧退了，Marie Huber第二天还煤时多塞了一把粗盐。Rosa当天下午就开始流清鼻涕，第三天咳得直不起腰。
  _effects: health -0.08000000000000002 (受凉咳嗽); food; relation friend with person-marie-huber_

  首演夜，Rosa在第二首歌的中间忘了两句词，干脆停下来听乐队多走了一个小节，再接着唱。台下有人以为这是新加的停顿，笑了一声，随后整间屋子都放松了。Emil Hofer在后台说，今晚之后，节目单上可以印Rosa的名字。
  _effects: state_add fame state:world-vienna-1912:630; cash 2 (首演酬劳与加演分成); role; reputation 0.05999999999999997; skill; flag +debut_success_

  破产拍卖会上，Rosa用四克朗买下一张没人举牌的瓶盖专利，只因为图纸画得好看。三个月后，屋顶无线电修理铺的矿泉水厂找上门：那圈软木垫正好能解决他们的漏气问题。Mitzi Bauer 3核对完专利号，屋顶无线电修理铺当场开出八十克朗的支票。
  _effects: cash 80 (sale of the bottle-cap patent); state_add ml18-sudden-fortune state:world-vienna-1912:629; reputation 0.06; flag +sold_patent; mood 0.09999999999999998; flag +sudden_fortune; reputation 0.020000000000000004; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  Rosa点着煤油灯缝到后半夜，把一捆领子全做完了。针脚密是密，可天亮时她看东西重影，手指被针扎了三回。星期六交活，Hermann Weiss 2量了量尺寸，收下整捆，数出七十五赫勒推过桌面。
  _effects: cash 0.75 (交了一整捆衬衫领子的计件工钱); health -0.03999999999999998 (通宵缝纫，眼睛疲劳，手指扎伤); mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-92_

  亲戚的婚礼上，Rosa被安排和伴侣分开坐。司仪问有没有人反对，Rosa低头喝汤；伴侣在另一桌替别人倒酒，手很稳，酒没有洒出来。散席时，两个人在衣帽间碰见，只说了一句“外面下雨”。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 91 -> 100; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.grief 4 -> 8; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 100 -> 100; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 28 -> 24; reputation -0.020000000000000018_

  Rosa把代言费换成了一叠工厂浴票，请Josef Lechner 2以肥皂厂名义发给纺织女工。厂主起初嫌麻烦，听说报纸会登照片，才点头。女工们周末排着队去公共浴场，Rosa没有剪彩，只在门口收票。那一年，肥皂没有多卖多少，Rosa走在街上却开始有人让路。

  就在法官要敲槌时，Elisabeth Kral忽然站起来，说糖罐是她打碎的，碎片埋在院子里的李树下。第二区夜班出租合作社的脸涨得通红，Elisabeth Weiss被叫去后院挖了半个钟头。Rosa被当庭释放，门外下着雨，Elisabeth Kral追出来，把一个纸包塞进Rosa手里，里面是三个月的工钱。

  有人问盟友，晚上回家时会不会替Rosa数钱。盟友笑着挡了回去，Rosa却看见对方之后整场都没有再碰话筒；回程的马车上，两个人第一次为一句没有说完的话争吵。

  观众开始厌倦同一个故事。园艺会请了更年轻的发明家，女子学校把Rosa的名字从海报上撤下；Rosa在咖啡馆听见有人说“那个人还在讲火柴”。同一个月，一张报纸把旧照片配上新标题，问Rosa是否已经“过气”。纠正的冲动又回来，但没有人再提问。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.legitimacy 45 -> 37; state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.boredom 4 -> 16; state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.invitations 2 -> 1; reputation -0.01999999999999999; mood -0.029999999999999916_

  Rosa在市场上被人叫住，对方笑着说“就是那个人”。Rosa也笑了一下，买完土豆就走；回到家才发现，手里的零钱少了一枚。
  _effects: state_parameter known-affair.notoriety 68 -> 76; reputation -0.03_

  楼上住户投诉夜里有人拖椅子，管事的把巡查簿翻到Rosa的那一页，说签字太潦草，看不出巡过几趟。Rosa想辩解，却想起那晚确实在楼梯间多站了一刻钟；扣掉的工钱刚好够买一双不漏水的鞋。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 30 -> 14; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.reliability 55 -> 45_

  盟友替Rosa办了一件没有记者在场的小事：去医院看了一个人，或者把一封信送到该送的地方。Rosa后来才知道，那件事比任何一场集会都更花时间。
  _effects: state_parameter pl19-public-ally.loyalty 69 -> 75; state_parameter pl19-public-ally.cost 23 -> 20_

  Rosa没有再修钟，把上工的时间交给楼下教堂的钟声。风从多瑙河那边刮过来时，钟声就散，窗户一关更听不见。有一回她误了钟点，到车间时工头已经在门口站着，扣了半个克朗。
  _effects: cash -0.5 (迟到被扣工钱); mood -0.050000000000000044_

  Rosa请了律师，说姑婆立遗嘱时已经认不清人。官司打了八个月，律师费、鉴定费和往返车钱加起来，最后拿到的十五克朗还不到原数的一半。Alois 2在法院门口递给Rosa一张圣诞卡，里面没有字；Rosa把它放进大衣内袋，回家路上买了两根蜡烛。
  _effects: cash 15 (the reduced settlement after contesting the will); relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002110:child; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-22; flag +contested_the_will; knowledge “律师费和鉴定费吃掉了遗嘱争议的大部分收益。”; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  她被拒绝入境，理由是“没有固定住所”。她在边境站给Mitzi打电话，电话转到邻居家；对方说“先回来再说”，她买了一张反向车票。她在边境站的长椅上坐到天亮，才买下反向车票。

  Rosa赢了三克朗，起身时才发现牌背有指甲划出的记号。Franz Winter笑着把牌收进袖子里，说这叫“借来的手气”。Rosa没有把钱退回去，只把那张有记号的牌偷偷带走。第二天，Rosa在当铺门口看见Franz Winter正在和一个收旧货的人分钱。

- **1946.12** `office16-scandal.public-hearing::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/resigned` tags: office, scandal, media, reputation, politics, power, vienna, urban
  Klara在门口堵了三天，第四天把Rosa的辞职信全文登了出来。信只有四行，最后一行是“为避免机构继续为我的判断付出代价”。Rosa把办公室的钥匙交给Mitzi Hofer，没有带走任何文件。
  _effects: position_end 夜班饲养员 @zoo:person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:008344; reputation -0.08; condition; flag +resigned_over_scandal; knowledge “辞职信被全文刊登，接任者当天就搬进了办公室。”_
