- **1933.01** `kernel-migrate.refugee-aid::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement::civic-refugee-rural/civic-refugee-rural-farm` tags: migration, refugee, missing_relative, care, postwar, rural, agriculture, small_town
  收容所把一批难民送到乡下，Mitzi Bauer 3被分到葡萄园做工。她去看时，对方正在搬筐，手上全是划痕；农场主说“管饭就不错了”，她把自己带的奶酪留下。她离开时，Mitzi Bauer 3把一只编好的草篮塞给她。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-72; cash -2 (带给难民的奶酪和车钱); flag +refugee_farm_placement; knowledge “乡下安置只保证管饭，工钱要等收成后按桶算”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-72_

- **1933.06** `ad20-stranger-request::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/take-the-envelope` tags: stranger, envelope, request, everyday, obligation, risk, rural, agriculture
  Ferdinand Berg在巷口拦住Lena，把一个没有字的信封塞过来，说“明天中午之前，别打开”。Lena问里面是什么；Ferdinand Berg说“你不需要知道”。信封很薄，捏起来却像有一块硬东西。
  _effects: state_add ad20-private-bargain state:world-vienna-1912:388; cash 2 (payment for carrying the envelope); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4; flag +envelope_taken; mood -0.010000000000000009; knowledge “信封很薄，里面像有一块硬东西。”; flag +private_bargain_active_

- **1933.08** `ad20-favor-called-in::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/do-the-favor` tags: favor, obligation, everyday, trust, debt, risk, rural, agriculture
  Josef Winter说只需要Lena在明天下午去一趟车站，把一个箱子交给穿灰外套的人。Lena问箱子里是什么；Josef Winter说“你不需要知道”。Lena还是去了，因为当年Josef Winter替Lena垫过一笔医药费。
  _effects: state_add ad20-private-bargain state:world-vienna-1912:389; cash -1 (the trip to the station); relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000142:child; flag +favor_done; mood -0.010000000000000009; knowledge “箱子里是什么，邻居没有说。”_

- **1933.11** `vienna-women-housework.wash-day/wash-day.quarrel` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  水龙头滴滴答答地漏，两人都伸手去扶桶沿。Lena看见自家未洗的床单还堆着，胸口发闷，把桶让了半掌宽。晾绳上她的衬衣角被风掀起，正挡着邻家的窗户。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-anna-bauer; reputation -0.02_

  包厢里已经坐了五个人，Elisabeth Bauer把一只藤箱放在第六个座位上，说箱子里是易碎品。Hermann Berg 2查票时数了数人头，让Lena站到走廊去，说“票上没写座位”。Elisabeth Bauer低头看报，藤箱占着座位，一路没有挪。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; flag +stood_in_corridor_with_ticket; knowledge “a wicker trunk can hold a seat on a crowded train”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-86_

  葡萄收获后，Alois Lechner把Lena领到酒窖边的长桌。Ferdinand Weiss 2刚从压榨机那边过来，袖口沾着葡萄汁；两人一起抬了一筐葡萄，Alois Lechner在旁边说今年的霜冻和嫁妆。Lena留下帮忙到天黑，手上全是紫印。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81; cash 2 (harvest wages from the afternoon in the vineyard); role; flag +vineyard_match; knowledge “对方家有三行葡萄，酒窖在坡下。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81_

  _effects: cash -2 (按月多付两克朗房租); food; mood -0.030000000000000027_

  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-89_

  Lena把话咽回去，只说等明年工钱稳一点再谈。Ferdinand Weiss 3没有追问，两人把桌上的面包分完，各自去上工；出门时Lena回头看了一眼，Ferdinand Weiss 3正把掉在桌上的面包屑拢进掌心，谁也没有把那个话头重新拾起来。

- **1938.10** `vienna-history-freud-letter/letter-answered` tags: history, psychoanalysis, freud, freud_contact, letter, medical_consultation
  Lena 把症状、睡眠和童年的一件小事写进四页信纸，寄到 Berggasse 19。十天后回信到了：Sigmund Freud 只写了两行，约Lena 在某个下午带信来，诊费在见面时再谈。Lena 把回信折好，发现信封背面有一道铅笔写的编号。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-sigmund-freud; knowledge “Freud 在 Berggasse 19 按固定诊时接待病人，先看信或听叙述，再按半小时收取诊费。”; cash -2 (postage and writing paper for the letter); mood 0.020000000000000018_

  媒人把最后一块杏仁糕吃完，两家还在为二十克朗争。Josef Lechner的父亲说少一个子儿也不行，Lena的母亲把清单揉成一团。媒人把盘子往自己那边挪了挪，继续吃。Josef Lechner送Lena到巷口，从口袋里掏出那枚戒指，又放回去；两人在巷口站了一会儿，各自回家。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000670:child; role; flag +dowry_dispute; cash -3 (coffee and pastries for the matchmaker); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-69_

  Lena在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang靠窗的桌边，把一只空咖啡杯转了半圈，说房租两人合付、桌子两人共用。Josef Lechner的手停在糖罐盖上，问了一句“要是过不下去呢”，没有当场答应，只说要想一想；杯里的咖啡凉了，话还悬在桌面上。

  Lena只答应让Elisabeth 2住三天，腾出半张床，又托Anton去打听便宜床位。三天里Elisabeth 2白天出去找活，傍晚回来坐在行军床边上，藤箱始终没有打开。第三天晚上Anton带回消息，说有一处工人宿舍，一周一克朗；Lena数出一克朗硬币放在箱盖上，替Elisabeth 2垫了第一周。
  _effects: cash -1 (替表亲垫付工人宿舍第一周); relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000925:child_

- **1944.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Lena在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Lena把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.050000000000000044 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:011903_

- **1944.01** `state_tick:lc17-caregiver/care-year/carried` tags: lc17-caregiver, care, family, illness, elder, duty, household, state
  Lena又撑过一年。药盒按星期分好，账本上多了一栏“看护”，工钱少了一些，但还没有断；Friedrich Hofer今年记得住Lena的名字，偶尔也记得住昨天吃了什么。
  _effects: state_parameter lc17-caregiver.burden 38 -> 34; state_parameter lc17-caregiver.resentment 18 -> 16; health -0.010000000000000009 (a year of broken nights); mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1944.01** `state_expire:office-under-siege` tags: office-under-siege, office, scandal, pressure, politics, legal, risk, state
  Lena的「职位受围」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove office-under-siege state:world-vienna-1912:675; flag -office_under_siege_

- **1944.01** `r13-civ.submit-manuscript::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-university/rejected-with-note` tags: culture, writing, manuscript, editor, publication, vienna, urban, crisis
  Hermann Weiss 2把稿子退回来，夹着一张写得潦草的便条：细节好，人物像纸。Lena在电车上把便条读了两遍，回家后把稿子摊在桌上，从第一行开始改。改到天亮，只删掉一句。
  _effects: mood -0.07999999999999996; flag +manuscript_rejected; skill; knowledge “the editor thought the characters were made of paper”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-55_

- **1944.02** `ld18-wedding.private-rite::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-university/rings-in-a-borrowed-room` tags: love, wedding, marriage, queer, secret, ritual, commitment, secret_intimacy
  婚礼在借来的房间里举行，证人是洗衣房的Helene 2。戒指是从当铺买的，誓词写在洗衣单背面；Helene 2念到“无论贫穷还是疾病”时，楼下有人敲门，三个人同时屏住呼吸。门开了，是来收煤气费的。
  _effects: state_add ld18-impossible-attachment state:world-vienna-1912:765; cash -1 (the pawnshop rings and the gas bill); mood 0.05999999999999994; flag +private_wedding_rite; flag +impossible_attachment_active_

- **1944.03** `vienna-women-housework.piecework-bundle/piecework.overnight` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family, injury
  Lena点着煤油灯缝到后半夜，把一捆领子全做完了。针脚密是密，可天亮时她看东西重影，手指被针扎了三回。星期六交活，Rosa Kral 2量了量尺寸，收下整捆，数出七十五赫勒推过桌面。
  _effects: cash 0.75 (交了一整捆衬衫领子的计件工钱); health -0.040000000000000036 (通宵缝纫，眼睛疲劳，手指扎伤); mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1944.07** `vienna-parish-aid.sick-call/sick-call.with-priest` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Lena觉得一个人去不合适，绕到教堂请了修女一同上楼。修女看过病人，留下药包和一小瓶圣水，说会从教区账上出钱请医生来看一趟。Ferdinand Berg在楼下等着，说这样妥当，病人家里没男人，多个见证人心里安稳。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4; mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +parish_sick_visitor_

- **1944.12** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-neighbors-together` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Lena把Mitzi Bauer 4和楼里另两户人家叫到楼道，把那张纸摆在窗台上传看。看门人在楼梯拐角听着，说房东礼拜三来收钱。几家人合计好，到那天一起等在门厅，先把克朗递过去，再把修补的事一件件摆出来。Lena把纸折进围裙口袋，觉得人凑齐了话也硬气些。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-105; mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +tenant_collective; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-105_

- **1945.01** `vienna-life-1945-liberation/survives` tags: war, liberation, 1945
  苏联士兵进城时，Lena躲在门后听见坦克履带压过石板路。几天后街口贴出告示，Lena去领了一份汤和一块面包。
  _effects: role; food; cash -2 (replacement documents)_

- **1945.01** `ld18-intimacy.hairwash::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-university/the-water-goes-cold` tags: love, intimacy, gentle, everyday, queer, body, secret_intimacy, vienna
  Emil坐在床边，Lena用搪瓷盆替她洗头。水凉了又添，肥皂泡顺着耳后流下来；Emil闭着眼说，小时候她母亲也是这样替她洗的。窗外有人收煤，铁锹刮过石板，两个人谁也没有动。
  _effects: relation friend with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.devotion 70 -> 75; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 15 -> 12; mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +hairwash_sunday_

  Lena的「照料一个衰弱的亲人」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove lc17-caregiver state:world-vienna-1912:577_

- **1945.01** `state_tick:ld18-impossible-attachment/invented-anniversary/a-form-with-an-empty-line` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  户籍窗口换了一张新表格，配偶一栏不能空着。Lena最后写了“同住人”，办事员抬头看了一眼，说这不作数。回家后，Lena把表格折成很小的一块，塞进针线盒；伴侣问今天顺不顺利，Lena说和平时一样。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 12 -> 18; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 55 -> 52; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1945.01** `p9-history-cultural-contact.join-circle/circle-rejects` tags: culture, knowledge, letter, lecture, editor, training, circle, task
  有人上下打量Lena的衣领，低声说了句什么，桌边笑起来。Lena把杯子放下，咖啡还剩一半。Maria Hofer送他到门口，说“别在意”，没有提下星期四。
  _effects: cash -1 (咖啡钱); mood -0.07999999999999996; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-115_

- **1945.04** `vienna-market-household.friday-fish/friday-fish.buy` tags: home, market, food, social
  鱼摊快收了，Lena挑了一条最小的鳟鱼，摊主顺手抓了把欧芹丢进纸里。黄油在锅里化开，鱼皮卷起来，楼道里都是味。Gustav Bauer敲门时，Lena从鱼尾切下一小块递过去。
  _effects: cash -6 (买鳟鱼和黄油); food; mood 0.07999999999999996_

- **1945.09** `vienna-historical-social-movement.public-meeting/back-immediate-action` tags: history, historical_process, social_movement, organization, politics
  Lena把帽子捏在手里上台，说救济表格要填三张、盖章两处，煤却今晚就见底。年轻人拍椅子背，Berta Weiss 2在门口皱着眉。主持人记下赞成与反对的人数，纸板上的字越写越小。
  _effects: reputation 0.05; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-26; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1946.01** `state_tick:ld18-impossible-attachment/invented-anniversary/the-date-that-is-not-on-paper` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  Lena和伴侣把认识的那一天当作纪念日。没有登记，没有证人，只有一块从旧货摊买来的蛋糕和两杯兑了水的酒；Lena在日历上画了一个别人看不懂的记号，第二年又画在同一个格子里。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.devotion 75 -> 79; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 18 -> 15; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1946.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.last-standing-ticket/p9-social-daily-culture.last-standing-ticket.buy-for-elder` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:culture
  前面的老人掏零钱时手抖得厉害，数了三遍也没凑够。Lena替老人把四十赫勒递进窗口，老人连声道谢，硬塞回一枚十赫勒铜板。等Lena自己买票时，窗口已经挂出“售罄”的牌子，只好站在剧院门廊下听里面隐约的乐声，脚底冰凉；这一趟净少三十赫勒，却让队伍里的人都记住了这份体面。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (替排队的老人垫付站票钱); cash 0.1 (老人硬塞回的铜板); reputation 0.04; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1946.11** `vienna-women-housework.wash-day/wash-day.rush` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family, injury
  木盆边的水渍映着天光，Lena跪在石阶上揉搓床单，肘弯发酸。晾绳上的湿布滴答作响，Luise Bauer 2探身把自家被单也挂上去，两人没多说话。
  _effects: health -0.020000000000000018 (长时间浸在碱水里，手部皮肤开裂); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-85_

- **1946.12** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.sick-parent-night/hospital-deposit` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Lena跑了一趟医院，把Friedrich Hofer送进病房：押金三克朗，搪瓷杯和旧毛巾一克朗二十赫勒，头三天的药费三克朗八十赫勒，一共八克朗。护士收走便盆，换上带刻度的玻璃壶。Lena回到空了的床边，把塌下去的椅子扶正，才发现自己一整天只喝了一杯咖啡。
  _effects: cash -8 (住院押金和住院用品); mood -0.05999999999999994; flag +parent_hospitalized_

- **1947.01** `ld18-absurd.moral-prize::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-university/a-medal-for-model-household` tags: absurd, irony, scandal, public, marriage, queer, secret_intimacy, vienna
  道德协会把一枚铜牌送到Lena和Rosa的店里，说她们的铺子窗明几净、账目清楚，是模范家庭。Hermann Berg 2握着她们的手，问什么时候请吃喜酒；Lena说等生意再好一点，Rosa在旁边把抹布拧成了麻花。
  _effects: reputation 0.07999999999999999; cash 2 (the prize money and the new customers); state_add ld18-public-desire state:world-vienna-1912:873; flag +model_household_medal; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +public_desire_active_

  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.spectacle 45 -> 53; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.shame 10 -> 15; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  Lena把报纸推到一边，和Franz Winter聊起电车票价要涨的传闻。两人拿过糖罐当算盘，在桌布上比划通勤开销，两杯咖啡四十赫勒，争论得忘了时间，直到窗外的煤气灯亮起来。Lena起身时发现大衣下摆沾了咖啡渍，晚饭的点早过了，却约好下回还坐这张桌子。

  Lena和Mitzi Bauer 3在同一个院子里租下两间相邻的小房，中间只隔一道薄墙。晚上收工后，一个人敲三下，另一个就敲两下；谁也没有提婚书，但每个月的房租都各付一半，煤也买双份。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.devotion 79 -> 89; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.improvisation 60 -> 75; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 15 -> 10; relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-72; cash -2 (two rents and double coal); mood 0.050000000000000044; flag +two_rooms_one_wall_

  互助金的铁盒上了两把锁，钥匙一把在Lena手里，一把在邻街的面包师那里。取钱时面包师说“月底前要补回来”。药买回来了，雇主那边的人说手续“走流程”，说完就去忙别的。
  _effects: cash -2 (从互助金垫付药费); health 0.05999999999999999 (及时买到药); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-85_

  _effects: reputation -0.08; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000925:child; cash -1 (互助会停止发放的补贴); condition; flag +reputation_damaged_by_betrayal_

  Lena的信写得好，笔画干净，算盘也快，可速记一栏空了大半。Josef Bauer 3把卷子批了“文书可用，速记欠熟”，人事科把他安排到档案室抄旧保单，日薪两克朗四十赫勒，比正式办事员少一截。桌旁没有窗，只有一盏悬在头顶的灯，他抄到下班时右肩发僵。

  _effects: cash -0.2 (次日再跑一趟所需的电车票); mood -0.040000000000000036; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-166; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-138_

  Lena没有立刻答话，先去敲Anna Bauer的门。两人一起数落墙上的水渍和门框里灌进来的风，楼梯间的窗户修好了，自家墙皮还在剥落。Anna Bauer提议一起把话带给看门人：先修好漏水的墙角，涨租的事再谈。Lena把纸重新折好，请他把话带到门外。
  _effects: mood 0.030000000000000027_

  他刚说到罢工基金，靠门那排就有人喊“先顾眼前”。话头被截断两三次，后屋分成两片声音，主持人敲桌子也没压住。散会时一个老工人拍拍他肩膀说太急了，他出门才发现手心全是汗。
  _effects: mood -0.06000000000000005; reputation -0.03; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000923:child_

  _effects: mood 0.030000000000000027; reputation 0.01999999999999999_

  _effects: reputation 0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:006074:child; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  鹦鹉死在第二个冬天。窗缝里灌进来的风把笼布吹开，它缩在食盆旁边，像一团被揉皱的灰纸。Gustav Bauer说条件没有完成，十二克朗归了姑妈的教子；Lena把鸟埋在院子里，留下那只笼子。春天，笼子里长出一丛野草，Lena没有拔。
  _effects: cash -2 (鹦鹉的药费和笼布); reputation -0.010000000000000009; relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-144; condition; flag +parrot_died_before_the_will; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-144_

  那孩子一边跑一边吹口哨，狗就跟着口哨声跑。Lena追到街角时，罗盘钟正敲整点。她扶着墙喘，低头看见鞋底磨穿的洞，算着补鞋匠那里补一只要收六十赫勒。
  _effects: health -0.020000000000000018 (追狗跑得脚底发疼); cash -0.6 (补鞋底的开销); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44_

### 146 subject events

- **1914.07** `iw18-infancy.first-word::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-tenement/a-word-for-bread` tags: infancy, first_word, memory, family, wonder, eerie, vienna, urban
  Anton指着桌上的面包说出一个短词，声音轻得像在试音。Josefine Hofer把面包掰下一角，泡进热水里喂给孩子；Anton吃完又指了一次，这次说得更清楚。厨房里的人都笑了，连灶上的水汽也像在往上冒。
  _effects: food; mood 0.03; flag +first_word_bread_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-half-flour` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  面粉袋压在胸口，越抱越沉。Anton前面的大婶一直往后靠，Anton就把膝盖顶住对方的篮子，硬留出半步。买到半袋面粉后，Anton的鞋跟留在人堆里，找回来时鞋面全是泥。回家路上布袋换了两次肩，赤脚踩过石板时脚底发麻。
  _effects: food; health -0.020000000000000018 (赤脚走石板路磨伤脚底); skill_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-three-heller` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  “拽平，别留褶。”Luise Kral在绳子那头喊。Anton踮着脚把床单拉直，碱水把指缝泡得发白。收工时天已经黑了，Luise Kral数了三赫勒，又在围裙上擦干手才递过来。Anton把钱塞进袜筒，回家路上一直攥着，虎口的裂口沾了汗，火辣辣地疼。
  _effects: cash 0.03 (帮邻居晾床单挣的三赫勒); health -0.030000000000000027 (碱水泡手，虎口裂开); relation acquaintance with person-luise-kral; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11_

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-both-families` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  Josef Bauer把Anton拽到队伍前头，往自己身前塞，说“孩子占不了多大地儿”。窗口一次递出两包面包，Anton把邻居那包夹在胳肢窝，自己这包抱在胸前。到Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang门口，Anton蹲下把两包并排，按Josef Bauer说的份量分开，连找零的铜板也分成两小堆。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-josef-bauer; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18_

- **1921.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-bread-with-friend` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  Katharina Kral家的孩子站在绳子另一头，Anton递夹子时故意慢半拍，两个人把床单拉得没有一道褶。收工后Katharina Kral端出半包隔夜面包，掰开时掉了一桌屑。Anton把分到的那块用围裙兜住，回家路上闻着麦味，硬是没咬。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5_

- **1922.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-both-families` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  队伍拐弯时，Maria Kral朝Anton招手。Anton挤过去，替两家把面包领出来，纸包用不同的绳结系住。家里人晚上解开绳结，先掂了掂份量，再问价钱；Anton把每一包的面包数、找零和欠的铜板都说了一遍，家里人听完才把两包分别收进柜子。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-maria-kral; skill_

- **1922.03** `vienna-childhood-friends.courtyard-race/fell-on-wet-stones` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  Anton想从床单底下钻过去，脚下一滑，膝盖磕在石板棱上。Friedrich Hofer听见哭声出来，先看了看伤口，又回去端水。手帕扎得很紧，Anton试着站起来，被Friedrich Hofer按回台阶上。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-friedrich-hofer_

- **1922.03** `vienna-childhood-street.washhouse-fever/fever-recover` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  Anton在洗衣房的长凳上睡着，醒来时喉咙像塞了棉花。Josefine Hofer把最后的干净布巾浸了凉水，敷在Anton额头上，整夜换了七八回。第四天Anton能坐起来喝粥，下嘴唇脱下一小片皮，Josefine Hofer用指尖替Anton拈掉。
  _effects: condition; relation kin with person-josefine-hofer_

- **1923.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-dragged-docked-pay` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  “你看看这角。”Rosa Kral把床单拎起来，煤渣簌簌往下掉。Anton伸手去拍，反而把灰按进布纹里。Rosa Kral从工钱里数回两枚，只留一赫勒在Anton手心，让重洗那角。晚饭前Anton一直蹲在井台边，换了两盆水，指甲缝里全是黑泥。
  _effects: cash 0.01 (被扣后只剩一赫勒); mood -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-20; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-20_

- **1923.05** `p9-childhood-household.strap-by-the-door/strap-raised-then-dropped` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors
  皮带从门后抽出来，Friedrich Hofer举到半空，停住了。Anton闭着眼等，只听见皮带扣碰在桌沿上。Friedrich Hofer把它放下，说晚饭后不许出门，把碎碗片捡干净。Anton蹲在地上一片片捡，窗外传来同伴的笑声，指尖被瓷片划出一道白口。
  _effects: mood -0.04; relation kin with person-friedrich-hofer; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10_

- **1923.05** `vienna-childhood-street.cigarette-card/card-trade` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  “三张换一张。”Hedwig把皇帝像在Anton眼前晃了晃。Anton数出三张攒了半年的烟画，一张张递过去。皇帝像到手还没捂热，Hedwig已经推开杂货铺的门，用同一张卡换了糖。Anton把卡举到光下看，觉得皇帝的脸有点歪。
  _effects: mood 0.04; relation friend with person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912_

- **1924.11** `vienna-adolescence.workshop-apprentice/apprenticeship-accepted` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne先看Anton的手，又让Anton推两下刨子试试。Anton用力过猛，刨刀啃进木头，Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne皱眉，让再来一遍。第二遍木花薄而匀。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne说“留下”，当面点了三克朗给Josefine Hofer，算头一周饭钱，又指了指墙上的钟，说迟到一刻钟扣半天。
  _effects: cash 3 ({subject}学徒首周的膳食预付款); education apprenticeship; reputation 0.1; relation kin with person-josefine-hofer_

- **1925.01** `r13-edu.music-lesson::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/first-recital` tags: education, music, lesson, craft, culture, reform, medical, schooling
  Anton在期末演奏会上拉完一首短曲，弓毛断了两根，最后一个音还是稳住了。Mitzi Weiss在台下点头，师母把手套摘下来鼓掌。回家后，Anton把琴擦了三遍，才发现肩托把脖子磨出一道红印，碰一下就疼。Anton没有告诉任何人。琴盒没关。
  _effects: reputation 0.05000000000000002; flag +first_recital; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-29_

- **1925.02** `statistical_infection`
  Anton在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1925.03** `vienna-adolescence.brothers-torn-jacket/sell-the-coat` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  旧货贩子把外套翻过来看了看里布，伸出两个手指。Anton接过两枚克朗，塞进鞋垫底下，走回家时脚心一直硌着。晚上Josefine Hofer站在门后，手搭在空衣钩上，问外套呢。Anton说不知道，声音比平时高了一点。
  _effects: cash 2 (卖掉哥哥旧外套); relation kin with person-josefine-hofer; mood -0.04000000000000001_

- **1925.03** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Anton的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1925.05** `statistical_recovery`
  Anton从肺炎中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

- **1925.09** `r14-edu.leaving-school::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/sent-to-factory` tags: education, school_dropout, child_labour, poverty, reform, medical, schooling, home
  Friedrich Hofer把Anton的书包从钉子上取下来，说厂里招装订工，明天就去。Maria Moser傍晚来家里，站在门口劝了半个钟头，Friedrich Hofer只说灶上还坐着锅。第二天清早，Anton穿着改短的围裙出门，书包留在门后，里面还有半块橡皮。头一个月工钱三克朗。
  _effects: cash 3 (书包挂在门后的钉子上); flag +left_school_for_factory; role; education none; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33_

- **1925.09** `yf18-firstlook.tram-glove::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/keep-it-in-the-satchel` tags: first_love, love, tram, glove, youth, courtship, reform, medical
  Anton把手套塞进书包最里层，回家后放在枕头下面。第二天上学，Anton每隔一会儿就摸一下书包带，像在确认一件不能给家里看见的东西；手套上还留着电车木座的气味。
  _effects: state_add yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:77; flag +yf18_kept_glove; mood 0.02; flag +yf18_first_awakening_active_

- **1926.01** `yf18-dare.bridge-rail::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/fall-on-the-ice` tags: dare, bridge, river, friendship, risk, reform, medical, schooling
  Anton走到第四步，鞋底在霜上滑了一下，整个人挂在栏杆外侧。Mitzi抓住Anton的手腕，Lena跑去叫桥头卖烤栗子的老人；Anton被拉上来时，膝盖已经湿透，第二天走路一瘸一拐。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 60 -> 66; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 5 -> 0; relation friend with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912; flag +yf18_bridge_scare; mood -0.03_

- **1926.01** `state_tick:yf18-first-awakening/half-year-of-glances/answered-glance` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state
  Anton在教堂门口或学校走廊里又遇见Hedwig。这一次对方先点了头，Anton准备好的整段话全忘了，只把手里的书换到另一只手上；回家后Anton才发现，自己把书拿倒了。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 66 -> 71; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 0 -> 8; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.reciprocated false -> true; relation friend with person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.03_

- **1926.01** `kernel-edu.prepare-for-exam::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/edu-prepare-barely` tags: task, adolescence, school, exam, study, reform, medical, schooling
  Anton白天帮家里干活，只能夜里复习。煤油灯熏得眼睛发酸，第二天考试时，Anton把会做的题做完，剩下的凭记忆填满。成绩不高，但够升级。Maria Moser在成绩单上写：尚有潜力。
  _effects: skill; condition; reputation 0.010000000000000009; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33; flag +passed_exam_barely_

- **1926.01** `state_reaction:yf18-first-awakening/exam-week-distraction` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state
  考试周里，Anton把课本摊在桌上，眼睛却一直看着窗外那条电车线。笔记的页边多了几个没有写完的名字，Anton用橡皮擦掉，纸面起了毛。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 71 -> 68; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 0 -> 2_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:002888_

- **1926.04** `r3-teen.first-cigarette::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/refuses` tags: smoking, friendship, risk, family, school, reform, medical, schooling
  Anton把烟推回去，说家里有人咳血。Emil Winter把烟叼在自己嘴上，说胆小鬼才拿家里当借口。放学路上两人隔着三步走，谁也没先开口。他把手插进空口袋，摸到上午剩下的一截粉笔，捏成了两段。
  _effects: reputation 0.03; flag +refused_first_cigarette; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-56; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-56; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-40_

- **1926.06** `r2-faith.parish-school-fee::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/free-place-granted` tags: faith, school, charity, education, fees, reform, medical, schooling
  Grete Berg在学费簿上找到Anton的名字，用红笔在旁边写了“免”。Grete Stein说课本还得自己买，Grete Berg就从柜子里翻出一本旧算术，封面写着上一个学生的名字。Anton把书抱回家，Josefine Hofer用报纸包了书皮，说念完这本再说。
  _effects: flag +received_free_parish_school_place; role; relation friend with person-grete-stein; knowledge “the parish keeps a red-ink list of fee exemptions”; education primary; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-6_

- **1926.09** `vienna-adolescence.school-or-brickworks/stay-in-school` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  教室里只剩炉子噼啪响。Ferdinand Lechner 2把算术本翻到最后一页，指着那道应用题，说再念一年，商业学校可以考。Anton用铅笔在边上重算了一遍。门外有脚步声，Friedrich Hofer站在走廊里，帽檐压低半指，听完才走。
  _effects: education secondary; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-40; relation kin with person-friedrich-hofer_

- **1927.01** `vienna-life-apprenticeship/printshop-apprentice` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, employment
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne让Anton下周一到排字房报到，先搬铅字和扫地，月薪十八克朗。家里用第一笔预付工资买了双结实的鞋。
  _effects: education apprenticeship; employment Schriftsetzerlehrling @ org-winter-print; cash 6 (apprentice advance); role_

- **1927.01** `yf18-firstlook.parish-dance::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/the-second-dance` tags: first_love, dance, church, courtship, jealousy, reform, medical, schooling
  舞卡上第一支曲子被Lena写走了。Anton在教会礼堂的木地板上踩错了两步，Lena说没关系，把手放回Anton背后；第三支曲子响起时，Hedwig在门口冲Anton比了个手势，Anton假装没看见。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 68 -> 76; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 8 -> 18; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.reciprocated true -> true; relation friend with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.04_

  Anton在教堂门口或学校走廊里又遇见Hedwig。这一次对方先点了头，Anton准备好的整段话全忘了，只把手里的书换到另一只手上；回家后Anton才发现，自己把书拿倒了。

  Anton回到学校，发现同班同学已经毕业，新班级里没人认识Anton。Anton在操场上站了一下午，最后没有进教室，跟着父亲去了工地。晚上Anton把课本放进箱底，没舍得扔。工地的工头看Anton年纪小，只让Anton搬轻的。课本压在最底下。
  _effects: mood -0.07; flag +dropout_confirmed; relation kin with person-friedrich-hofer_

- **1927.02** `yf18-runaway.cousin-letter::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/answer-in-secret` tags: runaway, letter, cousin, work, plan, reform, medical, schooling
  格拉茨的表姐来信，说旅馆缺一个叠床单的女孩，管吃住，工钱少但能攒下。Anton把信藏在算术本里，用铅笔在背面写回信；邮票是从Josefine Hofer的抽屉里偷的，信封上不敢写自己的名字，只写了“内详”。
  _effects: state_add yf18-runaway-plan state:world-vienna-1912:161; cash -1 (a stolen stamp and writing paper); flag +yf18_cousin_reply; mood 0.03; flag +yf18_runaway_plan_active_

- **1927.09** `kernel-edu.friendship::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/edu-friend-forbidden` tags: adolescence, school, friendship, books, family, reform, medical, schooling
  Friedrich Hofer说Emil家里没有正经职业，不许再来往。Anton嘴上答应，放学还是绕路去Emil家。被发现后，Friedrich Hofer把借书证撕了。Emil在门口等了两天，第三天没有再来。Anton把撕碎的借书证捡起来，夹进书里。
  _effects: relation kin with person-friedrich-hofer; flag +forbidden_friendship; mood -0.07_

- **1928.01** `vienna-life-young-worker/long-shift` tags: adolescence, labour, union
  Anton连续上了十二天夜班，最后一天在机器旁睡着，被工头踢醒。月底多拿了两克朗，咳嗽却一直没好。
  _effects: cash 2 (overtime pay); condition_

- **1928.01** `yf18-firstlook.essay-exam::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/read-aloud-as-a-warning` tags: first_love, exam, essay, school, humiliation, reform, medical, schooling
  Ferdinand Lechner 2在班上念了Anton的作文，念到“她的手套放在木座上”时停了一下，说这就是考试里最危险的东西：把私事当成论据。全班笑了，Anton盯着桌面上的墨水圈，听见Elisabeth Bauer在最后一排合上了书。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 2 -> 22; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 81 -> 85; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 26 -> 16; reputation -0.04000000000000001; relation friend with person-elisabeth-bauer; mood -0.05000000000000002_

  Ferdinand Kral用食指在字行上划过去，停在一处宽缝上，又停在下一处窄缝上。Anton想去改，Ferdinand Kral已经把字盘抽走，让先去洗墨辊。Anton蹲在后院搓了半下午，指关节被清洗剂咬得发白；收工前Ferdinand Kral才让Anton站在旁边看，记住字距该留几线。

- **1929.02** `r15-work.master-takes-on::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/signed-contract` tags: work, craft, apprentice, trade, poverty, reform, medical, schooling

  _effects: relation employer with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-68; state_add apprenticeship-contract state:world-vienna-1912:217; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-68; education apprenticeship_

- **1929.06** `yf18-dare.prater-slide::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/caught-by-the-watchman` tags: dare, prater, fairground, comic, friendship, reform, medical, schooling
  Ferdinand Kral的灯从摩天轮那边晃过来时，Mitzi已经跑没影了，Anton的鞋带却挂在木槽边。Ferdinand Kral把Anton带到园门口，记下住址，说星期一让学校来领人；Anton一路上只担心Mitzi会不会把这件事讲给全班。
  _effects: reputation -0.05; mood -0.03999999999999998; flag +yf18_prater_caught; relation friend with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912; cash -2 (a fine paid at the Prater office)_

- **1929.11** `r3-teen.sexual-rumor::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::reform-school-paint-order/confronted` tags: rumor, shame, school, reputation, institution, reform, medical, schooling
  Anton在操场边堵住Lena，问他从哪听来的。他先说是别人说的，后来承认是自己编的，因为Anton在课堂上答对了他答错的问题。Maria Moser让他们各写一份说明，两份都锁进抽屉。
  _effects: reputation 0.039999999999999994; flag +confronted_rumor_source; relation rival with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; knowledge “the classmate invented the rumour out of envy”_

- **1930.01** `oj18-perfume.apprentice::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/nose-returns` tags: perfume, apprentice, smell, shop, craft, work, odd_job, reform
  Anton感冒了三天，鼻子像塞了棉花。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne把十二只小瓶排在柜台上，让Anton闭眼闻；前十一只都答错了，最后一只，Anton说出“佛手柑和一点旧木头”。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne把那只瓶子推到一边，说“记住这个味道，它比你的名字值钱”。
  _effects: state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:263; skill; reputation 0.03; flag +perfume_apprentice; knowledge “第十二只瓶子是佛手柑和旧木头，师傅说这个味道比名字值钱。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2; role; flag +odd_job_apprentice_

- **1930.01** `vienna-social-movement.recruitment/join-and-carry` tags: development, social_movement, organization, politics
  Anton在楼梯下面接到一张折成四折的传单。纸上没有署名，只写着下一次集会的地址。Anton去了，在昏暗的屋里报上姓名，成为第二区夜班出租合作社的一员，并答应负责自己所在街区的联络。
  _effects: organization_membership; role; relation acquaintance with person-anna-bauer_

- **1930.01** `state_tick:apprenticeship-contract/workshop-year/mistake` tags: apprenticeship-contract, education, craft, apprentice, work, state, state_tick
  Anton废掉了一块料，师傅扣了工钱。晚饭时谁也没提这件事。
  _effects: state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.failures 0 -> 1; cash -1 (wasted material); mood -0.03999999999999998_

- **1930.01** `kernel-health.task-pharmacy-hunt::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/pharmacy-gives-up` tags: health, task, pharmacy, shortage, black_market, reform, medical, schooling
  Anton在柜台前站了很久，最后把处方留在那里，说等有货再来。回家路上经过医院，看见药房窗口也挂着“无货”；第二天，处方被雨水泡烂在口袋里。Anton把湿透的处方摊在炉边烤，字还是糊了。
  _effects: cash -1 (tram fare and a paper envelope for the prescription); condition; role; knowledge “医院药房也会挂“无货”；处方留在柜台，第二天下雨就泡烂了。”_

- **1930.11** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_found_by_neighbor` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Anton傍晚从面粉厂下工回来，发现挂在腰带上的房门钥匙不见了，在楼梯间来回找了四趟。Hermann Bauer在二楼拐角的煤堆旁捡到那把铜钥匙，钥匙上缠着红线，一眼就认出来。Hermann Bauer敲开Anton的门，把钥匙拍在桌上，说自己眼神好使。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7; mood 0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7_

- **1930.12** `vienna-legacy-worklife.sick-fund/sick-fund.keep-quiet` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  止咳药水是褐色的，瓶口塞着软木塞，摇一摇会起沫。Anton看着工友把表折成小块，折了三折才塞进帽檐。墙上的规章还贴在那里，纸角被风掀起，没人去按。
  _effects: mood -0.04999999999999999; relation colleague with person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912_

- **1931.01** `oj18-perfume.recipe::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/recipe-stolen` tags: perfume, shop, recipe, theft, customer, work, odd_job, reform
  对街的店员Anna Bauer抄走了Anton的配方：前调多放一滴橙花，后调少放半滴麝香，成本就能降一成。Anton在市场上看见同样的瓶子，标签换了名字；店主没有去告，只在门口挂了一块小牌：本店不卖便宜的味道。
  _effects: cash -2 (lost sales to the copied scent); reputation -0.03; relation rival with person-anna-bauer; flag +recipe_stolen; knowledge “对街的瓶子用了我的配方，前调多一滴橙花，后调少半滴麝香。”_

- **1931.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/learns` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Anton把Katharina Berg的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Katharina Berg今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Anton听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 39 -> 47; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 56 -> 53; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1931.01** `state_tick:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/strange-year/learned-the-building` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  又过了一个冬天，Anton能从楼梯的响声判断是谁在值夜，也能从煤气灯的颜色判断哪一段管道该修。师傅把巡查路线改了三次，最后仍然照Anton画的那张走；两个人都没有提这件事。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 32 -> 42; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 23 -> 33; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.nightsWorked 0 -> 24; cash 2 (night-shift wages); skill_

  Anton把带来的半块肥皂放在窗台上，说知道不够。Maria Hofer没有碰肥皂，只说那天在场的人都记得Anton笑的样子。Anton站到腿发麻，最后把肥皂收回口袋，转身时听见窗子从里面关上了。
  _effects: mood -0.11999999999999994; flag +apology_rejected; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2_

- **1931.06** `major-crisis.press-accident/hand-crushed` tags: crisis, work, accident, disability, death, printshop, health, unemployment
  送纸的滚筒没有停稳，Anton的袖口被卷进去，右手跟着进了两个铁辊之间。Lena扑过去扳离合器，机器又转了一圈才停。医院截掉了食指和中指的第二关节；厂里先垫二十四克朗，六周后事故保险结算，工厂说没有适合一只手排字的位置。
  _effects: condition; unemployment (the printshop had no composing work for a one-handed worker); cash 24 (accident insurance payment assessed after six weeks); role; flag +industrial_disability; relation colleague with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; move place-wien-allgemeines-krankenhaus; move place-wien-leopoldstadt-tenement_

- **1931.06** `state_reaction:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/the-thing-in-the-basement` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  夜班结束前，Anton听见地下室传来拖拽声。下去时，门开着，地上只有一道湿拖把的痕迹；第二天，所有人都说那间屋子锁了十年。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 15 -> 23; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 35 -> 32; mood -0.030000000000000027; knowledge “地下室那扇门后面有东西被搬动过，但巡查簿上没有记录。”_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:005031_

- **1931.09** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.quit` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Anton说互助会先退了，这笔钱要留给煤和面包。Otto Bauer没争辩，在蓝皮本上把名字划了一道，问清楚是不是全家都退，然后把划掉的那行折进本子夹缝。她说以后想再进，要重新排三个月。门关上，Anton听见铅笔在楼梯上轻轻敲了两下栏杆。
  _effects: cash 0.4 (省下本周会费留作煤钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10; flag +funeral_society_left_

- **1931.11** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.sick-parent-night/hospital-deposit` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Anton跑了一趟医院，把Josefine Hofer送进病房：押金三克朗，搪瓷杯和旧毛巾一克朗二十赫勒，头三天的药费三克朗八十赫勒，一共八克朗。护士收走便盆，换上带刻度的玻璃壶。Anton回到空了的床边，把塌下去的椅子扶正，才发现自己一整天只喝了一杯咖啡。
  _effects: cash -8 (住院押金和住院用品); health 0.05999999999999999 (父亲住进医院得到治疗); mood -0.06; flag +parent_hospitalized_

  Hermann Lechner把一只箱子和工票搬去合租房，带走一半锅碗。Anton留在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang，把空出来的床租给一个学徒，用租金补房租；搬家花掉四克朗，Anton在户籍上成了分居的人，夜里听见隔壁床板响，总要愣一下。
  _effects: relation_end partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59; household; cash -4 (moving and separate goods（其中4克朗记入债务）); mood -0.06000000000000005; role_

  _effects: cash 3 (门框上的石灰线); reputation -0.1; flag +broke_quarantine; knowledge “the workshop does not ask why a quarantined worker returned”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-22_

  _effects: cash -2 (临时找住处与搬家的费用（其中2克朗记入债务）); relation rival with person-otto-winter; reputation -0.04000000000000001; flag +rent_book_taken; condition; mood -0.09999999999999998_

  Anton把银餐具裹进围裙，塞给Marie Huber，说“先放你家煤窖”。Friedrich Hofer来查时，只看见空木盒和一张旧照片。Marie Huber晚上把围裙送回来，说“煤窖潮，银器会黑”。Anton把围裙藏在床垫下面，一夜没睡。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; condition; flag +hid_silver_in_neighbors_cellar; relation friend with person-marie-huber; knowledge “the neighbor’s coal cellar is never inspected”_

- **1932.12** `vienna-illness-household.mother-falls/hire-care` tags: health, family, home
  Anton托人请了巷尾的看护妇，每天来两回给Josefine Hofer擦洗换药，六克朗花得清清楚楚。看护费不便宜，Anton晚上多接了一份缝补的活，手指被针扎了几回；Josefine Hofer的腿伤有人料理，Anton却连着几夜睡不够。
  _effects: cash -6 (支付看护妇费用（其中6克朗记入债务）); health 0.07999999999999999 (看护妇料理后伤势好转)_

- **1934.11** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.sick-parent-night/hospital-deposit` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Anton跑了一趟医院，把Friedrich Hofer送进病房：押金三克朗，搪瓷杯和旧毛巾一克朗二十赫勒，头三天的药费三克朗八十赫勒，一共八克朗。护士收走便盆，换上带刻度的玻璃壶。Anton回到空了的床边，把塌下去的椅子扶正，才发现自己一整天只喝了一杯咖啡。
  _effects: cash -8 (住院押金和住院用品（其中8克朗记入债务）); health 0.06 (父亲住进医院得到治疗); mood -0.01000000000000012_

- **1935.01** `state_tick:pc20-second-chance/kept-word/small-relapse` tags: pc20-second-chance, second_chance, reconciliation, return, failure, trust, debt, hope
  Anton晚了半个钟头，进门时先解释路上的电车，又解释口袋里的收据。对方没有追问，只把已经凉掉的汤端回厨房；Anton听见锅盖碰在灶台上，知道这次不是天气的问题。
  _effects: state_parameter pc20-second-chance.credibility 55 -> 48; state_parameter pc20-second-chance.suspicion 30 -> 38; state_parameter pc20-second-chance.debt 40 -> 43_

  _effects: state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.attention 35 -> 29; state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.pain 20 -> 16; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1935.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.last-standing-ticket/p9-social-daily-culture.last-standing-ticket.got-ticket` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:culture
  Anton挤到窗口前，把一克朗硬币推进凹槽，换回一张印着座号的薄纸片。顶层站席的栏杆被前面的人汗湿，Anton侧身找了个能看见舞台边的位置，倚着烫手的铁栏看完两幕；散场时小腿发麻，嘴里还哼着刚听到的曲调，幕间也和邻座搭上了两句话。
  _effects: cash -1 (约瑟夫施塔特剧院顶层站票); mood 0.06000000000000005; skill_

- **1935.03** `vienna-market-debt.rent-arrears/rent-sell-furniture-move` tags: market, debt, money, home, shop
  邻居用麻绳把锅和铺盖捆在一起，打了个死结。他说城南有更便宜的屋子，但离厂远。你蹲在地上把最后几件衣服塞进布袋，听见看门人在楼下把垃圾桶挪来挪去。
  _effects: cash 2.8 (卖旧家具所得); relation acquaintance with person-marie-huber_

  _effects: food; flag +ration_card_issued; knowledge “the monthly ration is smaller than last year’s”_

- **1937.06** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.stopped-clock/stopped-clock.wire-fix` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, clock, time
  Anton把钟背板卸下来，发现一根发条断了。她从缝纫盒里找出一截细铁丝，把断头缠住，钟摆晃了几下又走了起来。走是走，每天慢一刻钟，她索性把指针往前拨了拨，凑合着看。
  _effects: skill; mood 0.040000000000000036_

  队伍在公路边停下，Anton Stein在田埂上支了一口锅，一勺开水两分钱。Anton替Hermann Kral买了一勺，Anton Stein又多舀了半勺，说“老人不算钱”。Luise Hofer 3在桥头喊队伍继续走，Hermann Kral把搪瓷杯揣进怀里，水洒了一路。
  _effects: cash -2 (公路边的开水锅（其中2克朗记入债务）); food; flag +bought_hot_water_on_the_road; relation friend with person-hermann-kral; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-95_

  _effects: role; cash -3 (new permits and stamps（其中3克朗记入债务）); condition; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-72_

  Anton傍晚从面粉厂下工回来，发现挂在腰带上的房门钥匙不见了，在楼梯间来回找了四趟。Franz Weiss在二楼拐角的煤堆旁捡到那把铜钥匙，钥匙上缠着红线，一眼就认出来。Franz Weiss敲开Anton的门，把钥匙拍在桌上，说自己眼神好使。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-65; mood 0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-65_

  夜里十一点，母狮不肯碰桶里的马肉。Anton举着煤油灯绕到兽栏后面，看见它右槽牙断了一截，牙龈肿得发亮。Anton把肉切碎，又去敲Grete Moser 2的门；第二天兽医来了，Mitzi Hofer 2在晨会上第一次记住Anton的名字。

  夜班结束前，Anton听见地下室传来拖拽声。下去时，门开着，地上只有一道湿拖把的痕迹；第二天，所有人都说那间屋子锁了十年。

  _effects: role; move place-wien-landwehr-barracks; cash 6 (family allowance)_

  Anton和Hermann Weiss 2谈妥只出租白天，夜里不留宿，收两克朗。床仍归Anton家，钥匙只在白天给。Hermann Weiss 2说不碍事，他住的工棚不许带人，白天有个落脚的地方就够。Rosa Lechner 2听了点头，说这样少些闲话；到了傍晚，Anton把床铺重新收好，屋里照旧几口人。
  _effects: cash 2 (白天出租床位的钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-92; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-92_

- **1914.08** `statistical_recovery`
  Hedwig熬过了这场肺炎，能坐起来喝一点汤，但身体还很虚。

- **1917.01** `vienna-childhood-street.hoop/hoop-win` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  巷子窄，两个孩子并排推着铁环，铁环撞在一起叮当作响。Hedwig的钩子先绕过最后一块界石。Hermann Berg不服，说再比一次，手却已经伸进口袋，摸出三颗彩色弹珠。Hedwig把弹珠举到眼前看，Hermann Berg在旁边踢墙，踢得鞋尖发白。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19; mood 0.05; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-19_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-sold-out-potatoes` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  “卖完了。”三个字从窗口里传出来，Hedwig后面的队伍立刻散了一半。Hedwig没走，趴在窗台上看伙计扫面屑，直到人家挥手赶人。旁边摊子的土豆带着泥，Hedwig用围裙兜住，一枚一枚数铜板，回家报账时连找零都说清了。
  _effects: food; mood -0.03; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-22_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-dragged-docked-pay` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  “你看看这角。”Josef Bauer把床单拎起来，煤渣簌簌往下掉。Hedwig伸手去拍，反而把灰按进布纹里。Josef Bauer从工钱里数回两枚，只留一赫勒在Hedwig手心，让重洗那角。晚饭前Hedwig一直蹲在井台边，换了两盆水，指甲缝里全是黑泥。
  _effects: cash 0.01 (被扣后只剩一赫勒); mood -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-josef-bauer; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-27_

- **1919.02** `p9-childhood-household.strap-by-the-door/took-punishment` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors, injury
  皮带落在掌心时，Hedwig的手缩了一下，又伸回去。挨完几下，Hedwig去墙角拿扫帚，把碎碗片扫进簸箕，瓷片碰着铁皮响。Josefine Hofer把皮带挂回门后，坐在桌边点了半支烟，烟灰积了很长也没弹。
  _effects: health -0.02999999999999997 (挨了几下皮带); mood -0.05; relation kin with person-josefine-hofer_

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-sold-out-potatoes` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  “卖完了。”三个字从窗口里传出来，Hedwig后面的队伍立刻散了一半。Hedwig没走，趴在窗台上看伙计扫面屑，直到人家挥手赶人。旁边摊子的土豆带着泥，Hedwig用围裙兜住，一枚一枚数铜板，回家报账时连找零都说清了。
  _effects: food; mood -0.03; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3_

- **1920.12** `vienna-childhood-friends.courtyard-race/fell-on-wet-stones` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  Hedwig想从床单底下钻过去，脚下一滑，膝盖磕在石板棱上。Franz Moser听见哭声出来，先看了看伤口，又回去端水。手帕扎得很紧，Hedwig试着站起来，被Franz Moser按回台阶上。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5_

- **1921.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-dragged-docked-pay` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  “你看看这角。”Karl Kraus把床单拎起来，煤渣簌簌往下掉。Hedwig伸手去拍，反而把灰按进布纹里。Karl Kraus从工钱里数回两枚，只留一赫勒在Hedwig手心，让重洗那角。晚饭前Hedwig一直蹲在井台边，换了两盆水，指甲缝里全是黑泥。

- **1922.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-both-families` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  队伍拐弯时，Luise Kral朝Hedwig招手。Hedwig挤过去，替两家把面包领出来，纸包用不同的绳结系住。家里人晚上解开绳结，先掂了掂份量，再问价钱；Hedwig把每一包的面包数、找零和欠的铜板都说了一遍，家里人听完才把两包分别收进柜子。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-luise-kral; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2_

- **1922.09** `vienna-childhood-street.market-errand/errand-bullied` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  市场拱廊下晒不到太阳，Hedwig却被堵出一头汗。带子越挣越紧，肩膀在柱子上磕了一下。Josef从人群里挤过来，先看了看Hedwig的肩，又看了看柱子上的结，拿出小刀割断。两个大孩子骂了一句，Josef把Hedwig拉到菜篮后面。
  _effects: relation friend with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.05000000000000002; condition_

- **1923.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-three-heller` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  床单浸了碱水，沉得拽不动。Hedwig站在木凳上，把湿布一点点递过绳，水顺着肘弯滴进袖口。Rosa Kral在绳头数夹子，最后一条挂好，从围裙口袋里摸出三枚赫勒，按进Hedwig掌心。晚上洗手时，虎口那道口子被水一蜇，Hedwig才看见。
  _effects: cash 0.03 (帮邻居晾床单挣的三赫勒); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-20; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-20_

- **1924.09** `p9-childhood-adolescence.church-club/club-one-visit-only` tags: adolescence, friendship, identity, work, school, secret, church
  Hedwig去了一个下午，看那些旧歌本里夹着褪色的圣像卡，工厂来的几个年轻人在最后一排用手指敲长凳打拍子。第二个星期天Hedwig没再去，在院子里帮母亲晾被单。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-mitzi-lechner; mood 0.010000000000000009; skill_

- **1925.01** `r15-work.master-takes-on::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/signed-contract` tags: work, craft, apprentice, trade, poverty, reform, medical, schooling
  Franz Lechner 2把学徒契约摊在桌上，指给Hedwig看期限、工钱和出师考试。Hedwig签了名，第二天站到刨床前，手指上多了第一道木刺。
  _effects: relation employer with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18; state_add apprenticeship-contract state:world-vienna-1912:80; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18; education apprenticeship_

- **1925.01** `r13-edu.exam-eve::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/passed-entrance-exam` tags: education, exam, study, poverty, adolescence, reform, medical, schooling
  Hedwig把煤油灯芯剪短，省下油熬到后半夜。代数题做错三遍，Hedwig就用冷水拍脸，再把公式抄到手腕内侧。考试那天，监考把袖口翻了一遍，Hedwig还是把最后一道题解完了。放榜那天，Hedwig在人群后面站了很久，才敢往前挤。
  _effects: mood 0.06; flag +passed_entrance_exam; skill_

- **1925.08** `vienna-adolescence.school-or-brickworks/go-to-brickworks` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  Friedrich Hofer把算术本合上，说家里等钱，下周一去砖厂。Hedwig没争。放学时Mitzi Weiss在门口等着，塞来一张纸条，上面抄着六区夜校的地址。Hedwig把纸条折小，塞进鞋垫。砖厂第一周结四克朗，Hedwig搬砖搬到手指发抖，晚上脱鞋，袜底全是红砖末。
  _effects: role; cash 4 ({subject}砖厂首周工钱); flag +has-evening-school-address; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-29; relation kin with person-friedrich-hofer; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-29_

- **1925.12** `r13-turn.exam-failure::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::reform-open-secondary/name-missing` tags: education, exam, failure, school, work, reform, medical, schooling
  补考那天，Hedwig在考场门口等到钟响，监考说名单上没有名字。Grete Stein跑去教务处，回来时手里只有一张转学建议。Hedwig去了印刷厂当学徒，师傅说铅字比字母好认，至少不会骗人。
  _effects: condition; flag +left_school_after_exam_failure; role; relation colleague with person-grete-stein; education apprenticeship_

  Hedwig把六枚克朗按在登记簿旁，Maria Moser抬起眼镜看了看他的出生日期，在夜班名单上写下名字。他领到一张油印课程表，纸角还湿着。
  _effects: cash -6 (职业夜校第一期学费); education apprenticeship; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45_

  普拉特的木滑道在闭园后还亮着一盏值班灯。Hedwig和Josef从围栏底下钻进去，用一块麻袋垫着滑下来，木槽里的蜡味呛得人直笑。滑到底时，Anna Huber提着灯站在出口，问他们要不要再买一张票。
  _effects: mood 0.05; cash -1 (a late ticket paid to the watchman); reputation -0.01; relation friend with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912; flag +yf18_prater_stunt; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-53_

- **1926.09** `vienna-youth-threshold.night-class/give-up-for-overtime` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, transition
  工头说这个月赶印一批账簿，晚上加班每小时多给二十赫勒。Hedwig站在教室门口听完课程表，转身回厂里搬纸。Ferdinand Lechner 2追出来说座位可以保留两周，Hedwig点点头，手已经摸上了作坊的门把，夜里机器的声音把课本的事盖了过去。
  _effects: cash 1.6 (夜间加班多挣的工钱); skill; mood -0.039999999999999994; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-40_

- **1926.10** `yf18-firstlook.tram-glove::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/let-friend-return-it` tags: first_love, love, tram, glove, youth, courtship, reform, medical
  Hedwig把手套交给Elisabeth Bauer，让Elisabeth Bauer替自己去还。Elisabeth Bauer回来时说，Lena问是谁捡到的；Elisabeth Bauer顺口报了一个名字，Hedwig听见那名字不是自己的，笑得比平时响。
  _effects: state_add yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:125; relation friend with person-elisabeth-bauer; mood -0.010000000000000009; flag +yf18_first_awakening_active_

  Elisabeth Bauer在第二支曲子前把Rosa请走，说Hedwig答应过要替她看管外套。Hedwig抱着一件不属于自己的大衣站在圣像下面，看他们在蜡烛光里转圈；回家路上，Hedwig把舞卡撕成小片，扔进多瑙运河。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 35 -> 40; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 10 -> 2; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 10 -> 15; relation friend with person-elisabeth-bauer; relation friend with person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.04000000000000001_

  _effects: mood -0.06; reputation 0.02; condition; flag +reported_blocked_fire_exit; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31; knowledge “the manager moved the fire hose back behind the screen”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-32; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31_

  Hedwig请楼下的Maria Kral白天来照看Friedrich Hofer，先付一周六克朗工钱，讲好每天另管两顿饭，煤钱也从这六克里出。Maria Kral上午换床单，下午喂药，临走把便盆提到楼道口。Hedwig下工回来先摸煤箱，再在练习本上记下当天的饭钱和煤钱，看见Friedrich Hofer的嘴唇不再干裂。
  _effects: cash -6 (支付邻居照看工钱和煤钱（其中4克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-maria-kral; health 0.04000000000000001 (白天有人按时喂药和换洗)_

  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-40; reputation 0.04; skill_

- **1932.10** `df18-dream.water-in-the-cellar::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/move-the-crates` tags: dream, omen, home, water, coincidence, eerie, everyday, reform
  梦里，Hedwig看见地窖的水漫过脚踝，木箱一只只浮起来。第二天一早，Hedwig把箱子和煤筐搬到床上。中午，楼下的水管裂了，水沿着墙根流进地窖，只浸湿了一只空桶。Elisabeth Bauer说Hedwig运气好，Hedwig没有解释。
  _effects: cash -1 (搬箱子时打碎了一只罐子); relation acquaintance with person-elisabeth-bauer; knowledge “我梦见地窖进水，第二天水管真的裂了。”; flag +df18_moved_crates_before_flood; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Gustav Berg把汇款单退回来，说收款村的地址在新区划里改了名，钱只能退回。Hedwig又填了一张，把旧村名和新村名并排写上。Gustav Berg说这样不行，系统只认一个名字。汇款单在柜台上放了一下午，最后被盖上“无法投递”。
  _effects: cash -1 (汇款单背面的三行字); mood -0.09999999999999998; condition; flag +remittance_address_failed; knowledge “the post office accepts only one official village name”_

- **1935.10** `r13-turn.addiction-recovery::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/stayed-sober` tags: health, addiction, recovery, support, family, vienna, urban, crisis
  Grete Berg每周三晚上来接Hedwig，两个人走四十分钟去教会的地下室。第十周，Hedwig第一次在会上开口，说自己昨晚路过酒馆没有停。散会时，家里人在门口等着，手里提着一壶热茶。Hedwig接过茶，手没有抖。
  _effects: cash -2 (戒酒会上的空椅子); health 0.1 (戒酒会上的空椅子); flag +sober_after_treatment; role; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-6_

  名单在委员会卡住了。Katharina Berg的名字被划掉，理由是“没有相关资历”；Hedwig没有争，只把名单收回公文包。晚上，Katharina Berg来问结果，Hedwig说再等一年；Katharina Berg没有坐，站了一会儿就走了。
  _effects: reputation -0.01999999999999999; mood -0.030000000000000027; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000453:child; flag +appointment_list_rejected; knowledge “委员会以“没有相关资历”为由划掉了联络员的名字。”_

  _effects: historical development:initiative-org-sdap-1933 as participant; knowledge “我参加了Sozialdemokratische Arbeiterpartei Österreichs (SD。”; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  主教下了禁令，两篇布道都不许再念。Hedwig把抄本带到工会的读书会，在煤油灯下读给二十个人听。读到“借钱给穷人不可取利”时，Karl Kraus在门口咳了一声，没有人回头。
  _effects: reputation 0.04000000000000001; flag +read_banned_sermon_at_union_hall; role; relation colleague with person-otto-winter; knowledge “the bishop forbade both sermons on usury”_

- **1936.04** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.sunday-walk/part-at-arch` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family
  走到桥洞前，Otto Moser说该往回走了，家里炉子还没生。Hedwig站在原地看着Otto Moser拐上台阶，围巾的一角被风掀起来；下周的事谁也没有提，两人就此分开，口袋里只剩一颗凉透的栗子。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-61; flag +uncertain_courtship; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-61_

  _effects: mood -0.16000000000000003; condition; flag +return_refused_party_card; relation acquaintance with person-karl-kraus; knowledge “an old party card blocks the return visa”_

- **1937.03** `ld18-intimacy.hairwash::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-water-goes-cold` tags: love, intimacy, gentle, everyday, queer, body, secret_intimacy, vienna
  Rosa坐在床边，Hedwig用搪瓷盆替她洗头。水凉了又添，肥皂泡顺着耳后流下来；Rosa闭着眼说，小时候她母亲也是这样替她洗的。窗外有人收煤，铁锹刮过石板，两个人谁也没有动。
  _effects: relation friend with person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.devotion 62 -> 67; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 37 -> 34; mood 0.040000000000000036_

- **1937.08** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_scald_hand` tags: home, social, family, housing

  _effects: relation friend with person-mitzi-lechner_

  _effects: cash -20 (贴了封条的钢琴); mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +piano_listed_as_worthless; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-22; knowledge “a bribe can change an inventory entry to “no residual value””_

  Mitzi Lechner嫌Hedwig的床单滴水滴到Mitzi Lechner家衬衫上，一把扯下床单扔在院子的石板上。Hedwig下楼捡起来时，床单角沾了泥，重洗又要费肥皂。两人在院子当中对喊，楼上有人推开窗看热闹。Hedwig把床单重新搭上绳子时，故意把水拧在Mitzi Lechner那侧。

  _effects: mood -0.04999999999999993; condition; flag +parent_refused_reconciliation; relation rival with person-friedrich-hofer_

- **1938.05** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_stolen_by_stranger` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Hedwig记得出门时钥匙还在，回来却没了。Maria Kral说下午看见一个穿灰外套的男人在楼道里转悠，手里晃着一把铜钥匙。两人下楼查看，房门虚掩，抽屉被拉开，攒着买煤的六克朗不见了。Maria Kral陪着Hedwig去街角警察岗亭报了案，警察记下灰外套，说会留意。
  _effects: cash -6 (被盗的买煤钱); relation friend with person-maria-kral; mood -0.09999999999999998_

  _effects: cash 0.6 (争执后按降低的工价结算); reputation 0.03; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; mood -0.030000000000000027_

  介绍所的门关到一半，里面还在喊下一位。Hedwig和Helene 2站在廊下，听着货栈、面粉厂、马厩各处的消息，最后只记住北站那条。烟盒纸塞进上衣口袋，走路时窸窸窣窣响。
  _effects: flag +has_work_lead; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001852:child_

- **1941.01** `state_expire:ld18-impossible-attachment` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  Hedwig的「无法登记的伴侣」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove ld18-impossible-attachment state:world-vienna-1912:219; flag -impossible_attachment_active_

- **1941.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/learns` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Hedwig把Berta 4的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Berta 4今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Hedwig听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 23 -> 31; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 45 -> 42; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1941.01** `r13-fam.take-over-family-shop::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::shop-crisis/shop-kept-open` tags: task, commerce, shop, family_firm, succession, debt, vienna, urban
  街上的人都在排队领救济，铺子里一天没有进来一个客人。Hedwig把面粉分成小包，赊给楼里的邻居，账记在门板背面。Friedrich Hofer说这样下去撑不过冬天；Hedwig把最后一袋盐倒进罐子，说撑一天算一天。
  _effects: cash -8 (把家里的招牌重新钉上（其中8克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +kept_shop_open_during_crisis; role; relation kin with person-friedrich-hofer; knowledge “the neighbours owe the shop more than they can pay”; employment Kaufmann @ cinema:person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-22-evt:world-vienna-1912:002325_

- **1941.07** `mb17-border.permit-window::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/admitted-with-papers` tags: migration, border, papers, permit, station, official, belonging, vienna
  火车在清晨进站，Hermann Berg 2把Hedwig的护照翻到最后一页，又看了看那张没有照片的出生纸。蓝章落下去，手续费和住址登记表一起被推到窗口；Hedwig把临时居留纸折成四折，塞进内衣口袋，走出站门时天还没有完全亮。
  _effects: cash -2 (the passport fee and the first week's rent（其中2克朗记入债务）); state_add mb17-newcomer state:world-vienna-1912:625; state_add mb17-remittance-web state:world-vienna-1912:626; role; flag +mb17_admitted_at_border; mood 0.030000000000000027; knowledge “临时居留纸要求在四十八小时内到区公所登记住址。”; flag +mb17_newcomer_active; flag +mb17_remittance_web_active_

- **1942.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/difficult-birth` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, state, state:raising-child, raising-child
  Hedwig难产，接生的人守了两天。孩子最后生下来，取名Elisabeth 4；Hedwig一直发烧，医院又收走二十克朗，Ferdinand Lechner 4把钟表送进当铺。Hedwig落下产后虚弱的毛病，抱孩子时手臂发抖，站久了眼前就发黑。
  _effects: birth female; condition; cash -20 (midwife and hospital（其中20克朗记入债务）); state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:671_

- **1942.01** `curse15-betrayal.signature::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-files-went-first` tags: betrayal, office, power, politics, curse, institution, vienna, urban
  Otto Winter先把Hedwig抽屉里的往来信件搬空，再在会议上说Hedwig从来没有保存过记录。没有人能证明那些信存在过，除了Hedwig自己。散会后，有人让Hedwig回家休息几天，工资照发。Hedwig知道这不是照顾，是让Hedwig别再回来。
  _effects: reputation -0.06000000000000001; relation rival with person-otto-winter; knowledge “我经手的信件在会议前被人搬空了。”; condition; flag +records_removed_

- **1942.01** `state_reaction:suspected-witchcraft/another-name` tags: suspected-witchcraft, witchcraft, rumor, religion, legal, reputation, fear, supernatural
  Otto Winter在指控Hedwig的人面前添了一句话，说Hedwig曾经在夜里念过谁的名字。那句话没有证据，但登记册上又多了一行。Hedwig后来才知道，添这句话的人就是Otto Winter。
  _effects: state_parameter suspected-witchcraft.evidence 20 -> 30; reputation -0.03; relation rival with person-otto-winter_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:011100_

- **1942.01** `state_reaction:cursed-by-wish/curse-betrayal` tags: cursed-by-wish, wish, curse, supernatural, revenge, special, state, state_reaction
  背叛发生后的夜里，Hedwig听见墙里有一声很轻的响。第二天，Otto Winter病倒了；没有人把这两件事放在一起。
  _effects: health -0.05 (the curse answers a real betrayal); condition; state_parameter cursed-by-wish.triggers 2 -> 3; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:011100_

- **1942.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/struggles` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Hedwig被叫到学校，老师说Maria 4这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Hedwig没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 28 -> 23; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 42 -> 47; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils（其中2克朗记入债务）); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1942.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/struggles` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Hedwig被叫到学校，老师说Luise 4这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Hedwig没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 20 -> 15; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 45 -> 50; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils（其中2克朗记入债务）); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1942.01** `state_tick:suspected-witchcraft/rumor-season/new-accuser` tags: suspected-witchcraft, witchcraft, rumor, religion, legal, reputation, fear, supernatural
  又一个人站了出来，说自家的母牛在Hedwig经过之后不再产奶。Hedwig没有辩解，因为辩解本身也需要被登记。Hedwig开始绕远路去市场，每月多出几枚铜币的车钱。
  _effects: state_parameter suspected-witchcraft.danger 25 -> 35; reputation -0.04000000000000001; cash -1 (绕远路增加的车钱（其中1克朗记入债务）); mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1942.01** `state_tick:mb17-newcomer/first-year/puts-down-roots` tags: mb17-newcomer, migration, newcomer, language, network, housing, belonging, state
  一年过去，Hedwig能在菜市场用德语讲价，也知道哪家铺子肯赊半块黄油。房东仍叫他“新来的”，但楼下的面包师已经记住了他买哪一种面包，还会把前一天剩的那只小圆面包留出来。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-newcomer.language 18 -> 22; state_parameter mb17-newcomer.localNetwork 6 -> 11; state_parameter mb17-newcomer.housingStrain 65 -> 63; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1942.01** `state_tick:mb17-remittance-web/remittance-year/month-behind` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  这个月厂里少发了工钱。Hedwig把汇款单填到一半又放下，先给房东送去一部分；夜里他给故乡写信，说“下个月一定补上”，写完把信纸折了三折，像怕它自己会开口。
  _effects: cash -1 (the rent was paid first（其中1克朗记入债务）); state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 2 -> 3; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.obligation 55 -> 59; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1942.05** `state_reaction:mb17-remittance-web/border-closes` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  边境又收紧了，往故乡的列车要换乘三次。邮局说汇款还能走，但信可能压在路上；Hedwig还是把两张单子一起递进窗口，像把一句话交给一个不保证送达的人。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 0 -> 2; mood -0.040000000000000036; flag +mb17_border_slow_letters_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:010898_

- **1942.05** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-neighbors-together` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Hedwig把Berta Weiss 2和楼里另两户人家叫到楼道，把那张纸摆在窗台上传看。看门人在楼梯拐角听着，说房东礼拜三来收钱。几家人合计好，到那天一起等在门厅，先把克朗递过去，再把修补的事一件件摆出来。Hedwig把纸折进围裙口袋，觉得人凑齐了话也硬气些。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-26; mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +tenant_collective_

- **1942.12** `vienna-legacy-worklife.union-office/union-office.write-off` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  账本的纸边已经发毛，Hedwig翻到那一页时，手指沾上一层灰。有人说“三十四克朗够买两袋面粉”，没人接话。最后举手表决，赞成销账的手比反对的多两只，老成员把手放下去时，椅子又晃了一下。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81; flag +union_debt_written_off; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81_

- **1943.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Hedwig在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Hedwig把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.05 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages（其中8克朗记入债务）)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:011102_

  故乡来了一封电报，说屋顶塌了一角，老人暂时住到邻居家。Hedwig当晚就去邮局，把本来留着交学费的钱先汇了出去；回家后，他把墙上的缴费单取下来，折好放进抽屉。

  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 28 -> 31; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Elisabeth Weiss把Hedwig的话记成了另一层意思。报纸出来时，标题写着“她说她从不后悔”；Hedwig记得自己说的是“后悔也没有用”。Hedwig把报纸折起来，又打开，最后还是把它塞进了炉子。

  边境又收紧了，往故乡的列车要换乘三次。邮局说汇款还能走，但信可能压在路上；Hedwig还是把两张单子一起递进窗口，像把一句话交给一个不保证送达的人。

- **1945.01** `statistical_death/violence` tags: death
  Hedwig在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang卷入一场冲突，头部和胸口受了重伤。旁人把人抬回家，夜里伤势恶化。Ferdinand Lechner 4在场。
  _effects: death (暴力); flag -mb17_newcomer_active; flag -protected_scandal_active_

### 415 subject events

- **1913.01** `r13-edu.school-roll-call::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-school/attends-through-frost` tags: education, school, attendance, childhood, poverty, vienna, urban, peace
  Mitzi Lechner踩着结冰的台阶进教室，第三个空位还空着。Grete Stein让Mitzi Lechner把昨天的课文背完，又用戒尺敲了敲黑板，叫全班跟着念。放学时名字被写在红榜末尾，Mitzi Lechner把冻僵的手指揣进袖口，觉得明天还能来。放学路上，Mitzi Lechner把冻裂的手贴在热煤渣上。
  _effects: mood 0.03; flag +school_attendance_streak; skill_

- **1913.04** `vienna-childhood-street.market-errand/errand-lost-coin` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  菜摊前的地面是湿的。Mitzi Lechner弯腰捡土豆时，一枚铜板从指缝里滑出去，顺着水沟格栅掉了下去。Mitzi Lechner把剩下的钱摊在柜台上，让摊主帮忙数，还是少了三十克鲁泽。晚上Rosa Lechner把钱一枚枚排开，问了三遍怎么少的，Mitzi Lechner每答一次，声音就低一点。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (铜板掉进下水道); relation kin with person-rosa-lechner; mood -0.06_

- **1913.04** `r1-child.father-scaffold::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-tenement/called-down-for-a-wrench` tags: bereavement, work_accident, father, poverty, school, vienna, urban, peace
  父亲那天被叫下去找一把丢了的扳手，回来时脚手架已经塌了。他在门口站了很久，鞋上全是灰。Alois Lechner问他怎么了，他说老约瑟夫掉下去了。那晚Mitzi Lechner第一次看见父亲把汤勺拿反。
  _effects: cash 2 (脚手架下面的一只鞋); mood -0.08000000000000002; flag +father_missed_scaffold_collapse; relation friend with person-rosa-lechner; knowledge “the scaffolding collapsed minutes after the father left it”_

- **1913.10** `iw18-secret.cellar-door::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-school/the-door-was-just-coal` tags: childhood, secret, family, cellar, knowledge, fear, vienna, urban
  地窖里只有煤、旧箱子和一只缺腿的凳子，Mitzi Lechner转了一圈，什么秘密也没找到。上楼时Rosa Lechner正在切洋葱，问孩子下去做什么；Mitzi Lechner说找坛子，Rosa Lechner便让孩子把手洗了来帮忙，案板上的洋葱辣得两人都流泪。
  _effects: mood 0.010000000000000009; food; flag +cellar_was_only_coal_

- **1913.12** `r1-child.lodger-knee::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-tenement/hand-under-the-tablecloth` tags: harassment, lodger, boundaries, mother, housing, vienna, urban, peace
  晚饭时，Hermann Kral的手在桌布下面碰到Mitzi Lechner的膝盖。Mitzi Lechner僵住，把腿挪开，筷子掉在地上。Alois Lechner说吃饭别乱动，Hermann Kral笑着替她捡起筷子。那天夜里，Mitzi Lechner第一次把厨房门闩上。
  _effects: mood -0.12; condition; flag +lodgers_hand_at_the_table; relation rival with person-hermann-kral_

- **1914.01** `iw18-home.ration-tin::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-school/puts-back-the-coin` tags: childhood, family, money, hunger, secret, school, vienna, urban
  Mitzi Lechner已经把硬币攥在手心，走到门口又折回来，把硬币放回铁盒，还照着原来的顺序摆好。Rosa Lechner回来时，Mitzi Lechner正在数面粉袋上的补丁；那天下午孩子没有出门，只把面包铺的香味想了一遍又一遍。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.childConfidence 55 -> 57; mood -0.010000000000000009; flag +put_coin_back_

- **1914.01** `r1-fam.childcare-burden::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-school/childcare.missed-school` tags: family, childcare, childhood, school, exhaustion, vienna, urban, peace
  Rosa Lechner天不亮就去洗衣房，Mitzi Lechner把最小的弟弟绑在背上，给另外三个煮面糊。锅开了，弟弟在背上哭，Mitzi Lechner一边晃一边数着还剩几根柴。学校点名册上，Mitzi Lechner的名字后面画了一排小圈；Anna Moser说再这样下去，老师要上门了。
  _effects: condition; flag +kept_siblings_out_of_school; role; knowledge “学校在点名册上用一个个小圈记旷课。”; education primary_

  Grete Stein看了Mitzi Lechner的手，说指头太短，够不到八度，学下去也是白费。Mitzi Lechner把手收进袖子里，回家后把琴谱还回去，放在琴房的窗台上。那天晚上，Mitzi Lechner在院子里坐了很久，听见隔壁有人在练同一支曲子。
  _effects: mood -0.10000000000000003; flag +rejected_by_music_teacher; relation rival with person-grete-stein; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7_

  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (讲台上的干豌豆); mood -0.1; condition; flag +knelt_on_dried_peas; skill_

- **1914.11** `kernel-child.punishment::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-tenement/edu-punish-neighbor-stops` tags: childhood, family, punishment, neighbor, conflict, vienna, urban, peace
  皮带举起来时，Anna Moser正好来借盐。Anna Moser站在门口没有走，说孩子打碎东西是常事。Rosa Lechner把皮带放下，嘴上说自家孩子自己管，晚上却把Mitzi Lechner的碗添满了。Mitzi Lechner从此见了Anna Moser会低头。那碗汤Mitzi Lechner喝得很慢。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-anna-moser; relation kin with person-rosa-lechner; flag +neighbor_intervened_in_punishment; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1915.01** `vienna-childhood-street.washhouse-fever/fever-recover` tags: childhood, street, social, home, injury
  Mitzi Lechner在洗衣房的长凳上睡着，醒来时喉咙像塞了棉花。Rosa Lechner把最后的干净布巾浸了凉水，敷在Mitzi Lechner额头上，整夜换了七八回。第四天Mitzi Lechner能坐起来喝粥，下嘴唇脱下一小片皮，Rosa Lechner用指尖替Mitzi Lechner拈掉。
  _effects: health -0.09999999999999998 (热病高烧); condition; relation kin with person-rosa-lechner_

- **1915.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.stay-after-school/stay-list-instead` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, teacher, learning
  Grete Stein把教案夹到腋下，走廊尽头有人晃着钥匙串催。Grete Stein从本子背面撕下一页，匆匆写了三道题，墨迹还没干就递过来。Mitzi Lechner把纸对折两次，塞进衣兜，站在校门口看Grete Stein走远，纸角在手心里越捏越软。
  _effects: skill; mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein_

- **1915.07** `p9-childhood-household.strap-by-the-door/strap-raised-then-dropped` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors, injury
  Rosa Lechner的手举起来，又慢慢垂下去。皮带被扔回门后，Mitzi Lechner被留在桌边，把碎瓷片包进旧报纸里。窗外有人在巷子里追跑，鞋底拍在石板上。Mitzi Lechner听着，手下一滑，瓷片在食指上割出一条细线，血珠慢慢冒出来。
  _effects: mood -0.03999999999999998; health -0.010000000000000009 (手指被瓷片划破); relation kin with person-rosa-lechner_

- **1915.10** `vienna-school-childhood.spelling-bee/spelling-bee.fail` tags: school, social, childhood
  Mitzi Lechner拼到一半停住，Grete Stein的尺子落在桌角上。放学后楼梯间只剩Mitzi Lechner一个人，把“Notwendigkeit”抄了二十遍，墨水瓶见了底。
  _effects: mood -0.06; skill; relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein_

- **1915.11** `r1-child.library-card::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school::reading-room-culture/confiscated-in-class` tags: library, reading, books, poverty, school, university, culture, schooling
  Mitzi Lechner把书藏在课桌里，被Otto Bauer没收。Otto Bauer翻了两页，发现是讲蒸汽机的，放学后把书还回来，说下次别在课上读。Mitzi Lechner问能不能借老师的书，Otto Bauer说先把这本读完。
  _effects: flag +teacher_lent_book; skill; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10; knowledge “the teacher keeps a shelf of engineering books”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10_

- **1916.01** `r13-edu.ask-teacher::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/note-under-inkwell` tags: education, teacher, help, school, confidence, university, culture, schooling
  Mitzi Lechner等到天黑，Grete Stein一直在改别的班的卷子，头也没抬。Mitzi Lechner把问题写在纸条上压在墨水瓶下，第二天发现纸条还在原处，只是背面多了一行字：“先看第三章。”Mitzi Lechner按纸条翻到第三章，发现那页夹着一张旧的辅助线图，图上的线画得很轻。
  _effects: skill_

- **1917.01** `major-education-teacher-recommendation/appeal-granted` tags: education, scholarship, appeal, major
  Josef Hofer把三页申诉信送到学校董事会，附上Mitzi Lechner的作文本和医生开的病假条。Gustav Berg起初说名额已经定了，翻到最后一页才停下来：作文上有Josef Hofer用红笔写的批注。两周后，Mitzi Lechner收到一张免学费通知和二十克朗书费，条件是新学期不得缺课。
  _effects: education secondary; cash 20 (book grant after the appeal); role; flag +appeal_granted; reputation 0.06; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14_

  Mitzi Lechner把老师讲的例题步骤全背下来，却没弄懂为什么移项。卷子发下来，第一道大题就卡住，Mitzi Lechner在草稿纸上画满小人，交卷前只写出一个答案。回家后Mitzi Lechner把那张卷子折成纸飞机，扔出窗外，又下楼捡了回来。纸飞机摔在煤堆上。
  _effects: mood -0.07999999999999996; skill_

  面试官问Mitzi Lechner最近读的一本书。Mitzi Lechner说的是从旧书摊上买来的植物图鉴，还背出了拉丁名。Friedrich Hofer在表格上写了“给予”，Grete Stein在门外听见，先把眼泪擦掉才进来。
  _effects: reputation 0.06; flag +won_scholarship_interview; relation colleague with person-grete-stein; education secondary_

  _effects: state_add scholarship-holder state:world-vienna-1912:19; cash -15 (family pays remainder)_

  _effects: cash -1 (落榜名单上的第三个名字); mood -0.15000000000000002; condition; flag +failed_entrance_exam; skill; relation guardian with person-rosa-lechner_

- **1918.01** `r13-edu.school-transfer::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/acquaintance-in-office` tags: education, school, transfer, bureaucracy, family, university, culture, schooling
  Mitzi Lechner的邻居在市政厅当差，替Mitzi Lechner把材料直接送到教育科。三天后，转学证寄到家里，上面多了一行手写批注：“准予插入三年级。”Mitzi Lechner把证翻来覆去看，发现自己的名字被写错了一个字母。名字写错的事Mitzi Lechner没去改，反正学籍号是对的，老师点名时也这么叫。
  _effects: flag +school_transfer_approved; knowledge “the school transfer certificate misspells the name”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-22_

- **1918.04** `recipe-education.scholarship-exam::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/cheated` tags: education, exam, class, poverty, ambition, university, culture, schooling
  最后一题，Mitzi Lechner把邻桌的答案抄在掌心。Alois Lechner 2收卷时多看了一眼那只手，什么也没说。放榜时Mitzi Lechner过了，但Alois Lechner 2把Mitzi Lechner叫到走廊，让她把掌心的字洗掉再进教室。
  _effects: reputation -0.08; flag +cheated_scholarship_exam; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-15; education secondary; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-15_

- **1918.05** `r3-teen.vocational-school::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/commercial-track` tags: education, vocational, class, craft, ambition, university, culture, schooling
  算盘和复写纸把Mitzi Lechner留在教室里。Grete Stein让全班抄一份提货单，Mitzi Lechner把数字写得比字还工整。放学时，Katharina Kral说银行招练习生只看字，不看力气。回家后，他把提货单上的数字又抄了一遍，煤油灯把纸边熏黄。
  _effects: flag +assigned_to_commercial_track; role; skill; skill; education secondary; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23_

- **1918.08** `r1-child.scholarship-vetting::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-university/mended-cuffs-at-interview` tags: scholarship, exam, politics, class, school, university, culture, schooling
  面试前一天，Mitzi Lechner把父亲的旧外套改小，袖口用黑线缝了两道。Elisabeth Bauer 2看见袖口的针脚，问是谁缝的。Mitzi Lechner说父亲。Elisabeth Bauer 2在表格上写了“家境清寒”，又用红笔圈了一下。
  _effects: flag +scholarship_noted_poverty; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-21; education secondary; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-21_

- **1918.09** `vienna-adolescence.stolen-roll/caught-and-returned` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  面包房后门的筐里堆着隔夜面包。Mitzi Lechner拿起半个黑面包，刚要往怀里塞，Josef Bauer从晾衣绳后面绕出来，手一伸，掌心朝上。Mitzi Lechner把面包放回去，Josef Bauer用指节在Mitzi Lechner手背上敲了两下，声音不大，巷口的人都听见了。
  _effects: reputation -0.06; relation acquaintance with person-josef-bauer; mood -0.09999999999999998_

- **1919.01** `state_tick:night-school/annual/slipped` tags: night-school, education, night_school, work, state, state_tick
  Mitzi Lechner在这一年失去了一点余地；夜校的报名费和工厂的班次第一次正面撞上。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; state_parameter night-school.progress 0 -> 0; state_parameter night-school.tuition_arrears 0 -> 35_

- **1919.01** `r13-edu.library-study::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-university/no-deposit-borrowing` tags: education, library, study, reading, poverty, university, culture, schooling
  Mitzi Lechner付不起借书押金，只能站在书架间读。Mitzi Hofer 2第三次转过来时，把一本缺封面的旧书塞给Mitzi Lechner，说算馆里报废的。Mitzi Lechner把书藏在柴堆底下，每晚读两页。那本书的扉页写着一个陌生的名字，Mitzi Lechner在下面添上自己的，又觉得不妥，用橡皮擦淡了。
  _effects: flag +borrowed_discarded_book; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-22_

- **1919.02** `r3-crime.juvenile-court::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/sent-to-reform-school` tags: crime, youth, court, family, school, university, culture, schooling
  Gustav Berg说家里管不了，不如让学校管。Rosa Lechner想说什么，被Mitzi Hofer 2递过来的一张表格挡住了。Mitzi Lechner被带走时，Rosa Lechner追到走廊尽头，把一包糖塞进Mitzi Lechner的口袋。Mitzi Hofer 2说“到了那边会检查”，又把糖拿走了。
  _effects: condition; flag +sent_to_reform_school; role; relation rival with person-rosa-lechner; move place-wien-volksschule-leopoldstadt; education apprenticeship_

- **1919.03** `r3-teen.night-school-return::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/re-enrolled` tags: education, night_school, work, literacy, fatigue, university, culture, schooling
  夜校点名时，Grete Stein念到Mitzi Lechner的旧名字，停了一下。Mitzi Lechner举起沾着机油的手，说想接着上。Grete Stein把半截粉笔递过来，让她在黑板上写自己的住址，写错了两个字母。
  _effects: flag +returned_to_night_school; skill; relation colleague with person-grete-stein; education secondary; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-28_

- **1919.10** `r3-teen.first-friend-group::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/admitted` tags: friendship, roof, belonging, betrayal, class, university, culture, schooling
  屋顶上的暗号是两声口哨。Mitzi Lechner吹对了一声，第二声卡在喉咙里，Katharina Kral笑着替她补上。四个人分吃一袋偷来的栗子，壳扔到楼下，砸在Grete Berg的雨棚上。
  _effects: mood 0.07999999999999996; flag +joined_roof_group; role; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-6_

- **1919.10** `r1-child.choir-voice-break::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-university/voice-cracks-at-rehearsal` tags: choir, confirmation, church, voice, adolescence, university, culture, schooling
  复活节前，Grete Stein把Mitzi Lechner单独留下，让唱最高那一句。Mitzi Lechner唱到一半，嗓子像被砂纸蹭过，声音劈开。Grete Stein让再试一次，第二次更低。他把谱子合上，说今年你站第二排。回家后Mitzi Lechner对着水缸练了一个钟头，水面上只有气泡，没有高音。
  _effects: mood -0.07999999999999996; flag +voice_broke; skill_

- **1920.01** `state_expire:night-school` tags: night-school, education, night_school, work, state, state_tick, state:night-school
  Mitzi Lechner的「夜校生」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove night-school state:world-vienna-1912:28_

- **1920.01** `r13-civ.vaccination::ctx-vienna-medical-1930::milieu-school/parent-refused` tags: health, vaccination, school, parent, public_health, medical, university, knowledge
  Rosa Lechner在门口把Mitzi Lechner拉到一边，说邻居家的孩子种痘后发烧三天，不让进。Ferdinand Weiss追出来解释，说发烧是正常的。Rosa Lechner还是把Mitzi Lechner带回了家，卡片留在窗台上，被风吹到地上。
  _effects: flag +vaccination_refused_by_parent; knowledge “the parent refused vaccination after a neighbour’s reaction”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11_

- **1920.02** `r3-teen.dormitory-theft::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/found-thief` tags: education, dormitory, theft, trust, institution, university, culture, schooling
  Mitzi Lechner在柜门合页上抹了一层粉笔灰，第二天看见灰印落在邻床的鞋面上。Grete Stein打开那只箱子，表就压在脏袜子下面。Maria Lechner把欠Mitzi Lechner的半块面包还了回来。
  _effects: flag +found_dormitory_thief; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31; relation colleague with person-grete-stein; knowledge “the watch was hidden in the neighboring bed”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31_

- **1920.05** `r3-teen.boarding-school::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/expelled` tags: education, boarding_school, dormitory, homesickness, class, university, culture, schooling
  舍监从Mitzi Lechner的箱底翻出父亲留下的折刀，说寄宿学校不许带凶器。Mitzi Lechner解释那是削葡萄枝用的，没有人听。第二天，她背着行李站在校门口，Katharina Kral追出来塞给她一块肥皂。
  _effects: mood -0.08999999999999997; flag +expelled_from_boarding_school; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-23; move place-wien-leopoldstadt-tenement; education none; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-29_

- **1920.09** `r1-cul.fan-letter::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/quoted-without-permission` tags: culture, letters, fandom, literacy, ambition, university, schooling, school
  Mitzi Lechner的信寄出半年，没有回音。后来Elisabeth Moser在一篇文章里引用了这封信，没有署名，只写一个读者说。Mitzi Lechner在报上读到自己的句子，先是高兴，随后发现文章里的意思和自己写的相反。
  _effects: reputation 0.03; flag +letter_quoted_without_permission; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24; knowledge “the quoted sentence means the opposite of what was written”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-27_

  亲戚临时反悔，不肯为一个刚离开旧作坊的学徒作保，说自己在厂里还要做人。他站在门口，手里攥着熨平的衬衫袖口，把路上想好的话又咽回去。
  _effects: mood -0.07999999999999996; relation acquaintance with person-friedrich-hofer_

  _effects: flag +returned_to_school_after_dropout; skill; relation colleague with person-grete-stein; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35; education secondary_

  _effects: state_add scholarship-holder state:world-vienna-1912:40_

- **1921.08** `r14-edu.matriculation::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/paid-the-fee` tags: education, university_admission, class_shame, university, culture, schooling, school, teacher
  Elisabeth Bauer 2把注册表推过来，指着学费一栏。Mitzi Lechner数出十克朗，硬币在柜台上排成一行。Elisabeth Bauer 2一枚枚点过，在表格上盖了蓝章。走廊里，Hermann Weiss抱着一摞新书从旁边经过，看了一眼Mitzi Lechner脚上的旧鞋，没有停。
  _effects: cash -10 (注册处窗口的旧外套（其中9克朗记入债务）); flag +university_admitted; education higher_

  Marie Huber把回信写在电车月票背面：星期三，六点，第二站。Mitzi Lechner提前半小时到，站在报亭旁边，把那张月票看了十几遍。Marie Huber从电车上下来时，围巾被风吹到脸上，两个人都笑了。
  _effects: mood 0.12; flag +first_love_reciprocated; relation partner with person-marie-huber; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33_

  _effects: mood -0.07999999999999996; flag +manuscript_rejected; skill; knowledge “the editor thought the characters were made of paper”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-38_

  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5; food; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5_

- **1922.08** `major-crisis.rent-increase/pay-the-increase` tags: crisis, housing, rent, eviction, family, poverty
  房东没有露面，只让看门人Friedrich Hofer把补款单塞进门缝：每月要多十四克朗，先交十八克朗（本月十四加四克朗手续费）才肯谈续租。Mitzi Lechner把准备买冬煤的钱数了两遍，还是把钱交了，收据上写的是“自愿捐助”。Friedrich Hofer低声说，不签的人已经有两家搬走了，楼下的煤价牌又换了数字。

  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036_

- **1926.04** `vienna-parish-aid.sick-call/sick-call.door-only` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Mitzi Lechner把药包和半块肥皂交给开门的孩子，嘱咐用水冲开、一天两次，就下了楼。楼梯间里孩子一直站在门口看着，手里攥着纸包，没关门。Mitzi Lechner走到二层又折回去，帮他把门带上，说自己家里也等着火。
  _effects: health 0.010000000000000002 (没有久留，避开了病人的屋子); relation acquaintance with person-otto-winter; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1926.10** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.family-table/nod-and-pots` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, partnership
  汤喝到一半，Alois Lechner放下勺子点了头，但说得很清楚：先攒出一套锅碗、一只挂钟，再把话往下说。Anna Bauer在桌下用膝盖碰了碰Mitzi Lechner，Alois Lechner把最后一块煮牛肉推过来，饭桌上的空气总算松了；这一顿让对方家里管了饭，自家灶上省下两克朗的吃食，也吃出一个带条件的准信。
  _effects: relation partner with person-anna-bauer; food_

- **1926.11** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/barber-pull` tags: health, medical, family, money
  理发铺里还坐着等刮脸的老主顾，肥皂味和烟味混在一起。弗朗茨把围布往Mitzi Lechner胸前一系，说忍着点。钳子第一次滑脱，第二次才夹稳，牙出来时带着一小块肉，他用明矾水按住了。

  Mitzi Lechner把湿布拧到不再滴水，敷在Friedrich Winter的额头上。孩子烧得说胡话，Mitzi Lechner就一遍遍念窗外的街名，直到天快亮时，孩子的手心终于出了一层汗。Mitzi Lechner把被子掖好，自己靠在床沿睡了过去，醒来时锅里还剩半碗冷粥。

  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018; food; flag +quiet_train; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18_

  Mitzi Lechner把最后一章改了七遍，脚注核对到凌晨。Maria Moser看完只说，可以交了，但答辩时不要替自己辩护太多。Mitzi Lechner把稿子送去打印，回来路上买了一块面包，边走边吃，纸袋上印着油墨的字，被手汗洇开了。打印费三克朗。
  _effects: cash -3 (论文最后一章的脚注（其中3克朗记入债务）); flag +thesis_completed; skill_

  楼梯间的灯泡忽明忽暗，Mitzi Lechner让Friedrich Hofer记下今晚进出院子的人。名单留在桌上没动，像一件没人认领的活。快到午夜，Friedrich Hofer只说了一句，明早再核一遍。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-friedrich-hofer; mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +kept_district_list_private_

  一个多年没走动的亲戚提着一盒点心上门，先问Mitzi Lechner的身体，再问那笔钱是怎么来的。临走时他说起小时候替Mitzi Lechner家垫过一冬天的煤钱，数字记不清了，但意思很清楚。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 1 -> 2; flag +windfall_claim_by_relative; mood -0.020000000000000018_
