  报纸在头版登了Mitzi Lechner和承包商在一张桌子上的照片，配文只有一句“谁签的字”。质询会开了三个小时，Mitzi Lechner把合同、比价单和验收记录一页页摊开；记者第二天没有再写，但把照片留在了档案里。
  _effects: reputation -0.030000000000000027; mood -0.030000000000000027; state_add office-under-siege state:world-vienna-1912:184; flag +scandal_weathered; knowledge “照片是真的，合同也是真的；报纸没有再追，但档案里留了底片。”; flag +office_under_siege_

- **1928.12** `state_reaction:ml18-sudden-fortune/the-papers-hear-about-it` tags: ml18-sudden-fortune, money, windfall, sudden_fortune, household, luck, inheritance, lottery
  报纸的社会版把Mitzi Lechner家的门牌号和那笔钱的数目印在一起，标题是“一笔不该有的好运”。第二天门口多了两个陌生人，一个说是远亲，一个说代表一家孤儿院。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.public false -> true; state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 2 -> 4; reputation -0.020000000000000018; flag +windfall_went_public_

- **1929.01** `r14-edu.defence::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/failed-defence` tags: education, mentorship, thesis, publication, university, culture, schooling, school
  Alois Hofer说论证站不住，Maria Moser当场替Mitzi Lechner辩了十分钟，声音越来越高。最后结果是修改后重新答辩。走出教学楼，Maria Moser说刚才不该替Mitzi Lechner说话，但说完又摆摆手，让Mitzi Lechner回去把第三章重写。重新装订花了两克朗。
  _effects: cash -2 (答辩室窗外的钟声（其中2克朗记入债务）); mood -0.09999999999999998; flag +thesis_rejected; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45_

  Mitzi Lechner在预算表上签了字，把冬季煤房的款项从三笔并成一笔，又给夜班守卫加了半份津贴。Alois Hofer算出省下的钱刚好够修屋顶；没有人鼓掌，但一月的会议室不再需要穿大衣。
  _effects: reputation 0.03999999999999998; skill; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +signed_balanced_budget; knowledge “把冬季煤房的款项并成一笔后，省下的钱够修会议室屋顶。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-49_

- **1929.01** `state_tick:ml18-sudden-fortune/fortune-year/money-keeps-working` tags: ml18-sudden-fortune, money, windfall, sudden_fortune, household, luck, inheritance, lottery
  一年过去，钱没有变多，却也没有一下花完。Mitzi Lechner把一部分存进邮局储蓄所，一部分借给楼下的鞋匠；鞋匠每月送来一双补好的鞋，利息就夹在鞋垫下面。街口的杂货商开始把好面粉留到Mitzi Lechner来的时候。
  _effects: cash 2 (interest and small repayments on the windfall); state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 4 -> 5; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  Mitzi Lechner把答应过的事又做了一遍：按时出现，把欠下的小钱还掉，没有在旧话上添新的解释。对方没有鼓掌，只把杯子往Mitzi Lechner这边推了推；这一次的沉默比上次轻。
  _effects: state_parameter pc20-second-chance.credibility 60 -> 66; state_parameter pc20-second-chance.suspicion 28 -> 23; state_parameter pc20-second-chance.hope 73 -> 77; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1929.01** `agency:support_process` tags: agency, agency:support_process
  Mitzi Lechner想帮忙，但组织者不信任新来的人，只让其在外围等消息。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1929.04** `oj18-cinema.reel::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/burned-frame` tags: cinema, film, projection, night, censorship, work, odd_job, reform
  胶片在片门里起火，火苗顺着观察窗舔进大厅。Mitzi Lechner没有先去拿灭火沙，而是把后排的孩子往侧门推；Luise Huber用湿毯子捂住片盒，手背烧掉一层皮。第二天，Leopoldstädter Volksschule把烧黑的片盒留在柜台上，说“留着，提醒我们”。
  _effects: condition; reputation 0.04999999999999999; state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:215; cash 1 (emergency pay for the fire shift); flag +cinema_fire; knowledge “硝酸片起火时，观察窗像一只被点燃的眼睛。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44; role; flag +odd_job_apprentice_

- **1929.04** `hs18-cinema.newsreel::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/recognize-a-neighbor` tags: cinema, newsreel, neighbor, recognition, media, history, reform, medical
  银幕上是城西的街口，镜头扫过一排晾衣绳。Mitzi Lechner认出绳下站着Mitzi Hofer，也认出旁边那个被两名便衣夹住的人。散场灯亮时，Mitzi Hofer还在笑，Mitzi Lechner却把围巾往上拉了拉。
  _effects: knowledge “新闻片里那个被便衣夹住的人，我认识。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; flag +recognized_neighbor_on_newsreel; mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-67_

- **1929.06** `hs18-queue.figure::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/pretend-not-to-see` tags: historical_figure, queue, recognition, bread, witness, history, reform, medical
  Mitzi Lechner把头转向橱窗，假装在看一双标价离谱的鞋。Friedrich Hofer从队伍旁走过，靴跟敲得很慢。Karl Kraus往前挪了一步，帽檐仍压着；两人之间隔着三个人和一整条街的沉默。
  _effects: state_add hs18-street-witness state:world-vienna-1912:216; knowledge “保持沉默也需要记住每一个细节。”; flag +ignored_historical_figure_in_queue; mood -0.030000000000000027_

  名单在委员会卡住了。Ferdinand Berg的名字被划掉，理由是“没有相关资历”；Mitzi Lechner没有争，只把名单收回公文包。晚上，Ferdinand Berg来问结果，Mitzi Lechner说再等一年；Ferdinand Berg没有坐，站了一会儿就走了。
  _effects: reputation -0.020000000000000018; mood -0.020000000000000018; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4; flag +appointment_list_rejected; knowledge “委员会以“没有相关资历”为由划掉了联络员的名字。”_

- **1930.01** `state_tick:office-under-siege/pressure-year/held-on` tags: office-under-siege, office, scandal, pressure, politics, legal, risk, state
  质询会开了两个小时，Mitzi Lechner把每一笔签字都念了一遍。没有人被说服，但也没有人再追问；散会时，支持Mitzi Lechner的人比进门时多了两个。
  _effects: state_parameter office-under-siege.support 54 -> 59; state_parameter office-under-siege.exposure 40 -> 36; state_parameter office-under-siege.months 1 -> 2; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  巡查簿在换班时不见了，第二天却出现在煤筐底下，最后几页被水泡得发皱。管事的说签字不能算数，扣了Mitzi Lechner半个月的夜班钱；Mitzi Lechner没有争，只在新的簿子第一页把日期写得很大。

  _effects: historical development:seed-vienna-social-democracy-1912 as supporter; historical_influence; reputation 0.015000000000000013_

  _effects: cash -5 (分摊母亲药费中自己的一份（其中5克朗记入债务）)_

- **1930.10** `df18-mark.first-appearance::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/burn-bird` tags: body, mark, omen, illness, rumor, supernatural, eerie, reform
  印刷机的滚筒咬住Mitzi Lechner的袖口时，谁都以为只擦破了一层皮。伤口结痂后，却留下一只张开翅膀的鸟形烫痕。Anton Lechner给了Mitzi Lechner两枚银币，说别去报工伤，厂里正查安全。Mitzi Lechner把钱收下，回到排字房，觉得那只鸟在袖子里发烫。
  _effects: cash 2 (工头给的封口费); state_add df18-uncanny-mark state:world-vienna-1912:261; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36; flag +df18_mark_appeared_after_injury; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36; flag +df18_mark_noticed; knowledge “我身上有一处说不清的痕迹，别人对它各有各的说法。”_

- **1930.12** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.quit` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Mitzi Lechner说互助会先退了，这笔钱要留给煤和面包。Hermann Kral没争辩，在蓝皮本上把名字划了一道，问清楚是不是全家都退，然后把划掉的那行折进本子夹缝。她说以后想再进，要重新排三个月。门关上，Mitzi Lechner听见铅笔在楼梯上轻轻敲了两下栏杆。
  _effects: cash 0.4 (省下本周会费留作煤钱); relation acquaintance with person-hermann-kral; flag +funeral_society_left_

- **1931.01** `office16-hire.protege::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/refused-by-committee` tags: office, hire, patronage, bureaucracy, work, power, reform, medical
  编制委员会只用了十分钟就否掉了Grete Moser的名字：预算冻结，岗位归并，连桌子都要搬去仓库。Mitzi Lechner把那张表折起来，答应明年再试。Grete Moser说没关系，第二天去了另一家事务所面试。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52; flag +protege_hiring_refused; knowledge “编制委员会今年冻住了所有新增岗位。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-52_

  Mitzi Lechner退烧第二天就回工厂，站到下午，眼前一阵阵发黑。回家后半夜又烧起来，她把湿毛巾搭在额头上，听见自己的心跳撞着枕头。第二天早上，Mitzi Lechner又去了，因为请假的扣款比药贵。

- **1931.10** `ml18-inheritance.uncles-press::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/sell-the-press-for-scrap` tags: inheritance, family, business, debt, legal, money, reform, medical
  Mitzi Lechner把压机拆开卖给收废铁的人，得了八克朗；还掉六克朗欠账，剩下两克朗。订阅名单被Mitzi Lechner带回家，夹在电话簿里，偶尔拿出来看看那些名字。印刷所的门从里面锁上，窗户上贴了一张招租纸，风把纸角吹得一直响。
  _effects: cash 2 (scrap value of the press after paying its debts); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3; flag +sold_the_press; knowledge “那台压机卖作废铁只值八克朗，订阅名单却可能值更多。”; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1931.10** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_burns_curtain` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Mitzi Lechner在公用灶台上热猪油，油星子溅到旁边晾着的床单上，火苗顺着床单爬上木隔板。Elisabeth Bauer拎起水桶泼过去，火灭了，但床单烧掉半幅，隔板熏黑一片。房东扣了那户人家两克朗押金，那家的女人在楼道里哭了一下午。
  _effects: cash -2 (赔给房东的隔板熏黑损失); relation acquaintance with person-elisabeth-bauer_

- **1931.11** `ad20-public-mistake::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/the-wrong-person-apologizes` tags: public, mistake, shame, everyday, reputation, comic, reform, medical
  一个路过的男人以为是自己撞了Mitzi Lechner，连声道歉，还帮Mitzi Lechner把纸包重新系好。Mitzi Lechner说不是他；男人说“那就当我替别人道歉”。他走后，Mitzi Lechner站在街口，忽然觉得这件事比刚才轻了一些。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018; reputation -0.010000000000000009; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3; flag +wrong_apology_

- **1932.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/compromise_split_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Mitzi Lechner把洗好的床单搭上院子里的公用晾衣绳，占了整条。Elisabeth Bauer抱着一盆湿衣服下来，说自家孩子明天要穿干净衬衫上学。两人在绳子两头扯了一阵，最后Mitzi Lechner把床单对折，腾出一半，Elisabeth Bauer用木夹子把衬衫一件件排上去。谁也没道歉，但第二天见面都点了头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-elisabeth-bauer; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:005025_

- **1932.01** `df18-mark.child-at-the-font::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/clean-skin` tags: body, mark, family, child, baptism, inheritance, eerie, reform
  Mitzi Lechner在洗礼前一直盯着Friedrich Berg的耳后，那里干干净净，什么也没有。Mitzi Lechner松了一口气，又在同一口气里觉得失落。Anna Bauer问怎么了，Mitzi Lechner说教堂里太冷。回家的路上，Friedrich Berg在襁褓里睡着，手指抓着Mitzi Lechner的衣扣。
  _effects: relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001604:child; relation partner with person-anna-bauer; state_parameter df18-uncanny-mark.attention 45 -> 40; flag +df18_child_without_mark; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-55_

- **1932.01** `state_tick:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/strange-year/learned-the-building` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  又过了一个冬天，Mitzi Lechner能从楼梯的响声判断是谁在值夜，也能从煤气灯的颜色判断哪一段管道该修。师傅把巡查路线改了三次，最后仍然照Mitzi Lechner画的那张走；两个人都没有提这件事。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 24 -> 34; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 10 -> 20; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.nightsWorked 0 -> 24; cash 2 (night-shift wages); skill_

- **1932.01** `state_tick:hs18-street-witness/memory-drift/holds` tags: hs18-street-witness, witness, memory, history, testimony, rumor, risk, street
  Mitzi Lechner又把那天的顺序在脑子里走了一遍：先是门响，再是靴子，最后才是那声喊。细节还在，只是每讲一次，旁边就多出一点别人替你补上的颜色。
  _effects: state_parameter hs18-street-witness.certainty 59 -> 61_

- **1932.02** `statistical_infection`
  Mitzi Lechner在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1932.03** `office16-corruption.inspection::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/cover-up` tags: office, corruption, audit, legal, power, dark, reform, medical
  Mitzi Lechner请Friedrich Hofer去咖啡馆，把一只没有标记的信封留在桌上。Friedrich Hofer没有碰信封，只把报告里“重复报销”改成“记账口径不一”；作为交换，Mitzi Lechner签了Friedrich Hofer侄子的实习证明。两个人都没有提那只信封后来去了哪里。
  _effects: reputation -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-friedrich-hofer; flag +bribed_auditor; knowledge “审计报告里的“重复报销”被改成了“记账口径不一”，实习证明由我签字。”; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1932.03** `statistical_recovery`
  Mitzi Lechner从肺炎中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

- **1932.07** `vienna-women-housework.piecework-bundle/piecework.overnight` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  Mitzi Lechner点着煤油灯缝到后半夜，把一捆领子全做完了。针脚密是密，可天亮时她看东西重影，手指被针扎了三回。星期六交活，Grete Moser 2量了量尺寸，收下整捆，数出七十五赫勒推过桌面。
  _effects: cash 0.75 (交了一整捆衬衫领子的计件工钱); mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-74_

  Mitzi Lechner把口袋按住，说要看搜查令。Hermann Berg 2笑了一声，反手把她的胳膊别到背后。Marie Huber站在面包店门口，没有过来。到了岗亭，登记簿上写的不是“拒搜”，而是“妨碍公务”，Mitzi Lechner的胳膊肿了三天。

  Katharina Hofer只给了Mitzi Lechner三句台词和一个端盘子的动作。排练时，Mitzi Lechner把那三句念得太用力，导演让重来，说“你不是在演一个端盘子的人，你是在演一个怕被看见的人”。首演后没有人记住Mitzi Lechner，但乐队指挥记住了Mitzi Lechner每次上台前都在后台数拍子。
  _effects: state_add fa17-starving-artist state:world-vienna-1912:459; cash 1 (小角色的排练补贴); role; mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +debut_small_part; state_add fa17-muse state:world-vienna-1912:460_

- **1936.01** `office16-favor.private-sinecure::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-university/the-favor-was-already-owed` tags: office, favor, corruption, patronage, family, power, vienna, urban
  Ferdinand Weiss 2早在Mitzi Lechner上任前就挂在一个“临时调查员”的名下，工资由上一任签字。Mitzi Lechner不能新设岗位，只能补一份追认函；函上写明“工作内容另行通知”，而通知一直没有发出。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81; flag +retroactive_sinecure; knowledge “临时调查员的岗位是上一任留下的，工作内容从未书面通知。”; mood 0.010000000000000009; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81_

  Friedrich Hofer在街口设了登记桌，要求住户交出姓名、职业和宗教。Mitzi Lechner排队填了表，回家把门牌擦干净。楼里有人连夜搬走，也有人站在阳台上挥手。
  _effects: role; cash -3 (new permits and stamps); condition_

- **1938.01** `ad20-child-teacher::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-university/the-teacher-has-a-question` tags: family, child, school, teacher, everyday, public, vienna, urban
  Mitzi Weiss问Mitzi Lechner，Rosa Huber最近为什么总在课上打瞌睡。Mitzi Lechner说家里晚上在赶一批活；Mitzi Weiss点点头，说“别让孩子熬太晚”。Mitzi Lechner应了一声，心里却在算那批活还能不能按时交。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-29; state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 75 -> 77; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +teacher_asked_homework_

- **1938.01** `r2-leisure.intercepted-letter::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::intercepted-letter-war/letter-returned-undelivered` tags: leisure, letters, privacy, romance, family, vienna, urban, crisis
  Mitzi Lechner把回信写了七页，写到第八页时，发现信纸不够。Alois Lechner说前线的人不看长信，Mitzi Lechner还是把七页都装进信封。一个月后，信退回来，信封上盖着“收件人已转移”。Mitzi Lechner把信拆开，重新读了一遍自己写的话。
  _effects: mood -0.12; condition; flag +letter_returned_undelivered; relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-88; knowledge “the recipient was transferred before the letter arrived”_

- **1939.01** `state_tick:bc18-ridiculous-fame/ridiculous-fame-year/the-story-holds-another-year` tags: bc18-ridiculous-fame, fame, reputation, mistaken_identity, promotion, media, absurd, public
  又一年过去，Mitzi Lechner仍在被介绍成那个人物。邀请函从市郊的园艺会、女子学校和退伍军人酒馆寄来；Mitzi Lechner学会在签名后加一句模糊的谦辞，听起来像有许多往事不便细说。每一次应付都带来一点小钱，也让真正的解释更不合时宜。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.notoriety 38 -> 45; state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.invitations 1 -> 2; cash 1 (一场小型演讲的车马费); reputation 0.010000000000000009; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1939.01** `state_tick:ml18-sudden-fortune/fortune-year/money-keeps-working` tags: ml18-sudden-fortune, money, windfall, sudden_fortune, household, luck, inheritance, lottery
  一年过去，钱没有变多，却也没有一下花完。Mitzi Lechner把一部分存进邮局储蓄所，一部分借给楼下的鞋匠；鞋匠每月送来一双补好的鞋，利息就夹在鞋垫下面。街口的杂货商开始把好面粉留到Mitzi Lechner来的时候。
  _effects: cash 2 (interest and small repayments on the windfall); state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 3 -> 4; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1939.01** `p9-work-career.clerk-post/p9-work-career.clerk-post.hired` tags: work, career, craft, clerk, education, task, office, application
  Mitzi Lechner在保险公司写字间的小桌上抄完一页德文书信，又算出利息表上最后一行，速记只漏了两个词。Hermann Berg 2把卷子翻过来看背面，说手是稳的。月底他领到七十克朗，扣掉十五克朗保证金，签字画押，坐进靠窗那把椅子，桌上有一块吸墨纸和一支借来的笔。
  _effects: cash 55 (首月工钱扣除保证金后实发); skill; employment 保险写字间试用办事员 @ cinema:person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-22-evt:world-vienna-1912:002325; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-86_

- **1939.02** `vienna-church-community.tenement-prayer/rent-prayer-argument` tags: faith, social, family, home
  Maria Kral怪Mitzi Lechner把人都招到楼梯上，木头泡了潮气才裂的。Mitzi Lechner没还嘴，蹲下去看那道裂缝，指甲刮下一层朽木屑。
  _effects: reputation -0.030000000000000027; mood -0.05999999999999994_

- **1939.03** `state_reaction:ml18-sudden-fortune/a-relative-remembers` tags: ml18-sudden-fortune, money, windfall, sudden_fortune, household, luck, inheritance, lottery
  一个多年没走动的亲戚提着一盒点心上门，先问Mitzi Lechner的身体，再问那笔钱是怎么来的。临走时他说起小时候替Mitzi Lechner家垫过一冬天的煤钱，数字记不清了，但意思很清楚。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 2 -> 3; mood -0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:009284_

  洗衣妇把工服从报纸里抖出来，翻开领口看了看油垢，点头收下。Mitzi Lechner转身回屋，铜板在围裙口袋里磕出轻响。Mitzi还在楼梯上嘟囔，说这钱不如买半斤猪油。
  _effects: cash -0.5 (请洗衣妇代洗工服); mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912_

- **1939.08** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.pay` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Mitzi Lechner数出八赫勒交给Friedrich Hofer，看她在蓝皮本上找到自家门牌，把这一周的格子划掉。Friedrich Hofer说上个月隔壁巷子的人走了，公账出了棺材和一场小弥撒，剩下的钱记在册尾。楼梯口的灯很暗，Mitzi Lechner盯着自己的名字看了两遍才转身。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (一周丧葬互助会会费); relation acquaintance with person-friedrich-hofer; flag +funeral_society_member_

  Mitzi Lechner把半条黑面包搁回篮子，篮底垫着旧衬衫。Josef Moser问你家孩子怎么办，Mitzi Lechner说碗橱里还有燕麦。窗台上的雪水化开，沿着砖缝往下淌，屋里比往常冷。

  Maria Kral 2半夜敲墙，说同屋的人烧得说胡话。Mitzi Lechner隔着板壁问要不要去叫医生，Maria Kral 2说医生来了也只给退热粉，还要收出诊费。天亮时，楼梯间多了一股石灰水味，Luise Hofer 3把白线画到了隔壁门口。

  Mitzi Lechner在合租房走廊的公用灶台上煮土豆，转身取盐时袖子扫翻了铁锅，滚水浇在左手背上。Mitzi Hofer从隔壁冲出来，扯下自己的围裙裹住烫伤处，又跑下楼叫来住在二楼的接生婆。接生婆用蛋清和旧亚麻布包扎，嘱咐三天不能沾水。

- **1940.06** `vienna-housing-move.sublet-room/sublet-full` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money, household
  Mitzi Lechner和Ferdinand Kral讲好一张床按月四克朗，晚饭另算，钥匙只一把。Ferdinand Kral把铺盖卷搬进来，靠在墙边，第一天就把厨房的碗顺手洗了。Anna Berg过来看了一圈，低声说夜里门要栓好，Mitzi Lechner点头，把每月四克朗记在包糖纸上。
  _effects: cash 4 (出租空床收进的月租); household; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-77; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-68_

- **1940.10** `ld18-performance.tango::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-impresario-offers-a-contract` tags: performance, desire, dance, public, queer, nightlife, public_desire, vienna
  Josef Lechner 2把合同推过来，说只要她们每星期跳三场，就付双倍的钱。Mitzi Lechner问要不要改舞步，Josef Lechner 2说不用，越像现在这样越好。Mitzi看着合同上的数字，先笑了，又有点想哭。
  _effects: state_add ld18-public-desire state:world-vienna-1912:594; cash 2 (the first week of the scandal contract); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-112; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +scandal_contract; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-112; flag +public_desire_active_

- **1941.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/neighbor_yanks_clothes` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Mitzi Hofer嫌Mitzi Lechner的床单滴水滴到Mitzi Hofer家衬衫上，一把扯下床单扔在院子的石板上。Mitzi Lechner下楼捡起来时，床单角沾了泥，重洗又要费肥皂。两人在院子当中对喊，楼上有人推开窗看热闹。Mitzi Lechner把床单重新搭上绳子时，故意把水拧在Mitzi Hofer那侧。
  _effects: relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; cash -0.15 (重洗床单的肥皂钱)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:009992_

  Mitzi Hofer 2把一只没有字的信封压在审计文件下面，说这是“朋友之间的周转”，等风头过去再还。Mitzi Lechner把信封收进抽屉，Friedrich Hofer第二天来问账目时，Mitzi Lechner第一次没有把每一页都摊开。抽屉合上时发出一声很轻的响。
  _effects: cash 15 (the envelope left under the audit file); state_parameter office-under-siege.support 60 -> 70; state_parameter office-under-siege.exposure 30 -> 45; flag +accepted_office_loan; knowledge “信封是那个中间人替一个承包商送来的，条件是审计报告晚两周提交。”; mood -0.010000000000000231_

  一个陌生女人在电车上叫住Mitzi Lechner，说自己认识她的“那位”，还劝她趁年轻找个正经人家。Mitzi Lechner没有回答，提前一站下了车；走在回家的路上，她发现自己的步子越来越快，像在躲一个并不存在的人。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.rumorHeat 50 -> 58; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.shame 10 -> 14; reputation -0.030000000000000027; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  煤袋落在台阶上，扬起细细一层黑末。Anna Berg用铅笔头在蓝格册上写门牌号，问房东姓什么，又叮嘱别把面包票弄丢。灶上水壶开始叫，Mitzi Lechner才想起屋里连只干净的杯子都没有。
  _effects: cash 6 (互助会补上本月房租差额); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-77; flag +parish_registered_

  Mitzi Lechner点着煤油灯缝到后半夜，把一捆领子全做完了。针脚密是密，可天亮时她看东西重影，手指被针扎了三回。星期六交活，Katharina Kral量了量尺寸，收下整捆，数出七十五赫勒推过桌面。
  _effects: cash 0.75 (交了一整捆衬衫领子的计件工钱); health -0.03999999999999998 (通宵缝纫，眼睛疲劳，手指扎伤); mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Mitzi Lechner急着去看伤者，一脚踩进了粉笔线里，鞋底在血上滑了一下。Elisabeth Weiss蹲下去看了半天，说“这下只能靠口供了”。Franz Winter递过来一块抹布，Mitzi Lechner没有接。那天晚上，局里传话说“现场保护不力”，名字写在通报的第二行。

  Mitzi Lechner把钥匙丢在了回家路上的有轨电车轨道旁，天黑也没找着。房东太太说换锁芯要一克朗五十赫勒，从下月房租里扣。Karl Kraus借给Mitzi Lechner一把旧挂锁，但锁鼻和门扣对不上，只能用铁丝拧着凑合。那夜Mitzi Lechner睡不踏实，总觉得有人在拧门。
  _effects: cash -1.5 (换锁芯费用（其中1.5克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-karl-kraus; mood -0.040000000000000036_

  Mitzi Lechner听到Ferdinand Weiss 2把工钱和家底夸过了头，脸沉下来，当晚就照地址找上门去解释。话到嘴边绕成一团，Anna Berg的母亲把网兜往桌上一搁，说年轻人有话该早说，不该让旁人来传。Mitzi Lechner站在门里，名声在面包店门口折了一道，Anna Berg始终没有出来。
  _effects: reputation -0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-77_

  _effects: cash 2 (白天出租床位的钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-68; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9; food_

- **1947.04** `p10-crisis-migration.eviction/eviction-deal-landlord` tags: adulthood, crisis, migration, war, police, eviction, refugee, housing
  谈的时候Alois Lechner靠在门框上听着。房东说月底前结清，不然下回连箱子都不许搁院子里。锁重新装上，Mitzi Lechner在门后站了好一会儿，摸了摸新钉子的头，才去灶上烧水。
  _effects: cash -20 (补齐部分欠租才换回门锁（其中20克朗记入债务）); mood 0.050000000000000044; flag +rent_arrears_partial; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-114; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-114_

- **1947.05** `p9-gap-adult-ordinary-day.organ-grinder/organ-grinder.listen-coins` tags: adulthood, daily, leisure, neighborhood, ordinary, music, street
  她本来只是去面包店买盐，被琴声绊住了脚。猴子蹲在帽沿上啃一颗栗子，风琴师脱下礼帽扇风，露出汗湿的额角。曲子完了，她数出两枚铜板放进帽子，听见身后有人吹口哨。
  _effects: cash -0.02 (给手摇风琴师的赏钱（其中0.02克朗记入债务）); mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1947.10** `vienna-parish-aid.sick-call/sick-call.door-only` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Mitzi Lechner把药包和半块肥皂交给开门的孩子，嘱咐用水冲开、一天两次，就下了楼。楼梯间里孩子一直站在门口看着，手里攥着纸包，没关门。Mitzi Lechner走到二层又折回去，帮他把门带上，说自己家里也等着火。
  _effects: health 0.01 (没有久留，避开了病人的屋子); relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000904:child; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1948.01** `p10-migration-tasks.pawn-belongings/pawn-watch-and-coat` tags: adulthood, migration, housing, family, travel, task, pawn, sale
  他把怀表和两件冬衣递上铁栅栏后的柜台，当铺的人翻过表壳、抖了抖衣领，报出九克朗，写在一张蓝纸当票上。他留下那套木工工具，把当票折成小方块塞进身份证后面，数钱时手指有点抖。
  _effects: cash 9 (当掉怀表与两件冬衣换来的现钱); mood -0.050000000000000044; knowledge “当铺赎当期限六个月，过期死当，蓝纸当票要收好”_

- **1948.10** `vienna-postwar-housing-1950s/stay-with-relatives` tags: adulthood, postwar, housing, family
  分房名单又往后推了一年，Mitzi Lechner一家搬进亲戚家的厨房隔间。Ferdinand Bauer送来一块旧帘子当隔断；夜里有人起身，全屋都听得见。
  _effects: cash -3 (shared household costs（其中3克朗记入债务）); condition; mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:003897:child_

  Mitzi Lechner把Katharina Huber的名字写在编制表的空行上，又在旁边注了“试用三个月”。Hermann Berg 2提醒说预算只够半个位置，Mitzi Lechner还是把名字留下了。第二天，Katharina Huber提前半小时到，把走廊里的旧档案按年份重新排了一遍。
  _effects: position 助理文书 @cinema:person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-22-evt:world-vienna-1912:002325; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-148; reputation 0.030000000000000027; flag +hired_protege; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-148_

  Mitzi Lechner替Marie Huber把名字、出生年和最后住址写在纸上，递进窗口。Franz Weiss翻了三本名册，手指停在一行被水洇开的字上，说这个人登记在临时收容所。Marie Huber听见时，手里的伞掉在地上；Mitzi Lechner替她把伞捡起来，才发现自己的手也在抖。
  _effects: cash -2 (念错的名字); flag +found_missing_relative_on_list; role; relation kin with person-marie-huber; knowledge “the missing relative was registered at a transit shelter”_

  Mitzi Lechner还不上钱，趁夜把工具装进麻袋，去车站买了一张最远的票。Josef Lechner第二天带人上门，Anton Stein说人已经走了；他们搬走了缝纫机和两把椅子，在墙上用粉笔写了一个日期。
  _effects: cash -6 (牌桌上的欠条); flag +fled_gambling_debt; relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9; move place-wien; unemployment (left the shop to escape a gambling debt)_

- **1950.01** `kernel-migrate.refugee-aid::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/civic-refugee-error` tags: migration, refugee, missing_relative, care, postwar, vienna, urban, reform
  她在一张错误名单上找到熟悉的名字，跑去找Katharina Berg确认。对方看了很久，说“不是他，是我哥哥”；她把纸折好，没有把那个名字划掉。她把那张纸折好，决定明天再去名单墙前问一次。
  _effects: flag +refugee_list_error; knowledge “名单上的名字可能属于同姓的另一个人，划掉前要当面确认”; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000453:child; cash -1 (跑去确认的车钱); reputation -0.010000000000000009_

- **1950.05** `r2-law.legal-aid::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::r2-law.legal-aid-reform/municipal-clinic` tags: law, legal_aid, poverty, court, bureaucracy, vienna, urban, postwar
  市里新设了法律咨询处，每周三下午开门。Mitzi Lechner去时门口已经排了二十多人，Grete 2一次只能谈十五分钟。轮到Mitzi Lechner时，Mitzi Hofer敲了敲钟，说时间到；Grete 2把案卷塞进包里，让Mitzi Lechner下周三再来，带上所有欠条。
  _effects: flag +used_municipal_legal_clinic; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000923:child; knowledge “the municipal clinic allows fifteen minutes per case”_

  放映到第十一本，胶片在片门里卷成一团。Mitzi Lechner剪掉烧焦的一截，发现后面接的是一卷没送审的新闻片：军靴踏过广场，镜头扫过一张张被按下去的脸。Gustav Bauer 3在黑暗里说“接回正片”，Mitzi Lechner照做了。
  _effects: organization_found 小巷电影院 (household_firm); position 放映员 @cinema:person-mitzi-lechner-evt:world-vienna-1912:015985; state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:987; cash 1 (projectionist wages); skill; flag +cut_the_reel; knowledge “第十一本胶片里夹着没送审的新闻片，{projectionist}让我接回正片。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-107; role; flag +odd_job_apprentice_

- **1950.08** `vienna-historical-social-movement.district-list/demand-open-books` tags: history, historical_process, social_movement, organization, politics
  负责人说这是不信任，Mitzi Lechner回说数目对得上才叫信任。Luise Lechner把卷角的账页压平，指着一行重复的支出，屋里安静得能听见煤气灯嘶声。最后决定下次集会再报账。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:003423:child; reputation 0.040000000000000036; skill_

- **1951.01** `office16-scandal.public-hearing::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/weathered` tags: office, scandal, media, reputation, politics, power, vienna, urban
  报纸在头版登了Mitzi Lechner和承包商在一张桌子上的照片，配文只有一句“谁签的字”。质询会开了三个小时，Mitzi Lechner把合同、比价单和验收记录一页页摊开；记者第二天没有再写，但把照片留在了档案里。
  _effects: reputation -0.030000000000000027; mood -0.029999999999999916; state_add office-under-siege state:world-vienna-1912:1019; knowledge “照片是真的，合同也是真的；报纸没有再追，但档案里留了底片。”; flag +office_under_siege_

- **1951.01** `state_tick:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/strange-year/blamed-for-the-noise` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  楼上住户投诉夜里有人拖椅子，管事的把巡查簿翻到Mitzi Lechner的那一页，说签字太潦草，看不出巡过几趟。Mitzi Lechner想辩解，却想起那晚确实在楼梯间多站了一刻钟；扣掉的工钱刚好够买一双不漏水的鞋。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 30 -> 14; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.reliability 50 -> 40_

- **1951.04** `vienna-social-movement.recruitment/join-and-carry` tags: development, social_movement, organization, politics
  Mitzi Lechner在楼梯下面接到一张折成四折的传单。纸上没有署名，只写着下一次集会的地址。Mitzi Lechner去了，在昏暗的屋里报上姓名，成为圣马克斯殡葬合作社的一员，并答应负责自己所在街区的联络。
  _effects: organization_membership; role; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:003423:child_

- **1951.10** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/drops-and-endure` tags: health, medical, family, money, injury
  夜里疼醒时，Mitzi Lechner摸黑往棉球上又滴药水，手抖得洒在枕头上。Katharina Berg第二天一早来敲门，看见肿脸就说别硬撑了，可Mitzi Lechner只是摆摆手，说再熬一天也许就好了。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (牙痛药水); health -0.01999999999999999 (牙病拖延未治); condition_

  Mitzi Lechner没有去诊所，只用热水和旧布洗了伤口。第二天伤口周围发红，家里人把仅剩的干净床单撕成条。
  _effects: health -0.02999999999999997 (untreated wound); cash -2 (clean cloth); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:016602_

  第二轮投票是十七比十七。按章程，主席可以投第二票；Mitzi Lechner把自己的名字念了两遍，会议室里没有人笑。散会后，Luise Hofer 3在门口说下一次会带更多的人来，Mitzi Lechner说椅子够用。
  _effects: reputation -0.020000000000000018; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-95; organization_funds; flag +tie_broken_by_chair; knowledge “章程允许主席在平票时投第二票，但没有人记得上一次使用是什么时候。”_

  第二天的报纸又登了一封匿名信，说Mitzi Lechner把一份合同给了亲戚。信里没有证据，但足够让两个原本答应到场的人临时出差。
  _effects: state_parameter office-under-siege.exposure 30 -> 40; state_parameter office-under-siege.support 60 -> 54; state_parameter office-under-siege.months 0 -> 1; reputation -0.030000000000000027_

  Mitzi Lechner咬牙买了音乐厅楼座的票，两克朗五十赫勒，又向人借来一件硬领衬衫，领子磨得后颈发红。演奏到慢板时，Mitzi Lechner手心里的汗把节目单边角浸软，却连咳嗽都憋着，生怕在周围体面人中间出声。散场后Marie Huber等在门口，问值不值，Mitzi Lechner只把节目单折好收进口袋。
  _effects: cash -2.5 (音乐厅楼座票价); mood 0.07999999999999996; reputation 0.020000000000000018; skill_

  Mitzi Lechner说胡话时念着车间的排班表，Marie Huber一句句应着，拿小勺往嘴里送温水。床头那半瓶退烧药早见了底，她没舍得倒最后一滴，留着天亮再说。
  _effects: health -0.03999999999999998 (高烧硬熬伤身)_

  Mitzi Lechner在第二本胶片放到一半时起身，摸黑从侧门出去。Luise Hofer 3在门口抽烟，问是不是片子太旧。Mitzi Lechner说不是，是座位太硬；街上的雨刚停，海报被泡得只剩半张脸。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-95; flag +left_newsreel_early_

  _effects: state_add stage-sovereign state:world-vienna-1912:1053; cash 1 (替角的排练补贴); flag +understudy_king; flag +stage_sovereign; reputation 0.040000000000000036_

- **1955.01** `office16-admin.quiet-ledger::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-university/balanced` tags: office, administration, budget, competence, quiet, power, vienna, urban
  Mitzi Lechner用三个月把账本上的零碎支出并成四类，又停掉两份没有人看的简报。年底结账时，Gustav Bauer发现不仅没有赤字，还剩下够换一批档案柜的钱；没有剪彩，只有仓库里新柜子的木头味。
  _effects: organization_funds; reputation 0.040000000000000036; skill; mood 0.020000000000000018; knowledge “停掉两份简报、合并零碎支出后，年底还剩一笔够换档案柜的钱。”_

- **1955.01** `vienna-social-movement.meeting/quiet-solidarity` tags: development, social_movement, organization, meeting
  Mitzi Lechner没有发言，只把带来的面包分成几份。散会时，一个不认识的女人把她的地址塞给Mitzi Lechner，说那条街还需要有人继续收会费。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000453:child_

- **1955.01** `agency:recruit_members` tags: agency, agency:recruit_members
  Mitzi Lechner试图招募Mitzi Bauer 3，但对方担心工作和警察，没有给出承诺。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036_

- **1955.11** `vienna-legacy-worklife.union-office/union-office.enroll-first` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  一克朗硬币在桌上排开。Mitzi Lechner数了两遍，才在名册上写“已缴”。窗外有人探头问这里是不是工会，接待的人这次知道怎么开口了，把门拉开一半。
  _effects: cash 1 (新会员的入会费先记在桌上); relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000923:child; skill_

- **1956.01** `office16-favor.private-sinecure::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-favor-was-already-owed` tags: office, favor, corruption, patronage, family, power, vienna, urban
  Luise Bauer 2早在Mitzi Lechner上任前就挂在一个“临时调查员”的名下，工资由上一任签字。Mitzi Lechner不能新设岗位，只能补一份追认函；函上写明“工作内容另行通知”，而通知一直没有发出。
  _effects: organization_funds; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-85; knowledge “临时调查员的岗位是上一任留下的，工作内容从未书面通知。”; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1956.01** `agency:attend_meeting` tags: agency, agency:attend_meeting
  Mitzi Lechner走到Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang门口，看见便衣和拥挤的人群，最终没有进去。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036_

- **1956.09** `ml18-inheritance.conditional-will::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-university/keep-the-candle-workshop-open` tags: inheritance, legal, family, money, conditions, cinema, quiet, vienna
  姑婆的遗嘱只有一页：六十克朗留给Mitzi Lechner，条件是那间放无声电影的小影院再开五年，并且把她的侄孙女送进学校。Anna Bauer把三个条件念了两遍；Luise Bauer 2在门外等着，手里捏着另一份没有拆封的遗嘱。Mitzi Lechner先领了十克朗，把影院的钥匙挂在腰上。
  _effects: cash 10 (first payment under the conditional will); state_add ml18-sudden-fortune state:world-vienna-1912:1216; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-85; flag +conditional_heir; knowledge “遗嘱要求小影院再开五年，并把姑婆的侄孙女送进学校。”; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +sudden_fortune; reputation 0.020000000000000018; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1956.09** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.river-walk/river-walk.far` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, walk, river, injury
  Mitzi Lechner顺着河岸一直走，走过铁路桥又走回来，天已经黑透。回来时脚底发热，袜子磨在脚背上，她一路数着路灯走完最后一段巷子，进门先坐下脱了鞋。
  _effects: health -0.020000000000000018 (走了很远的碎石路，脚底发疼); mood 0.050000000000000044_

- **1956.12** `vienna-parish-aid.rent-box/rent-box.refuse` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Mitzi Lechner说这个月还能撑，房租差的那几克朗打算替人洗衣补上，请Grete 2把篮子提回去给更困难的人家。Grete 2在门口停了一会儿，把一小块肥皂留在窗台上，说不是救济，是教区多出来的。门关上后，屋里只剩煤箱底刮出的响声。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000923:child; flag +parish_refused_aid_

- **1957.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/wound-infection` tags: health, work, family, injury
  Mitzi Lechner的伤口在几天后肿起来，手腕发热。医生说要切开引流，费用比上次更高；Mitzi Lechner看着账单，先问能不能分两次付。
  _effects: health -0.12 (wound infection); cash -14 (drainage and medicine)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:019991_

- **1957.01** `ml18-tax.audit-of-the-windfall::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-university/the-inspector-was-an-old-friend` tags: tax, legal, money, windfall, bureaucracy, comic, vienna, urban
  Luise Hofer 3进门后没有先翻账本，而是问Mitzi Lechner是不是在战前服过役。两个人对上了部队编号，Luise Hofer 3把帽子放在桌上，说利息可以按小额储蓄申报，罚款就免了。Helene 2松了一口气，手里的计算尺却还停在半空，像是不知道该不该落下去。
  _effects: cash -2 (the reduced tax assessment); state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.principal 20 -> 18; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-95; flag +tax_audit_friendly; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1957.01** `r13-fam.visit-grave::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/wrong-grave` tags: task, family, grave, mourning, neighbor, grief, vienna, urban
  两人在坟场里绕了半个钟头，才找到那块刻着名字的石头。Katharina Berg忽然说不是这一块，自己记得旁边有一棵小槐树。Mitzi Lechner陪她一排排找，天快黑时，守墓人过来说小槐树去年就砍了；Katharina Berg站在空地上，把花放在了脚边。
  _effects: mood -0.09000000000000008; condition; flag +could_not_find_the_grave; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000453:child; knowledge “the old tree by the grave was cut down last year”_

- **1957.05** `office16-budget.sign-or-block::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/signed-with-margin` tags: office, budget, permit, administration, power, economy, vienna, urban
  Mitzi Lechner在预算表上签了字，把冬季煤房的款项从三笔并成一笔，又给夜班守卫加了半份津贴。Gustav Bauer算出省下的钱刚好够修屋顶；没有人鼓掌，但一月的会议室不再需要穿大衣。
  _effects: organization_funds; reputation 0.040000000000000036; skill; mood 0.020000000000000018; knowledge “把冬季煤房的款项并成一笔后，省下的钱够修会议室屋顶。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-128_

- **1957.08** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.leaking-shoe/leaking-shoe.gave-up` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, repair, shoe, poverty
  Mitzi Lechner缝了半个钟头，线老是从针眼里脱出来，最后把鞋往床底下一踢。第二天清早她穿着这双鞋站在厂门口等开门，湿石头地的凉气从裂缝里钻进来，脚趾冻得发麻，只能来回换脚。
  _effects: health -0.050000000000000044 (穿着漏水的鞋在冷地上站久了); mood -0.040000000000000036_

- **1957.09** `vienna-women-housework.wash-day/wash-day.rush` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family, injury
  木盆边的水渍映着天光，Mitzi Lechner跪在石阶上揉搓床单，肘弯发酸。晾绳上的湿布滴答作响，Katharina Berg探身把自家被单也挂上去，两人没多说话。
  _effects: health -0.020000000000000018 (长时间浸在碱水里，手部皮肤开裂); relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000453:child_
